# Release notes

Learn about the latest updates to our API, and Drop-in/Components for web, iOS, and Android.

This page contains the release notes for [Pay by Link](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link), [Checkout API](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/overview), and Drop-in/Components for web, iOS, Android, React Native, and Flutter starting from **2020**.

You can also use the [API Diff Tool](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/api-diff-tool) to compare any two versions of an API.

[![](/user/themes/adyen-docs/assets/icons/rss.svg) Get updates by RSS](/online-payments/release-notes.xml)

## Available filters

Category..., Type..., Version..., Release date...

## 2026-09-10 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.45.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* For Google Pay: You can now use a Content Security Policy nonce without allowing `'unsafe-inline'`. Set the `nonce` configuration property.

* For the Sessions flow: After the shopper selects the **Pay** button, you can now show them a review page before you submit the payment. This adds the following:

  * `onReview`: The callback that returns the payment data.
  * `processPayment`: The method that submits the payment.
  * `onAction`: The callback that returns an action for you to show the shopper.

### Improved

* In the payment form: The address fields now reject unsupported special characters.

### Fixed

* In the payment form: Dropdown menu option spacing and supporting text colors now appear correctly.
* For the Drop-in payment method list: Payment methods now keep their state when the shopper selects another payment method.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.45.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.45.0
```

### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

The base URLs for the script source are different for each environment. Use the one that corresponds to your [live endpoint](/development-resources/live-endpoints#checkout-endpoints):

| Environment                          | URL                                               |
| ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------- |
| Test                                 | `https://checkoutshopper-test.cdn.adyen.com`      |
| Europe (EU) live                     | `https://checkoutshopper-live.cdn.adyen.com`      |
| United States (US) live              | `https://checkoutshopper-live-us.cdn.adyen.com`   |
| Australia (AU) live                  | `https://checkoutshopper-live-au.cdn.adyen.com`   |
| Asia Pacific & Southeast (APSE) live | `https://checkoutshopper-live-apse.cdn.adyen.com` |
| India (IN) live                      | `https://checkoutshopper-live-in.cdn.adyen.com`   |

If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/development-resources/live-endpoints#checkout-js-endpoints), use the same SRI hashes below.

```
<script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.cdn.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/6.45.0/adyen.js"
     integrity="sha384-7KW64UN6T0xZoacpNpRg2KVxMMVwfqwUNTDmalkE1roPBtjOpWe8JuJoUU7OCMvF"
     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>
```

## 2026-08-19 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.44.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Fixed

* For cards: The **Expiry date** and **Security code** fields now keep their format guidance when the field enters an error state.
* For shoppers who use screen readers: The card form, QR code payment methods, and redirect payment methods now announce when content is loading.
* In the payment form: The fields no longer stop working when you pass **null** configuration data, such as a billing address or `personalDetails`.

## 2026-08-12 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.43.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

## 2026-08-04 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.42.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For 3D Secure 2: The challenge iframe now supports WebAuthn and Secure Payment Confirmation. Shoppers can use passkeys when the card issuer supports them.

### Fixed

* In the payment form: When you use a partial billing address, the US postal code field now keeps the correct format and passes validation.

## 2026-08-04 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.12.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Improved

* For Card Component on Android: The configured `countryCode` is now applied as the default country for full-address forms.
* For Apple Pay and Google Pay Components: Reduced unnecessary re-rendering on widget rebuilds by caching the availability result.

### Changed

* iOS: [The minimum version](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration#ios) has increased from 12.0 to **13.0**. This aligns with Flutter's own minimum iOS requirement.

* Android: [The minimum API level (`minSdkVersion`)](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration#android) has increased from 21 to **23**. This aligns with the latest [`pay` package](https://pub.dev/packages/pay) requirements.

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                                                                   | Version              |
  | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------- |
  | [pay](https://pub.dev/packages/pay)                                                                                                                    | 3.2.1 -> **3.3.0**   |
  | [Android Drop-in/Components](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Android%2BComponents%2FDrop-in\&version%5B0%5D=5.20.0) | 5.19.0 -> **5.20.0** |

## 2026-07-30 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.41.1

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

**(Internal change)** TypeScript types are now stricter for the following internal modules:

* `triggerBinLookUp`
* `CardElement` and `CustomCardElement`
* `ClickToPayProvider`
* `DoChallenge3DS2`
* `CardBinLookupData`

### Fixed

* For open invoice payment methods (such as Riverty, RatePay, FacilyPay, and Atome): when multiple open invoice Components are rendered on the same page and a validation error occurs, focus now correctly moves to the field with the error in the submitted Component. Previously, focus always moved to the first open invoice Component on the page.
* For payment forms with a select element or dual-brand selector: pressing **Enter** inside an active select element no longer triggers payment submission.
* For address fields: typing with an [IME](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Input_method) (used for Chinese, Japanese, Korean, and other composing input systems) no longer produces duplicate characters. Previously, the input formatting function interfered with the browser's IME composition, causing composed text to appear twice.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.41.1):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.41.1
```

## 2026-07-16 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.41.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* For card payments: The `onBinLookup` callback now includes a `healthcare` array when the shopper's card has associated healthcare information.

### Improved

* For 3D Secure 2: Drop-in and Components now detect when the challenge token contains an invalid `threeDSNotificationURL`. If it is invalid, the authentication process stops and shows an error to the shopper.

* (Internal change) Drop-in and Components now use the `keydown` event instead of the deprecated `keypress` event. No behavior changes.

* (Internal change) TypeScript types are now stricter for the following:

  * Drop-in
  * ANCV Component
  * Gift card Component
  * 3D Secure 2 Component

### Fixed

* For card payments with the Sessions flow: If you configure installment options in the card configuration object, it no longer has an effect on the options shown on the payment form. Instead, include configuration for installments in the [installmentOptions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions#request-installmentOptions) object in the `/sessions` request.
* For Drop-in: When `openFirstPaymentMethod` is set to **false**, the `aria-checked` attribute now renders correctly on payment method list items. Previously, the attribute was missing, which affected screen readers. This fixes [GitHub issue #3824](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-web/issues/3824).

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.41.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.41.0
```

### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

The base URLs for the script source are different for each environment. Use the one that corresponds to your [live endpoint](/development-resources/live-endpoints#checkout-endpoints):

| ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------- |
| Test                                 | `https://checkoutshopper-test.cdn.adyen.com`      |
| Europe (EU) live                     | `https://checkoutshopper-live.cdn.adyen.com`      |
| United States (US) live              | `https://checkoutshopper-live-us.cdn.adyen.com`   |
| Australia (AU) live                  | `https://checkoutshopper-live-au.cdn.adyen.com`   |

## 2026-07-13 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.12.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* PayByBank is now available as a standalone Component.
* For Apple Pay: You can now configure `merchantCapabilities` in the Apple Pay configuration to specify the payment capabilities your app supports.
* For UPI: The Smart Intent flow is now supported, with automatic detection of installed UPI apps on the device.
* Supported payment method on iOS: Bizum, available as an instant payment method.

### Improved

* For the example app:

  * The **Sessions Component** tab now showcases instant payments.
  * The app now uses the `UIScene` lifecycle.
  * Added a searchable country/region and currency dropdown.

### Fixed

* On Android:

  * For Twint: The payment form no longer shows a **Store payment method** option, matching the iOS behavior.
  * For Google Pay and instant payments: The payment flow now works reliably during device rotation.
  * The **A payment process is still active** screen can no longer be dismissed with the back button or swipe gestures.

* On iOS: The app no longer crashes when dismissing the embedded `CardView`.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                      | Version              |
  | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |
  | [Android Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Android%2BComponents%2FDrop-in) | 5.18.0 -> **5.19.0** |
  | [iOS Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%2BComponents%2FDrop-in)         | 5.24.0 -> **5.25.1** |
  | React Native                                                                                              | **0.85**             |

## 2026-07-09 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.40.2

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For Drop-in and Components: Localized text now uses an exact locale match when one is available. For example, shoppers using `pt-PT` or `zh-TW` now see translations for that locale instead of a generic language match.

### Fixed

* For UPI and Iris: Brand logos in QR-code loading screens now include alternative text for screen readers.
* For PayTo: Screen readers now associate form instructions and account holder validation errors with the relevant input fields.
* For 3D Secure 2: Message handling now validates received messages more accurately and provides clearer diagnostic results when messages are malformed.

### Changed

* For 3D Secure 2 Redirect flows: Callback data now follows the current data structure.

### Updating

## 2026-06-23 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.40.1

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Fixed

* For the Card Component: The `role="presentation"` attribute is removed from secure field iframes (card number, expiry date, and CVC). This fixes an issue where screen readers could not correctly announce secure field content, and also fixes incorrect tabbing behavior on card fields while VoiceOver is enabled.
* For dropdown lists in the payment form: Keyboard navigation and screen reader announcements now work correctly.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.40.1):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.40.1
```

The base URLs for the script source are different for each environment. Use the one that corresponds to your [live endpoint](/development-resources/live-endpoints#checkout-endpoints):

| Environment                          | URL                                               |

## 2026-06-18 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.40.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* For Affirm: You can now use the `allowedCountries` configuration property to customize which countries appear in the address form dropdown. The supported values are **US**, **CA**, and **GB**. The default remains **US** and **CA**.

### Improved

* For the payment form: All personal details and address fields now include WCAG 2.2-compliant `autocomplete` tokens, so that browsers can autofill shopper information more accurately.

### Fixed

* For Affirm: When the `countryCode` does not match any of the configured `allowedCountries`, the address form now correctly pre-selects a valid country. Previously, the form could pre-populate with an invalid country, blocking payment completion.
* For Click to Pay: Screen readers now announce the OTP resend countdown timer and when the **Resend code** button becomes available again.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.40.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.40.0
```

| India (IN) live                      | `https://checkoutshopper-live-in.cdn.adyen.com`   |

## 2026-06-18 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.11.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* For the Apple Pay Component on iOS: You can now configure coupon codes and respond to dynamic changes in the payment sheet when the shopper updates the shipping contact, shipping method, or coupon code. Authorization validation is also now supported.

### Improved

* The iOS example app now uses `UISceneDelegate` for `returnUrl` handling.

### Changed

* On Android: The Google Pay Component now uses an internal flow instead of the deprecated `onActivityResult` flow. You no longer need the `onActivityResult` listener on the host activity.

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                      | Version              |
  | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |
  | [Android Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Android%2BComponents%2FDrop-in) | 5.18.0 -> **5.19.0** |
  | [iOS Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%2BComponents%2FDrop-in)         | 5.25.0 -> **5.25.1** |

## 2026-06-11 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.39.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* Error objects now include a translated error message (`errorI18n`) for all payment form fields. Previously, only card payment fields (card number, expiry date, CVC) included translated messages.

### Fixed

* For the Select component: Screen readers now announce the number of filtered results or "no options found" when the shopper filters the list.
* For PayPal: The `@paypal/paypal-js` package is now a production dependency instead of a development dependency.
* For PayPal: The `actions.reject` callback in the `onSubmit` event now works correctly.
* For Click to Pay: When the shopper enters an invalid email address, the error message now suggests a valid format.
* For gift cards: The **Remove** button now includes an `aria-labelledby` attribute for screen readers.

## 2026-06-04 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.38.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For Click to Pay and PayTo: When the shopper submits the form with invalid fields, keyboard focus now moves to the first invalid field, and error announcements are improved for screen readers.
* Input fields now have improved border contrast.
* **(Internal change)** Updated to `typescript-eslint` with corresponding TypeScript type changes.

### Fixed

* The card payment fields now recognize `ionic://` domains.

* For the Card Component and Drop-in: Brand icon lists now use correct list semantics for screen readers.

* For Click to Pay:

  * The **Read more** text now has an improved contrast ratio.
  * The Visa system iframe is now fully hidden from screen readers and keyboard navigation.

* The Await component now announces its status text to screen readers (WCAG 4.1.3).

## 2026-06-03 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.11.1

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Fixed

* On iOS: Internal cleanup processes now run on the main thread. Previously, running them on a background thread could cause native code crashes.

## 2026-05-29 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.19.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

  | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |
  | [3D Secure 2 SDK](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.27) | 2.2.26 -> **2.2.27** |

## 2026-05-21 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.25.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* Supported payment method: Bizum. Payment method [type](https://docs.adyen.com/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **bizum**. An instant payment method available in Spain.
* For UPI: Added support for the Smart Intent flow, with automatic detection of installed UPI apps on the device.

### Fixed

* For Apple Pay: The `supportedNetworks` property now returns all `PKPaymentNetwork` variants that match a brand.

## 2026-05-20 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.37.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For UPI with the Intent flow: The list of installed UPI apps now has an improved layout.

### Fixed

* The SegmentedControl now uses correct `tablist` and `tabpanel` WAI-ARIA semantics.
* For the Card Component: Decorative images are now removed from the accessibility tree.
* **(Internal change)** The 3D Secure 2 redirect (MD) flow now uses the same data structures as the native flow, removing a legacy hybrid approach.

## 2026-05-13 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.11.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* For Apple Pay: You can now use the following new callback events and configuration properties to customize the Apple Pay payment sheet:

  * `onAuthorize`: Validate payment data before submission.
  * `onShippingContactChange` and `onShippingMethodChange`: React to the shopper's shipping selection and update summary items, shipping methods, or errors.
  * `onCouponCodeChange`, `supportsCouponCode`, and `couponCode`: Enable and manage a coupon code field in the Apple Pay sheet (iOS 15+).

* For card payments: You can now configure installment payments using the new `installmentOptions` configuration property. Set `installmentOptions` in the card payment method configuration to define default and card-brand-specific installment values and plans.

### Improved

* **(Internal code change)** Resolved SonarCloud maintainability issues across TypeScript, Swift (iOS), and Kotlin (Android) codebases.

### Fixed

* For iOS: `ADYCardViewCls` was renamed to `CardViewCls` for compatibility with Expo 52.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                      | Version              |
  | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |
  | [Android Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Android%2BComponents%2FDrop-in) | 5.17.0 -> **5.18.0** |
  | [iOS Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%2BComponents%2FDrop-in)         | 5.23.1 -> **5.24.0** |

## 2026-05-13 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.36.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* Support for [Adyen Giving donations with Sessions flow](/online-payments/donations#sessions-flow): If you have an active [Adyen Giving](/online-payments/donations) donation campaign, a donation form now automatically renders after a successful payment. To configure this, use the `donation` object in the `AdyenCheckout` configuration.

### Improved

* **(Internal change)** Some HTML elements now use semantic HTML instead of ARIA role overrides, improving accessibility for assistive technologies.

### Fixed

* For Click to Pay: The info modal title and the **Address** section title now use the correct heading level, so screen readers correctly convey the heading hierarchy.
* For Pay by Bank US with Drop-in: On narrow viewports, issuer brand icons in the payment method list no longer disappear. The icons now use the standard overflow pattern.
* For payment methods with a segmented control (for example, IRIS): The control no longer switches the order of payment modes after the shopper selects the **Pay** button.
* For dropdown fields: Screen readers now correctly announce the field name, the selected value, and the association with the dropdown list.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.36.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.36.0
```

## 2026-05-08 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.10.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* For Drop-in: You can now hide previously stored payment methods from the payment method list. Set `showStoredPaymentMethods` to **false** in `StoredPaymentMethodConfiguration`. The default is **true**.
* For standalone action handling: `ActionComponentConfiguration` now supports `ThreeDS2Configuration`, so you can set the `requestorAppURL` and customize the 3D Secure 2 UI.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                                             | Version              |
  | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |
  | [Android Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Android%2BComponents%2FDrop-in\&version%5B0%5D=5.18.0) | 5.17.0 -> **5.18.0** |
  | [iOS Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%2BComponents%2FDrop-in\&version%5B0%5D=5.24.0)         | 5.23.1 -> **5.24.0** |

## 2026-05-06 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.24.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Improved

* For co-badged cards: The UI is redesigned. For BCMC, Carte Bancaire, and Dankort, the shopper can select their preferred card brand directly inside the card field, with a description below. For other brands, the card field shows both brand logos, but the shopper cannot select them.

### Changed

* For the `didSubmit` delegate method (`PaymentComponentDelegate`): The size of the payload has increased because of changes to the included `sdkData` parameter.

## 2026-05-05 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.35.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For co-badged cards: When the shopper enters a co-badged card number, the shopper can select their preferred card brand.

  * In the EU: the first brand is pre-selected by default, and the selected brand is included in the payment request.
  * Outside the EU: brand logos are displayed for information only.

* The payload returned in the `onSubmit` callback has increased in size, because of changes to the included `sdkData` field.

* For co-badged cards: The UI is redesigned. For BCMC, Carte Bancaire, and Dankort, the shopper can select their preferred card brand directly inside the card field, with a description below. For other brands, the card field shows both brand logos, but the shopper cannot select them.

### Changed

* For the `onSubmit` callback: The size of the payload has increased because of changes to the included `sdkData` parameter.

## 2026-04-30 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.34.1

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For UPI Autopay: the `endsAt` property in the mandate object is now required.
* **(Internal change)** TypeScript types are now stricter for utilities, language, and UMD entry modules.

### Fixed

* Drop-in and Components now validate the `countryCode` configuration property when you create the `AdyenCheckout` instance.
* Form labels in the payment form now use bold font-weight.
* For Drop-in: payment method item details now use the HTML `inert` attribute, preventing focus when the payment method is not selected.
* For the Drop-in payment method list: overflowing text content no longer shows up past the list item boundaries.

## 2026-04-23 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.34.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* The bundle now includes English (**en-US**) translations as a fallback. If the translation request fails, the payment form defaults to English.

* Drop-in and Components now resolve partial or imprecise locale codes to the closest supported locale. For example, `nl` resolves to `nl-NL`, and `en-GB` resolves to `en-US`.

* Drop-in and Components now only request translations from the CDN for supported locales, eliminating unnecessary 404 errors.

* For UPI with Drop-in: the payment method list item now shows brand icons.

* For the Drop-in payment method list: when brands in the list include icons, the icons now adjust responsively on mobile screens.

* **(Internal change)** TypeScript types are now stricter for the following:

  * Amazon Pay Component
  * PayPal Component
  * Cash App Pay Component
  * Card Component
  * SecuredFields internal module

### Fixed

* An internal analytics request no longer blocks the UI from rendering during initialization. The payment form now appears faster.

## 2026-04-21 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.10.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* For cards:

  * You can now render the payment form directly in your React Native layout, without a full-screen modal. Use the new `<CardView>` component.

  * You can now validate card details before encrypting them. Use the following new methods in the `AdyenCSE` module:

    * `validateCardNumber`
    * `validateCardExpiryDate`
    * `validateCardSecurityCode`

### Deprecated

* The `AdyenCheckout.start("scheme")` flow is deprecated. Use the embedded `<CardView>` component instead. If you use the `AdyenCheckout.start("scheme")` flow, you now get a runtime warning. It will be removed in v3.0.0

* **(Internal code change)** Various dependency updates (security patches and tooling).

* For Google Pay: The button now updates correctly after the default card finishes loading.

  | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |

## 2026-04-15 — Checkout API — Version 72

### Breaking Changes

* Added validations for the following request parameters (for example: in the [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) or [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) request). If the values that you send in the parameters do not meet validation requirements, the request now fails. Go to our [v72 upgrade guide](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/upgrade-to-checkout-api-v72) to see the new validation requirement for each parameter.

  * `billingAddress.postalCode`
  * `billingAddress.stateOrProvinceCode`
  * `captureDelayHours`
  * `dateOfBirth`
  * `deliveryAddress.postalCode`
  * `deliveryAddress.stateOrProvinceCode`
  * `entityType`
  * `metadata`
  * `reference`
  * `returnUrl`
  * `shopperEmail`
  * `shopperIP`
  * `shopperName.firstName`
  * `shopperName.lastName`
  * `socialSecurityNumber`
  * `telephoneNumber`

### New

* For cards: validation for values sent in card holder name parameters, to prevent card numbers from being sent in the wrong parameters. For example: if the shopper enters the card number in the **Card holder name** field, and you send the card number in the `holderName` parameter, we now return the **Invalid card holder name** error message.

### Changed

* For Drop-in/Components using Advanced flow: in the [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) request, the data in the [sdkData](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments#request-paymentMethod-listOfValues-sdkData) object takes precedence over the SDK-related information you include. For example, if you include [`checkoutAttemptId`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/71/post/payments#request-fundRecipient-paymentMethod-checkoutAttemptId), the `sdkData.checkoutAttemptId` value takes precedence.
* For [Pix](/payment-methods/pix): the default session validity is now 24 hours. Previously, it was one hour.

### Deprecated

* Unused parameters are now [deprecated](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/api-diff-tool?api=Checkout\&from=71\&to=72).

### Removed

* Previously deprecated parameters have been [removed](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/api-diff-tool?api=Checkout\&from=71\&to=72\&breaking=true).

### Fixed

* For some validation and rate limit errors: we now return the **422** or **429** [HTTP status code](/development-resources/response-handling#http-responses) instead of **500**.
* For the [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) endpoint: when used with Drop-in, an error where the `returnUrl` included `payload` instead of `redirectResult` no longer occurs. Previously, this caused the integration to fail, because `redirectResult` is required to complete the payment.
* For the [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) request: in the `lineItems` object, when `taxAmount` and `amountExcludingTax` fields are not set, the `amountExludingTax` value is no longer incorrectly set equal to the `amountIncludingTax` value. You now receive an error to inform you that these fields are missing or invalid.
* For [partial payments](/online-payments/partial-payments) that go through 3D Secure 2 authentication: you now receive the order data in the API response.
* For the Sessions flow: when the shopper makes a payment with previously stored payment details, the type shopper interaction that we record internally is now correct.

## 2026-04-02 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.9.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=72">Checkout API v72\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* For the 3D Secure 2 Component: You can now customize the user interface using `Adyen3DSTheme`. This includes support for `fromThemeData()`, which lets you use the styling from your existing app theme.

* Supported payment method: BLIK. Payment method [type](https://docs.adyen.com/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **blik**. The shopper pays using a 6-digit code generated in their banking app.

* For card payments: You can now offer shoppers the option to pay in installments.

  * Sessions flow: Use the [`installmentOptions`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions#request-installmentOptions) parameter.
  * Advanced flow: Use the new [`installmentConfiguration`](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-flutter/blob/main/lib/src/common/model/payment_method_configurations/card/installment_configuration.dart) property.

### Improved

* The internal component architecture now uses a shared layer. This improves maintainability and makes it easier to extend components that are based on platform views.
* For the Card Component: The implementation is now simplified and complies with the updated component architecture.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                                             | Version              |
  | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |
  | [Android Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Android%2BComponents%2FDrop-in\&version%5B0%5D=5.17.0) | 5.16.0 -> **5.17.0** |
  | [iOS Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%2BComponents%2FDrop-in\&version%5B0%5D=5.23.1)         | 5.22.2 -> **5.23.1** |

## 2026-03-12 — Customer Area

### Changed

To provide greater control over the data users in your Customer Area can see, admin users can now directly manage the **Merchant view PII** role. **Merchant view PII** is the primary control for masking and unmasking sensitive shopper data like billing addresses in the Customer Area.

**What you need to know:**

* **Audit your user access**: We have automatically mapped users to the correct roles on our side to help you maintain your business continuity. We recommend limiting the availability of this role to as few users as possible. Navigate to **Settings > Users** to check user permissions and edit any that might not need this role.
* **Admins can view PII**: By default, users who have the **Merchant admin** role can view sensitive data. This allows the admin users to grant the **Merchant view PII** role to other users.

To learn more about user roles, see [Manage user roles](https://docs.adyen.com/account/user-roles#manage-user-roles).

### Fixed

* For Hungarian Forint (HUF) payments: Drop-in/Components now explicitly overrides HUF amounts to use two [minor units](/development-resources/currency-codes), so that the amount formatting is consistent on all iOS versions. ## 2026-02-19 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.16.1

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### Improved

* For Drop-in: accessibility services now correctly announce headings, providing a better experience for shoppers using screen readers.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                          | Version          |
  | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- |
  | [Adyen 3DS2](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.26) | 2.2.25 -> 2.2.26 |

### Fixed

* When the shopper uses a connected hardware keyboard, the primary button can now be selected using keyboard input. Previously, a keyboard navigation issue prevented interaction with the button.
* For Drop-in: when a shopper selects a stored payment method and a regular payment method simultaneously, a crash no longer occurs.
* For Google Pay: the availability check callback is no longer garbage collected while the check is running.
* For cards: when the card view is displayed in a bottom sheet or dialog, screen recording is no longer possible. This prevents screen recording of the card view in Drop-in.
* Using `SessionSetupConfiguration.SERIALIZER.serialize(...)` now correctly serializes the `installmentOptions` field.
* For Sessions flow: the key is now correctly passed when obtaining a Component.

## 2026-02-12 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.31.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* When the payment amount is updated, it now gets updated in the UI without re-initializing Drop-in or the Component.

### Improved

* For Japanese payments: if a bonus installment is available, the payment form now shows a **Bonus payment** radio button that the shopper can select.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Dependency                      | Version                                                                  |
  | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
  | [Preact](https://preactjs.com/) | 10.22.1 -> [**10.28.2**](https://www.npmjs.com/package/preact/v/10.28.2) |

### Fixed

* For card payments that support installments: when the shopper selects the dropdown menu for installments in the payment form, it no longer blocks the **Pay** button.

### Improved

* Removed unused CSS classes.

### Fixed

* The `checkoutAttemptId` is now correctly retrieved from the Analytics module.
* For Components that use `securedField` (such as Cards): the library now correctly detects if the containing element is missing from the DOM and generates a `console.error`. This prevents configuration errors where `Window` object to send the `securedField` iframe configuration message does not exist.

### Removed

* For UPI, the Collect flow has been removed to comply with NPCI regulations. The default payment flows are now:

  * QR code for desktop devices.
  * Intent for mobile devices. For more information, see [UPI](/payment-methods/upi/).

## 2026-01-27 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.8.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* TWINT is now supported in Drop-in and as a redirect instant component. Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **twint**.
* For card payments with Drop-in and the Card Component, you can now configure a `requestorAppURL` in your `ThreeDS2Configuration`.
* The `data` object in the `onSubmit` callback includes the `sdkData` parameter. For Advanced flow integrations, we strongly recommend that you include this in your `/payments` request.

### Improved

* For Drop-in on iOS, the payment amount you specified in your `/sessions` request is used to show the amount at checkout.
* Updated Gradle and AGP to v8.13.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                                                                  | Version |
  | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- |
  | [iOS Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%20Components%2FDrop-in#releaseNote=2025-12-18-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.22.1) | 5.22.1  |
  | [Android Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-12-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.16.0)                                 | 5.16.0  |
  | [Android Gradle Plugin](https://developer.android.com/build/releases/gradle-plugin)                                                                   | 8.13.2  |

## 2026-01-20 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.29.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* Supported payment method: [IRIS](https://www.nbg.gr/en/individuals/daily-transactions/digital-banking/internet-mobile-banking-capabilities/iris-payments). Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **iris**.
* For Japanese convenience stores: When the voucher includes an alternative reference, the voucher now shows the alternative reference to the shopper.
  * For Advanced flow: When an alternative reference is available, the `action.alternativeReference` field is included in the `/payments` response.
* The new `sdkData` property is now included in the base `data` object.

## 2026-01-16 — Hosted Checkout

### Breaking Changes

You can no longer embed the Hosted Checkout page in an [`iframe` element](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Reference/Elements/iframe).

## 2026-01-16 — Pay by Link

### Breaking Changes

You can no longer embed the Pay by Link page in an [`iframe` element](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Reference/Elements/iframe).

## 2026-01-14 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.9.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                     | New version                                                                                                 |
  | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
  | [iOS Drop-in/Components](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-ios/releases/tag/5.22.1)         | 5.22.1                                                                                                      |
  | [Android Drop-in/Components](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-android/releases/tag/5.16.0) | 5.16.0                                                                                                      |
  | `react-server-dom-webpack`                                                               | 19.0.3. This addresses the [CVE-2025-55182](https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2025-55182) vulnerability. |

### Fixed

* For iOS:

  * When the application leaves the foreground, the `/session` request is no longer recreated.
  * When an invalid client key is provided, the error is now handled and provides a human-readable error message.

* For the Android Twint Component: instant payments no longer cause the app to crash.

* When a network error occurs during an internal session fetch, the app no longer crashes.

* For the **Example App**:

  * A bug related to session fetch errors no longer occurs.
  * A UI bug caused by rapid navigation after a card payment no longer occurs.

### Improved

* Source code cleanup: fixed code smells and reduced code duplication.

* Moved `plugin` inside `src`.

* For the **Example App:**

  * After an `onError` call, the session is now refreshed.
  * [Credentials from an untracked `secrets.json` file](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-react-native/blob/develop/example/README.md#step-2-provide-credentials) are now used.

### New

* The `paymentMethod` object now includes `sdkData`. For Advanced flow, we recommend that you include this parameter in your `/payments` request.
* For the Expo plugin: added support for overrides on the extended `MainActivity`.

## 2026-01-13 — Hosted Checkout

The UI for the shopper has been redesigned.

### Improved

* In the header:

  * The logo now remains visible on the screen when the shopper moves down the payment form.
  * The logo is now centered.

* In the payment form:

  * The background is now a single color. The background can no longer be an image.

  * The list of payment methods where the shopper selects a payment method is now in a dedicated section.

  * The order summary section now shows the following information:

  * The total payment amount.

  * The order reference number that you provide.

  * The description of the purchase, if you provide it.

  * For the section where the shopper enters their payment details:

  * The input fields are separate from the list of payment methods. Previously, the input fields were above the list of payment methods.

  * The following fields now have a clear format:

    * **Name**
    * **Billing address**
    * **Delivery address**

  * The shopper can now edit their personal details before submitting the payment.

* In the footer: the link for **Terms and Conditions** can now go directly to your URL.

## 2026-01-13 — Pay by Link

The UI for the shopper has been redesigned.

### Improved

* In the header:
  * The logo now remains visible on the screen when the shopper moves down the payment form.

* In the payment form:

  * The background is now a single color. The background can no longer be an image.

  * The list of payment methods where the shopper selects a payment method is now in a dedicated section.

  * The order summary section now shows the following information:

  * The total payment amount.

  * The order reference number that you provide.

  * The description of the purchase, if you provide it.

  * For the section where the shopper enters their payment details:

  * The input fields are separate from the list of payment methods. Previously, the input fields were above the list of payment methods.

  * The following fields now have a clear format:

    * **Name**
    * **Billing address**
    * **Delivery address**

  * The shopper can now edit their personal details before submitting the payment.

* In the footer: the link for **Terms and Conditions** can now go directly to your URL.

## 2026-01-07 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.28.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For payment methods where the shopper selects an option before submitting their payment (for example, selecting a bank from a dropdown menu):

  * The keyboard no longer automatically expands when the selection element receives focus.
  * The selection element now includes the `aria-disabled` and `aria-expanded` attributes.

### Fixed

* For the **Button** component: the `aria-live` attribute now enables screen readers to announce the loading and redirect states.
* For the 3D Secure 2 Component: fixed an issue where properties were manually added due to legacy code from the previous major version (v5.0.0).
* When the payment form includes input fields for the partial billing address: the postal code field now correctly shows the localized label. For example, it shows **Zip code** for the US, and **Postal code** for other countries/regions.

### Updating

## 2025-12-19 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.22.1

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Improved

* For Apple Pay:

  * For co-badged cards, the network selection now follows the order in the `brands` array provided by the backend.
  * Improved errors handling when no networks are available.

## 2025-12-19 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.16.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* In the `paymentMethod` object: `sdkData` has been added.\
  For Advanced flow integrations, we highly recommend that you include this in your `/payments` request.
* For Google Pay: you can now allow or block cards issued in specific countries. To do this, use the `allowedIssuerCountryCodes` and `blockedIssuerCountryCodes` configuration properties.

### Fixed

* For Components: when the shopper puts your app into the background and returns, the Components retain their state. Previously, Components were recreated and lost their state.

### Deprecated

* The following properties are now deprecated.

  | Property                | Previous parent object |
  | ----------------------- | ---------------------- |
  | `supportNativeRedirect` | `PaymentComponentData` |
  | `checkoutAttemptId`     | `PaymentMethod`        |
  | `threeDS2SdkVersion`    | `CardPaymentMethod`    |

  These properties are now in the new sdkData object.

* For the [Klarna](/payment-methods/klarna) widget: when a purchase is refused, the `details` parameter is now correctly populated.

## 2025-12-18 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.22.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### New

* For Apple Pay:

  * You can now validate payment details, such as the billing address, before the payment is submitted. To do this, set the `authorizationDelegate` property on your `ApplePayComponent` instance, and implement the [`didAuthorize(payment:completion:)` method](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-ios/blob/b952aeb041dd8094cc74fb8e21b2051f446b80e4/Demo/Common/IntegrationExamples/AdvancedFlow/Components/ApplePayComponentAdvancedFlowExample.swift#L201) in the new `ApplePayAuthorizationDelegate` protocol.
  * you can now choose to have the Apple Pay sheet dismiss automatically after the payment flow finishes. Set the new [`dismissesAutomatically` configuration property](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-ios/blob/b952aeb041dd8094cc74fb8e21b2051f446b80e4/Demo/Common/IntegrationExamples/AdvancedFlow/Components/ApplePayComponentAdvancedFlowExample.swift#L70) to **true** to enable this.

* The `sdkData` property in the `PaymentMethodDetails` protocol. For Advanced flow: we highly recommended that you include it in your [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) request, in `paymentMethod.sdkData`.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Dependency                                                                    | Version |
  | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- |
  | [3D Secure 2 SDK](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-ios/releases/tag/2.4.4) | 2.4.4   |

### Deprecated

* The following properties are now deprecated.

  | Property                | Parent object          |
  | ----------------------- | ---------------------- |
  | `supportNativeRedirect` | `PaymentComponentData` |
  | `checkoutAttemptId`     | `PaymentMethod`        |
  | `threeDS2SdkVersion`    | `CardPaymentMethod`    |

  These properties are now in the new `sdkData` object.

## 2025-12-11 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.27.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* For all [select elements](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Reference/Elements/select) in the payment form: a [focus](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/Reference/Selectors/:focus) border now appears on the active item.

### Improved

* The `SRPanelProvider` is now in `UIElement`, to reduce the number of required imports.

### Fixed

* A runtime error no longer occurs when an external script finishes loading after the Component has been unmounted. Previously, this caused a runtime error.

## 2025-12-04 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.26.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* For internal analytics: the `configData` property is now included in the data sent for payment methods.

* In the payment form:

  * Brand icons now display a box shadow.
  * Valid fields now display a black checkmark.

### Improved

* For analytics:

  * When you create an instance of `AdyenCheckout`, the `checkoutAttemptId` is now requested immediately. This checkout attempt identifier acts as a unique key to track the checkout session.
  * The integration type (Drop-in or Component) is now in the `flavor` property when the checkout UI renders.

* For open invoice payment methods like Oney, Riverty, and RatePay: the **Gender** field has been removed, to comply with payment industry requirements and for improved accessibility.

* For Click to Pay: labels that were incorrect are now correct, and text that was duplicated has now been removed. This improves accessibility for screen readers.

### Fixed

* For [Pay By Bank (US)](/payment-methods/pay-by-bank-us): the `showOtherInsteadOfNumber` configuration property is now spelled correctly. Previously, it was incorrectly spelled as `showOtherInsteafOfNumber`.
* For [Click to Pay One-time passcode (OTP)](https://developer.mastercard.com/click-to-pay-customer-experience-guide/documentation/cx-patterns/one-time-passcode/) and [UPI](/payment-methods/upi): when the shopper enters invalid input, the field now correctly displays an error message.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.26.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.26.0
```

## 2025-11-18 — Checkout API

### New

In the [/storedPaymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/get/storedPaymentMethods) response:

* The [storedPaymentMethods.mandate](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/get/storedPaymentMethods#responses-200-storedPaymentMethods-mandate) object. For stored payment details that have an associated mandate that specifies the how the payment details can be used (for example: a [mandate for SEPA Direct Debit](https://www.europeanpaymentscouncil.eu/what-we-do/epc-payment-schemes/sepa-direct-debit/sdd-mandate)), you now get the details of the mandate.

  Example response including the `mandate` object for stored details for UPI Intent:

  ```bash
  {
    "merchantAccount": "ADYEN_MERCHANT_ACCOUNT",
    "shopperReference": "your-shopper-reference-123",
    "storedPaymentMethods": [
      {
        "brand": "upi_intent",
        "id": "Z58FGTKBRCQ2WN27",
        "mandate": {
          "amount": "7005",
          "amountRule": "max",
          "billingAttemptsRule": "on",
          "billingDay": "24",
          "currency": "INR",
          "endsAt": "2026-08-09",
          "frequency": "adhoc",
          "mandateId": "123abc-abc123...",
          "providerId": "123abc-abc123...",
          "remarks": "mandate name/remarks",
          "startsAt": "2024-08-07",
          "status": "active",
          "txVariant": "upi_intent"
          },
          "supportedRecurringProcessingModels": [
            "CardOnFile",
            "Subscription",
            "UnscheduledCardOnFile"
          ],
        "type": "upi"
      }
    ]
  }
  ```

## 2025-11-12 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.21.1

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Fixed

* For PayTo: fixed phone number formatting to include the missing hyphen between country code and local number in the [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) request, for example: **+61-0666555444**.

## 2025-11-11 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.15.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Fixed

* For SEPA: when the shopper returns to the app after switching away, the component now correctly retains its previous state.
* For 3D Secure 2: if the shopper selects **Cancel** before the challenge is displayed, the challenge is no longer displayed.
* On devices running Android 15 and later: the `onError()` callback is no longer triggered for **Await**, **QR code**, and **Twint** actions when the shopper leaves and returns to the app.

### Improved

* For PayTo: the phone number input now allows leading zeros.
* For PIX: the QR code is now displayed, and the copyable code is displayed above the **Copy code** button.

## 2025-11-06 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.25.1

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For QR code and voucher payment methods: internal logic and styling updates for a more consistent user experience and better accessibility.

### Fixed

* For the Card Component: the `onAdditionalDetails` event handler now triggers correctly when defined directly on the Component instance. This fixes a regression from [v6.24.0](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-web/releases/tag/v6.24.0) where it would fail to trigger.

## 2025-11-06 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.7.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### New

* Pay by Bank is now supported as an instant component. Payment method [type](https://docs.adyen.com/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **paybybank**.

* Support for Swift Package Manager (SPM). When migrating an existing project to Swift Package Manager (SPM), you must manually import modules in your `AppDelegate.swift`:

  * `import AdyenActions`
  * If you apply custom styling to Drop-in, you must also include `import AdyenDropIn`.

### Improved

* For Apple Pay Component: the instantiation of the widget is now more efficient.
* For the Instant Payment Component: streamlined the implementation for Android by removing redundant code.
* For Components: the communication of `platformView` height now includes a type specification.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                                                                                        | Version |
  | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- |
  | [iOS Drop-in/Components](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%20Components%2FDrop-in#releaseNote=2025-11-04-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.21.0) | 5.21.0  |

## 2025-10-28 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.21.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

## 2025-10-24 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.24.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

For the Card Component: you must define the `onAdditionalDetails` event handler as a [configuration property of `AdyenCheckout`](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/build-your-integration/advanced-flow?platform=Web\&integration=Components\&version=6.23.0#add). Do not define it as a property of the Card Component. If you do so, it fails to trigger.

### Improved

* For the Card Component: the **Expiry date** and **Security code** fields are now stacked vertically on smaller screens to improve layout and usability.

### Fixed

* For Apple Pay: when used as an instant payment method, Apple Pay is now correctly set as the active payment method.
* When an action is initiated using `createFromAction`, the `onAdditionalDetails` event handler now correctly handles the resulting action.

### Updating

* For apps built with [Xcode 26](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/xcode-release-notes/xcode-26-release-notes): a crash caused by `ObservationManager` no longer occurs when a Component is deallocated.

## 2025-10-14 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.8.1

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### Fixed

* For Expo integrations:

  * `expo prebuild` now correctly generates imports in `MainActivity.kt`.
  * You are no longer required to use the `--clean` flag when running `expo prebuild` to avoid generating duplicate lines.

## 2025-10-09 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.23.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* For the Apple Pay Component: you can now use the `domainName` configuration property to set which domain performs the merchant validation request. This is useful when embedding the Component within an iframe.
* Supported payment method: Bank Transfer is now available in more countries.
* Secured Fields v6.0.0: includes a new JWE version, removes the ACH bundle, and disallows the "compat" version on live environments.

### Improved

* For Fastlane:

  * The legal text has been updated.
  * New analytics events have been added to the shopper recognition flow.

* For Pix: the full bank code is now displayed to allow shoppers to highlight and copy it directly.

* For PayTo: the phone number input field now accepts leading zeros.

* For Google Pay: card brands returned in the [/paymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods) response are now parsed and displayed.

* The Segmented Controller Component is now more responsive on different screen sizes and the font size has been adjusted for better readability.

* When loading third-party scripts, a retry mechanism is now in place to improve resilience against network issues.

* New analytics events are now sent when loading third-party script SDKs.

### Fixed

* For Klarna: the widget can now be opened using the **Enter** key, improving keyboard accessibility.
* For UPI: the checkmark in the app selector now displays correctly.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.23.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.23.0
```

## 2025-10-02 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.8.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* You can now use the new Fabric Native Components: `ApplePayButton` for iOS and `GooglePayButton` for Android.
* For the `AdyenApplePay` and `AdyenGooglePay` modules: you can now use the `isAvailable` method to asynchronously check if a shopper can make a payment before showing the payment button.
* For the `useAdyenCheckout` hook: the new `isReady` property provides an observable state for the readiness of the `<AdyenCheckout>` context.
* The `onSubmit` payload now includes a `supportNativeRedirect` parameter. Pass this to the `/payments` request to improve app-to-app redirects.

### Fixed

* For the Sessions flow: the `onComplete` method from the `<AdyenCheckout>` context now correctly returns a `SessionsResult`.
* For Voucher payments in the Advanced flow: the `onComplete` method is now correctly called.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                                 | Version |
  | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- |
  | React Native                                                                                                         | 0.78.3  |
  | [iOS Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2025-09-23-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.1)         | 5.20.1  |
  | [Android Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0) | 5.14.0  |

## 2025-10-01 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 2.8.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### New

* **Fabric Native Components:** We've introduced new **Fabric Native Components**: `ApplePayButton` for iOS and `GooglePayButton` for Android.
* **Payment Availability Checks:** The `AdyenApplePay` and `AdyenGooglePay` modules now include an **`isAvailable` method**. This allows developers to asynchronously check if a payment can be made before presenting the corresponding button to the user.
* **Context State Observability:** A new **`isReady` property** has been added to the `useAdyenCheckout` hook, providing an observable state for the `<AdyenCheckout>` context's readiness.
* **Improved Redirects:** The payment data payload in `onSubmit` now includes a **`supportNativeRedirect` field**. Passing this property to the `/payments` API improves app-to-app redirects.

### Changed

* Dependencies:

  * **React Native SDK:** The SDK now uses **React Native v0.78.3**.
  * **iOS SDK:** Updated to **5.20.1**.
  * **Android Drop-in:** Updated to **5.14.0**.

### Improved

* Example App

  * The Example app now uses **React Native v0.78.3**.
  * The Example app has been **refactored to TypeScript** for improved type safety.

* **Project Structure:** We've introduced a **better folder structure** for improved organization and maintainability.

### Fixed

* **Sessions Flow:** The `onComplete` method signature from the `<AdyenCheckout>` context now correctly returns a **`SessionsResult`** for the Sessions flow.
* **Voucher Payments:** The `onComplete` method is now also used for Voucher payments in the Advanced flow.

## 2025-09-23 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.20.1

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### Fixed

* For Drop-in analytics: when only one payment method is available and renders on the screen, the render analytics event is now sent.

## 2025-09-15 — Hosted Checkout

### Removed

* For Mail Order/Telephone Order (MOTO) transactions: Call Center is no longer available. Use [Virtual Terminal](/unified-commerce/mail-order-telephone-order/customer-area/) instead.

## 2025-09-12 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.22.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* Support for **UPI Autopay**: shoppers in India can now make recurring payments with UPI.

  * Sessions flow: in the [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) request, include the `recurringProcessingModel` parameter.
  * Advanced flow: in the [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) request, include the `recurringProcessingModel` parameter.

### Improved

* For analytics: the `checkoutAttemptId` is now correctly stored, so that tracking the payment attempt is more reliable when the shopper refreshes the checkout page.

### Fixed

* For Drop-in: keyboard navigation is now predictable when multiple payment methods with issuer lists are present. This prevents the focus from jumping incorrectly, improving accessibility for keyboard-only users.
* For the PayTo Component: the `aria-controls` attribute now points to the correct element ID, so that shoppers that use screen readers get accurate feedback when interacting with the Component.
* For address forms: the `OnAddressSelectedType` TypeScript type is now the same as the returned address object, preventing incorrect type errors.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.22.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.22.0
```

The base URLs for the script source are different for each environment. Use the one that corresponds to your [live endpoint](/development-resources/live-endpoints#checkout-endpoints):

| Europe (EU) live                     | `https://checkoutshopper-live.cdn.adyen.com`      |
| United States (US) live              | `https://checkoutshopper-live-us.cdn.adyen.com`   |

## 2025-08-27 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.20.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* Updated [3D Secure 2 SDK to version 2.4.3](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-ios/releases/tag/2.4.3). This version includes a fix for a bug where an object was prematurely released, which could cause the transaction to fail.

### Improved

**Improved the user experience for the UPI component:**

* The QR code flow has been removed. The shopper can pay the following ways:

  * Intent flow: the shopper pays with an installed UPI app.
  * Collect flow: the shopper enters their UPI ID.

* Added new instructions labels for better clarity across all UPI payment flows.

* Minor changes in design to improve the user experience.

* On iOS 17, fixed a bug that caused a crash when `present(viewController:)` was called multiple times in a short timespan. This fixes [Github issue #2182](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-ios/issues/2182).

## 2025-08-27 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.16.0

### New

* Updated [3D Secure 2 SDK to version 2.4.3](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-ios/releases/tag/2.4.3). This version includes a fix for a bug where an object was prematurely released, which could cause the transaction to fail.

## 2025-08-20 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.21.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For PIX using Open Finance: UI changes that improve the shopper's checkout experience.

### Fixed

* For the Pay button: updated the alignment and font size of the secondary amount label.
* For co-badged cards: the UI no longer automatically selects a card brand outside of Europe. This allows us to continue least-cost routing (LCR) outside of Europe.
* For ACH: the **Account holder name** field can now be correctly pre-filled based on the data configuration that you set.

## 2025-08-19 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.14.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### Fixed

* For components that require text input from the shopper: there is no longer a compatibility issue with Material (from version [1.13.0-alpha12](https://github.com/material-components/material-components-android/releases/tag/1.13.0-alpha12) or later) that causes a crash.
* When the device font is large, the input fields now get larger to fit the text.
* When the shopper selects the **Pay** button, the keyboard now automatically gets hidden.

### Changed

* For UPI:

  * The input field for the UPI ID is now in a more user-friendly location on the screen.
  * The option to pay by QR code is removed.

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                          | Now          | Previously |
  | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------- |
  | [Adyen 3DS2](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.25) | ***2.2.25*** | **2.2.24** |

### Deprecated

* The `Threeds2FingerprintAction` and `Threeds2ChallengeAction` actions are now deprecated. Use `Threeds2Action` instead.

* Configuration and configuration builder classes for Drop-in, payment methods, and actions are now deprecated. Use `CheckoutConfiguration` instead.

* Configuration setter methods are replaced by property access syntax. For example:

  ```kotlin
  CheckoutConfiguration(
      environment = environment,
      clientKey = clientKey,
  ) {
      card {
          // Deprecated
          setHolderNameRequired(true)
          // Replacement
          isHolderNameRequired = true
      }
  }
  ```

* The `setSubmitButtonVisible` method for configuration objects is now deprecated. Use `CheckoutConfiguration` instead:

  ```kotlin
      CheckoutConfiguration(
          ...
          isSubmitButtonVisible = false
      ) {
          ...
      }
  ```

## 2025-08-15 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.7.3

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Fixed

* **Android Standalone Action**: when the user swipes or taps outside the dialogue view, the redirect action dialog no longer disappears.
* **GooglePay**: the Google Pay sheet no longer opens randomly.

## 2025-08-12 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.20.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For UPI:

  * On mobile devices, the QR code flow has been removed. The shopper now pays with their installed UPI apps (intent flow) or by entering their UPI ID.
  * On desktop devices, the QR code flow is now the default. The shopper can choose to pay with UPI ID instead.
  * A validation alert now asks the shopper to select a UPI app before making the payment.
  * For all payment flows, the text labels now give clear instructions.

### Fixed

* For Click to Pay: if the payment card has the **ACTIVE** or **PENDING** status, it is not treated as expired.

* The installments dropdown menu now has the border and background color. Previously, a styling issue caused the border and background color to be missing.

* The Icelandic translation for the `storeDetails` property is now correct.

* TypeScript fixes:

  * The `PaymentData` interface now includes billing address and shopper details.
  * `CardConfiguration` now includes the `onAddressSelected` callback type.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.20.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.20.0
```
