## 2025-08-04 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.7.2

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Changed

* * Example app version: [React Native v0.77.1](https://www.reactnative.com/react-native-v0-77-released).

### Fixed

* Drop-in on Android now works with [New Architecture](https://docs.expo.dev/guides/new-architecture/). It no longer requires [bridgeless](https://github.com/reactwg/react-native-new-architecture/discussions/154) mode.

## 2025-07-18 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.7.1

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* Added **Swift support** for [Expo](https://expo.dev/).

### Improved

* Updated versions:

  | SDK                        | Version                                                                                                                            |
  | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
  | iOS Drop-in/Components     | [5.19.1](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%20Components%2FDrop-in\&version%5B0%5D=5.19.1)                           |
  | Android Drop-in/Components | [5.13.1](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Android%20Components%2FDrop-in\&version%5B0%5D=5.13.1) |

* **Apple Pay:** the amount displayed on the payment sheet is now correctly updated after a partial payment.

### Fixed

* **iOS Drop-in:** Gift cards and meal vouchers are now correctly displayed in the payment method list.
* **Android Redirect:** The redirect action dialog no longer disappears when a user swipes or taps outside the dialog view.
* **Android Drop-in:** The status bar color is now transparent in the base theme.
* **Cards:** The UI for selecting a card brand for a [co-badged card](/online-payments/co-badged-cards-compliance/) now only appears for supported brands.

## 2025-07-14 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.19.2

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

* For Apple Pay: the amount on the payment sheet is now correctly updated after a partial payment.

## 2025-07-11 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.19.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* For bank transfer payment methods: UI changes give an improved shopper experience and accessibility elements.

### Improved

* For Drop-in: when the shopper selects a stored card, the mechanism to show the name of the brand and `alt` text is improved.
* For ACH: the pay button now has the text **Pay {amount}**. Previously the text was **Continue purchase**.

## 2025-07-07 — Checkout API

### New

* For the [/payments/{paymentPspReference}/captures](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/\(paymentPspReference\)/captures) request: the `enhancedSchemeData` parameter. You can use it, for example, for data required for payments in the airline industry.

  We recommend that you use this instead of the [Classic API /capture](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Payment/latest/post/capture) endpoint.

## 2025-07-02 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.13.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

If you use 3DS2 with Drop-in or Components, and your app targets **SDK version 36 (Android 16)**, upgrading to this version is important to ensure proper compatibility and avoid potential issues during build or runtime.

### New

* For Google Pay, added the `checkoutOption` configuration which you can use to change the submit button text shown in the Google Pay sheet.

### Fixed

* For card payments, the expiry date field now works correctly when the shopper leaves the app, and resumes it from the background. This fixes the known issue in earlier versions.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                          | Version |
  | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- |
  | [Adyen 3DS2](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.24) | 2.2.24  |

## 2025-06-27 — Checkout API

### Changed

* For the [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) endpoint:
  * In the response, the length of the `sessionId` value has increased to 25 characters.

## 2025-06-27 — Pay by Link

### Changed

* For the [/paymentLinks](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentLinks) endpoint:
  * In the response, the length of the `id` value has increased to 25 characters.

## 2025-06-24 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.7.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* iOS Components/Drop-in version: [5.19.1](/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2025-06-16-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.19.1).
* Android Components/Drop-in version: [5.12.0](/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Android%20Components%2FDrop-in#releaseNote=2025-06-06-android-componentsdrop-in-5.12.0).
* When the shopper enters the details for a co-badged card, the available brand options are now shown in a separate section in the payment form.
* If you use Expo, the minimum version requirement is now 52 due to Android Target API Level requirements.

### Improved

* Improved the UI for stored cards in the [example app](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-react-native/tree/develop/example).

### Known Issues

* For card payments with Drop-in and the Component, the card expiry date field functions incorrectly when the shopper leaves the app, and resumes it from the background. We will fix this issue in the next release.

## 2025-06-18 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.18.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* For bank transfer payment methods: added support for the following payment method types (`TxVariants` values).

  * **bankTransfer\_BE**
  * **bankTransfer\_NL**
  * **bankTransfer\_PL**
  * **bankTransfer\_FR**
  * **bankTransfer\_CH**
  * **bankTransfer\_IE**
  * **bankTransfer\_GB**
  * **bankTransfer\_DE**

### Improved

* To follow [a11y](https://www.a11yproject.com) guidelines: for screen reader errors related to gift card payment methods, improved how errors are handled.
* For Fastlane: the utility function on the Universal Module Definition (UMD) bundle is now exposed.

## 2025-06-05 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.16.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* For UPI: the input field for the UPI ID now has the label **UPI ID**. Previously, it had the label **Enter UPI ID**.
* For cards: when the shopper pays with a co-badged card, the UI now preselects the brand and clearly gives the shopper the option to choose their preferred brand. This UI complies with guidelines from card schemes.

### Improved

* To follow [a11y](https://www.a11yproject.com) guidelines, when only one payment method is available, Drop-in shows the payment method in a [non-semantic `div` element](https://www.w3schools.com/html/html5_semantic_elements.asp). Previously, Drop-in showed a radio button.

## 2025-05-28 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.18.1

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### Fixed

* **Improved card brand display for co-badged cards, for a clearer and more familiar shopper experience.**\
  When the shopper uses a co-badged card, the UI now displays the local brand name (for example, **Bancontact card**). If a local brand name is not available, the generic brand type (for example, **BCMC**) is used as a fallback.

## 2025-05-23 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.18.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### New

* For cards: when the shopper enters the details for a co-badged card in the payment form, they can now select the card brand they want to pay with.
* When you are using an [enforced locale](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-ios/blob/develop/Adyen.docc/Localization.md#enforcing-locale), you can now add new localizations or override strings.

### Fixed

* For the Bancontact Card Component (`BCMCComponent`): when you specify [custom localization parameters](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-react-native/blob/develop/docs/Localization.md), the Component now correctly uses them.

## 2025-05-16 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.6.1

### Fixed

* Fixed an issue where running `expo prebuild` could generate incorrect `imports` in `MainActivity.kt`.
* Running `expo prebuild`, no longer requires the `--clean` flag and does not generate duplicate lines.
* For Android, fixed a crash in `AddressLookup` that was caused by a missing address field in the `onUpdateAddress` array.

## 2025-05-16 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.11.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### Fixed

* For Drop-in:

  * [Predictive back navigation](https://developer.android.com/guide/navigation/custom-back/predictive-back-gesture) is disabled to be compatible with Android 16.
  * When the payment form loads, the first input field is now automatically in focus.

* When an analytics event fails to be tracked, because the Component was cleared unexpectedly, Drop-in/Components no longer crashes.

* For the address lookup feature, the full country name in the **Search for your address** field is now shown. Previously, the country code was shown.

### Improved

* For Card Component: when the shopper enters the details for a co-badged card, they can now see the available brand options in a separate section in the payment form.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                | Version |
  | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- |
  | [Android Gradle Plugin](https://developer.android.com/build/releases/gradle-plugin) | 8.8.1   |

## 2025-05-15 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.14.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* The Riverty Component is now a redirect component. When the shopper pays with Riverty, they get redirect to a Riverty-hosted page to complete the payment.

### Improved

* For Drop-in: you can now use the `filterStoredPaymentMethods` callback to choose which stored payment methods are displayed in the payment form.

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.14.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.14.0
```

## 2025-05-06 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.13.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* Supported payment method:

  | Name               | [Payment method type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types/) |
  | ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
  | Fastlane by PayPal | **fastlane**                                                  |

* For Apple Pay, you can now disable editing the shipping address. You can use this for checkout options with in-store pickup. ## 2025-05-06 — Pay by Link

### Fixed

* For Google Pay, Apple Pay, and other payment methods: when a payment is successful, the error message with **request\_timeout** no longer shows up.
* For the **Terms and conditions** modal: when the shopper's `shopperLocale` language is the same as the browser's language setting, the text no longer gets set to English.

## 2025-04-30 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.6.0

### New

* [Expo Prebuild](https://docs.expo.dev/workflow/continuous-native-generation/#usage) now works with both modern Android API level 52 or later, and earlier levels.
* For cards, you can now configure the `onBinLookup` and `onBinValue` callbacks.
* Android Components/Drop-in version: [5.10.0](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Android%2BComponents%2FDrop-in#releaseNote=2025-04-07-android-componentsdrop-in-5.10.0).
* iOS Components/Drop-in version: [5.17.0](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%2BComponents%2FDrop-in#releaseNote=2025-04-08-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.17.0).
  * For [native 3D Secure 2](/online-payments/3d-secure/native-3ds2/?platform=iOS\&integration=Drop-in\&version=5.0.0%20and%20later), when a shopper cancels the payment during the payment flow, the `didProvide(...)` delegate method is now triggered.
* Payment method: [PayTo](https://docs.adyen.com/payment-methods/payto/), with support for recurring payments. [Payment method type](https://docs.adyen.com/payment-methods/payment-method-types/): **payto**.

### Fixed

* Fixed an issue where iDeal opened in the Drop-in instead of the instant payment component.
* For iOS, fixed a modal presentation issue when `SFSafariViewController` is dismissed with its presenter.

## 2025-04-22 — Pay by Link

### New

* Supported payment method: PayTo. [Payment method type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types/): **payto**.

## 2025-04-22 — Hosted Checkout

### New

* Supported payment method: PayTo. [Payment method type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types/): **payto**.

## 2025-04-22 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.4.0

### New

* For Sessions and Advanced flows, added methods to dismiss Drop-in.
* For the card component and cards within Drop-in, added support for BIN lookup callbacks that will be\
  called when the shopper enters their card details.
* Added a custom card Flutter widget to the example app using CSE and card validators.
* Android Components/Drop-in version: [5.10.0](/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-04-07-android-componentsdrop-in-5.10.0).
* iOS Components/Drop-in version: [5.17.0](/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-04-08-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.17.0).

### Changed

* Minimum SDK version: Flutter [3.16.0](https://docs.flutter.dev/release/release-notes/release-notes-3.16.0)/Dart 3.2.

### Breaking Changes

* For native 3D Secure 2, when a shopper cancels the payment during the payment flow, the\
  `onAdditionalDetails` callback is now triggered. The payment flow no longer ends with\
  **CANCELLED\_BY\_USER**. The `onAdditionalDetails` callback returns the details of the cancelled transaction.

## 2025-04-16 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.12.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Improved

* For ACH, added a drop-down to select account type and input field to verify the account number.
* `browserInfo.ts` no longer calls the deprecated `navigator.javaEnabled` function.

### Fixed

* We made corrections to Finnish translations.
* Added missing Typescript types to `CustomCardConfiguration` and `ApplePayButtonType`.
* For Google Pay, we fixed an issue where the component did not recognize the `paywithgoogle` payment method.

## 2025-04-11 — Hosted Checkout

### Changed

* For Google Pay, you can no longer use **paywithgoogle** as a TX variant, use **googlepay** instead.

## 2025-04-11 — Pay by Link

### Changed

* For Google Pay, you can no longer use **paywithgoogle** as a TX variant, use **googlepay** instead.

## 2025-04-08 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.17.0

### New

* Payment method: [PayTo](/payment-methods/payto). [Payment method type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types/): **payto**.

### Changed

* [Adyen Networking for iOS](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-networking-ios) version: 3.0.1
* [Adyen WeChat Pay for iOS](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-wechatpay-ios) version: 2.2.0

### Fixed

* For the card component:

  * Updated the separator layout to to be centered in the container view.
  * The security code and card number logo edges are now aligned with the input field edges.

## 2025-04-07 — Hosted Checkout

### Removed

* The back button on the payment page. Previously, the shopper could select the back button while the payment was processing, which caused issues with the payment session.

## 2025-04-07 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.10.0

### New

* Payment method: [PayTo](/payment-methods/payto), with support for recurring payments. [Payment method type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types/): **payto**.

### Fixed

* For 3D Secure 2, the subsequent challenges after an initial failed challenge can still be completed successfully. Previously, the subsequent challenges failed automatically.

## 2025-03-31 — Checkout API

### New

* Added the `encryptedCard` parameter to the [/storedPaymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/storedPaymentMethods) endpoint. This parameter is used for [card encryption with JWE](/online-payments/card-encryption-with-jwe/#encrypt-card-details).

## 2025-03-28 — Pay by Link

### New

* Pay by Link is now using [Web Components v6.9.0](/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2025-02-28-web-componentsdrop-in-6.9.0).

## 2025-03-28 — Hosted Checkout

### New

* Pay by Link is now using [Web Components v6.9.0](/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2025-02-28-web-componentsdrop-in-6.9.0).

## 2025-03-24 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.11.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For cards, when the shopper enters their card number in the **Card number** field, the length of the card number is no longer restricted based on the Bank Identification Number (BIN).

### Fixed

* For Apple Pay in apps that use [server-side rendering](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Glossary/SSR): the iframe detection code no longer crashes when rendering. This fixes the known issue from v6.10.0.
* For cards, when you select the icon for an inoput field, the corresponding field now correctly gets the focus state.

## 2025-03-19 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.10.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* You can now offer Apple Pay on third-party browsers. See [offering Apple Pay on the web](/payment-methods/apple-pay/web-drop-in/#offering-apple-pay-on-web) to learn more.

### Fixed

* For Drop-in, when using Google Pay as an instant payment type, the `challengeWindowSize` is now handled correctly.
* Fixed Brazilian social security number translation (CPF/CNPJ) for different languages.
* Fixed an issue where the exposed types for Google Pay and Apple Pay were not being found.

### Improved

* For single branded cards, the error message shown to when there configuration is incorrect is updated.
* Improved the contrast, `aria-expand` attribute for radio buttons, and SVG alt descriptions to follow [a11y](https://www.a11yproject.com/) guidelines.
* For Google Pay, an error is thrown when the configuration is missing the required merchant ID.

### Known Issues

* For Apple Pay in apps that use [server-side rendering](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Glossary/SSR): the iframe detection code crashes when rendering. Fixed in v6.11.0.

### Updating

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.10.0):

## 2025-02-28 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.9.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* Supported payment method: PayTo. [Payment method type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types/): **payto**.

### Improved

* When the shopper selects the **Pay** button, the Open Invoice and ACH Components become disabled.
* For Boleto: additional properties have been added to the `BoletoConfiguration` TypeScript interface.
* For cards: the `onAutoComplete` type has been added to the `CustomCardConfiguration` type.
* For Google Pay: the `challengeWindowSize` type has been added to the `GooglePayComponent` TypeScript interface.

### Fixed

* For Google Pay: when you call `reject` on the `onClick` event, an unhandled exception can no longer be triggered.
* For gift cards: when the shopper pays with a second gift card the UI no longer becomes unresponsive.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.9.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.9.0
```

## 2025-02-24 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.9.1

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Improved

* For the [address lookup feature](/payment-methods/cards/android-component/#address-lookup):

  * The input is now retained when changing countries.
  * The loading spinner moved to the search bar to prevent "jumping" of the view.
  * The keyboard is now hidden when:
  * An address is selected.
  * A search query is submitted
  * The manual entry button is selected.

### Changed

* For the address lookup feature, the address format is now two lines.

* For Google Pay, when a user cancels the flow, you now get `GooglePayCancellationException` in `onError`.

* The minimum supported [SDK versions](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration#android):

  | Name                | Version |
  | ------------------- | ------- |
  | Compile SDK version | 35      |
  | Target SDK version  | 35      |

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                 | Version       |
  | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------- |
  | [AndroidX Activity](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/activity#1.10.0)         | `1.10.0`      |
  | [AndroidX RecyclerView](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/recyclerview#1.4.0)  | `1.4.0`       |
  | [AndroidX Activity Compose](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/activity#1.10.0) | `` `1.10.0 `` |
  | [Android Gradle Plugin](https://developer.android.com/build/releases/gradle-plugin)                  | `8.8.0`       |

### Fixed

* For Twint with [Sessions flow](/online-payments/build-your-integration/#sessions-flow-a-single-api-request), setting `storePaymentMethodMode`: **enabled** now correctly stores the payment method.
* For the [address lookup feature](/payment-methods/cards/android-component/#address-lookup), when selecting a different address, it now correctly updates the input fields on the payment form.

## 2025-02-03 — Pay by Link

### New

* The payment page now contains a country selector. When a payment link does not contain country information, shoppers are now prompted to select their country.

## 2025-02-03 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.3.0

### New

* For custom card with API only integration, added validators for the following.

  * Card number.
  * Card expiry date.
  * Card security code.

* iOS Components/Drop-in version: [5.15.0](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%20Components%2FDrop-in#releaseNote=2025-01-07-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.15.0).

### Fixed

* For the Card Component, on Android 8 (API level 26) or earlier, the pay button no longer has ripple animations to prevent an animation crash.

## 2025-02-03 — Hosted Checkout

### New

* The payment page now contains a country selector. When a payment link does not contain country information, shoppers are now prompted to select their country.

## 2025-01-29 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.16.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2023-11-01-checkout-api-71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### Changed

* For [native 3D Secure 2](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/3d-secure/native-3ds2/?platform=iOS\&integration=Drop-in\&version=5.0.0%20and%20later), when a shopper cancels the payment during the payment flow, the `didProvide()` delegate method is now triggered. What this means for your integration depends on whether you already make a `/payments/details` call to handle 3D Secure 2 errors:

  * If yes, you do not need to make any changes to your integration.
  * If not, update your integration to make a `/payments/details` request to get the details of the canceled transaction.

### New

* Support to distinguish between the regular redirect flow where you redirect the shopper through the browser and native redirects to improve the handling of failed native redirects.

## 2025-01-27 — Pay by Link

### Changed

For iDEAL, the shopper now gets redirected to an iDEAL-hosted page to select their issuer and finish the payment.

## 2025-01-27 — Hosted Checkout

### Changed

* For iDEAL, the shopper now gets redirected to an iDEAL-hosted page to select their issuer and finish the payment.

## 2025-01-20 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.8.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

## 2025-01-17 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.9.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2023-11-01-checkout-api-71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### New

* Simplified integration for Google Pay:

  * The Component now includes the Google Pay button, and you no longer have to display the button with your own configuration.
  * The Component now does the availability check on initialization.
  * The deprecated `onActivityResult` is no longer needed.\
    See the [migration guide](https://docs.adyen.com/payment-methods/google-pay/android-component/migrate/) to learn about the changes you have to make to your integration to support Google Pay on this version.

* Drop-in now shows a toolbar on more intermediary screens to let shoppers navigate back easily.

### Changed

* For [native 3D Secure 2](/online-payments/3d-secure/native-3ds2/?platform=Android\&integration=Drop-in\&version=latest), when a shopper cancels the payment during the payment flow, the `onAdditionalDetails()` event is now triggered. What this means for your integration depends on whether you already make a `/payments/details` call to handle 3D Secure 2 errors:

  * If yes, you do not need to make any changes to your integration. You can remove the `Cancelled3DS2Exception` handler.
  * If not, update your integration to make a `/payments/details` request to get the details of the canceled transaction.

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                                                    | Version    |
  | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- |
  | [Android Gradle Plugin](https://developer.android.com/build/releases/past-releases/agp-8-7-0-release-notes#android-gradle-plugin-8.7.3) | 8.7.3      |
  | [AndroidX Compose BoM](https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/compose/bom/bom-mapping)                                                | 2024.12.01 |
  | [AndroidX ConstraintLayout](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/constraintlayout#constraintlayout-2.2.0)            | 2.2.0      |
  | [AndroidX Lifecycle](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/lifecycle#2.8.7)                                           | 2.8.7      |
  | [AndroidX Lifecycle ViewModel Compose](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/lifecycle#2.8.7)                         | 2.8.7      |
  | [KotlinX Coroutines Play Services](https://github.com/Kotlin/kotlinx.coroutines/releases/tag/1.9.0)                                     | 1.9.0      |

* We are changing the default branch of our SDK repository from `develop` to `main`. If you use our SDK repository and work with the `develop` branch, we recommend to switch to `main`. The `develop` branch will be removed within the next few weeks.

### Deprecated

* The styles and strings for the Cash App Pay loading indicator. use the new styles and strings instead.

  | Previous                                              | Now                                                              |
  | ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
  | `AdyenCheckout.CashAppPay.ProgressBar`                | `AdyenCheckout.ProcessingPaymentView.ProgressBar`                |
  | `AdyenCheckout.CashAppPay.WaitingDescriptionTextView` | `AdyenCheckout.ProcessingPaymentView.WaitingDescriptionTextView` |
  | `cash_app_pay_waiting_text`                           | `checkout_processing_payment`                                    |

## 2025-01-13 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.7.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* Added [tracking for API errors for internal endpoints](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-web/pull/3035) for analytics purposes.
* During the process of handling the payment response, Drop-in/Components no longer makes a duplicated internal API request.
* For the Custom Card Component, TypeScript types for configuration parameters are now more explicitly declared.

### Fixed

* For cards, the styling for the confirmation message to remove a stored card no longer causes responsiveness issues.
* For Click to Pay, button elements that appeared as links now appear as buttons.
* For Google Pay, when the value of the `GooglePayButton` attribute `buttonRadius` is **0**, it no longer fails to be passed to the `createButton` function.
* The Klarna Component now initializes the Klarna Widget SDK correctly. This fixes the known issue from v6.6.0.
* For Trustly, missing [translations](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-web/pull/3055) have been added.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.7.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.7.0
```

| Australia (AU) live                  | `https://checkoutshopper-live-au.cdn.adyen.com`   |
| Asia Pacific & Southeast (APSE) live | `https://checkoutshopper-live-apse.cdn.adyen.com` |
| India (IN) live                      | `https://checkoutshopper-live-in.cdn.adyen.com`   |

## 2025-01-07 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.15.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### New

* When using Drop-In: the `maxIssuerNumber` for Twint can now be set.

### Changed

* You can now highlight a list item by changing its background color when the shopper selects it. To do this, use the new optional `ListItemStyle.highlightedBackgroundColor` property. *Contribution by Github user.*

### Fixed

* For Drop-in, some components that are used in the same Drop-in session no longer have stale parts such as the following:

  * For gift cards, error messages.
  * For cards, brand logos.

* If your app uses more than one payment service provider SDK that uses the TwintSDK, a potential runtime crash no longer occurs.

## 2025-01-06 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.2.0

### New

* iOS Components/Drop-in version: [5.14.0](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%20Components%2FDrop-in#releaseNote=2024-12-03-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0).
* Android Components/Drop-in version: [5.8.0](/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2024-12-06-android-componentsdrop-in-5.8.0). Gradle v8 is now required.
* iDEAL is now available through the Instant Component.
* For the Google Pay Component on Advanced flow, added loading bottom sheet.
* The `paymentSessionFinished` payment result now contains `sessionResult`.
* You can now make partial payments with Drop-in also on the Advanced flow.

### Improved

* For the Card Component, improved the dynamic viewport.

### Removed

* Removed the alert message that appeared when deleting a stored payment method fails.

## 2024-12-10 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.6.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Known Issues

* The Klarna Component encounters an error when initializing the Klarna Widget SDK. Fixed in v6.7.0.

### New

* For the online banking payment methods in Slovakia and the Czech Republic, bank logo icons now appear in the list of banks.

### Improved

* For WeChat Pay QR codes, updated the text indicating the time left to pay for the `zh-CN` locale.
* For ACH Direct Debit, removed the text under the **Account holder name** field.
* When you set the **Country** and **State** fields to required in the payment form, the setting now correctly applies to the fields.
* For [donations](/online-payments/donations/web-component/), the nonprofit logo and description no longer cover the donation banner, and the banner now maintains it's aspect ratio when scaling according to the shopper's screen size.

### Fixed

* For cards, when the card is detected in the **Card number** field,

* When multiple variants of Klarna are available in the payment methods list, you can now make sure that the payment is made with the correct variant with the new `activate()` method.

  * For Drop-in: this happens automatically.
  * For Components: you must call `active()` when the shopper selects a variant of Klarna.

### Updating

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.6.0
```

## 2024-12-10 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.5.0

### New

* Drop-in now supports [partial payments](/online-payments/partial-payments/), including partial payments with gift cards and meal vouchers. You can also hide the PIN.

* Android Components/Drop-in version: [5.8.0](/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2024-12-06-android-componentsdrop-in-5.8.0), this version includes:

  * Support for Pay by Bank US.
  * Fix for the address lookup feature crash that happens when the shopper presses back when the postal code field is in focus.

* iOS Components/Drop-in version: [5.14.0](/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2024-12-03-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0), this version includes support for the following [payment method types](/payment-methods/payment-method-types/):

  * Boleto Bancario Itaú: **boletoBancarioItau**
  * PrimeiroPay Boleto: **primeiroPayBoleto**
  * Boleto Bancario: **boletoBancario**

### Fixed

* Corrected the TypeScript description and code auto-completion for `component` within the `onSubmit` callback.
* For the Sessions flow, the `sessionResult` for is now contains the full signature.
* The 3D Secure 2 configuration (`threeDS2`), now works correctly with code auto-completion.

## 2024-12-06 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.8.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2023-11-01-checkout-api-71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### New

* Payment method: Pay by Bank US. [Payment method type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types/): **paybybank\_AIS\_DD**.

### Changed

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                            | Version          |
  | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- |
  | [Android Gradle Plugin](https://developer.android.com/build/releases/gradle-plugin#android-gradle-plugin-8.7.1) | **8.7.1**        |
  | [AndroidX Fragment](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/fragment#1.8.5)                     | **1.8.5**        |
  | [AndroidX Activity](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/activity#1.9.3)                     | **1.9.3**        |
  | [AndroidX Autofill](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/autofill#1.3.0-beta01).             | **1.3.0-beta01** |
  | [AndroidX Annotation](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/annotation#1.9.1).                | **1.9.1**.       |

  | [AndroidX Lifecycle](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/lifecycle#2.8.6)                   | **2.8.6**        |
  | [AndroidX Lifecycle ViewModel Compose](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/lifecycle#2.8.6) | **2.8.6**        |

### Fixed

* For cards, the address lookup functionality no longer crashes if the shopper presses back when the postal code field is in focus.
* For Drop-in, fixed an issue where the error dialog showed loading state in some edge cases.

## 2024-12-03 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.14.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2023-11-01-checkout-api-71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* For Component integrations, you can now implement a custom pay button. Use the following:

  |                     | Description                                                         |
  | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
  | `showsSubmitButton` | Set to **false** to hide the default pay button. Default: **true**. |
  | `validate()`        | Validates the payment data.                                         |
  | `submit()`          | Submits the payment data.                                           |

* For Twint, you can now reduce the required number of `LSApplicationQueriesSchemes` values. In `TwintActionComponent.Configuration`, set the new `maxIssuerNumber` property.

* For Boleto Bancario, support for the following [payment method types](/payment-methods/payment-method-types):

  * Boleto Bancario Itaú: **boletoBancarioItau**
  * PrimeiroPay Boleto: **primeiroPayBoleto**
  * Boleto Bancario: **boletoBancario**

* For Pay by Bank in the US, support for stored payment methods.

### Changed

* For Pay by Bank payments in the US ([payment method type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types/):**paybybank\_AIS\_DD**): Drop-in now shows supported bank logos and a confirmation screen, before redirecting the shopper to the issuer.
* For Delegated Authentication: during Secure Checkout, you can now optionally reset credentials on the error screen.
* Twint SDK version: v8.0.2.

### Fixed

* For Titres-restaurant meal vouchers, the correct `type` and `brand` values are now included in the payment details.

## 2024-11-15 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.5.1

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Fixed

* For cards, when the `expiryDate` is only one digit, the `onBlur` validation now gets triggered.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

## 2024-11-14 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.7.1

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2023-11-01-checkout-api-71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### Fixed

* For the address lookup functionality:

  * Address data is now correctly saved to `PaymentComponentData`.
  * Address fields that were edited manually no longer lose their state when starting lookup mode.

## 2024-11-05 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.13.0

*\<p>We recommend that you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later. \</p>*

### New

* Support for tokenization in Twint payments, allowing shoppers to securely save their Twint payment details for future transactions.

### Improved

* For testing during development, the `UISwitch` testability is now enabled for external tools like Appium.
* For delegated authentication, payment information is consumed so that the UI shows the card number, brand logo, and payment amount.

### Changed

* CashAppPay SDK Version: 0.6.2

### Fixed

* For cards:

  * In the **Expiry date field**, when the shopper tries to enter additional characters to the field after having already entered characters, the field no longer ends up in an invalid state.
  * In the **Card number field**, the shopper can now move the cursor and edit the number where the cursor is positioned.

## 2024-11-04 — Online Payments

### Changed

* If you use Call Center for Mail Order/Telephone Order (MOTO) transactions: when you go to the Call Center URL, you are now redirected to the [Virtual Terminal](/unified-commerce/mail-order-telephone-order/) URL.

## 2024-10-31 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.5.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For PayMe, when the shopper pays, the UI shows instructions to complete the transaction.

  [![](/images/4/e/c/2/c/4ec2c3061da00b5817a9eeb2a0f7bab05139512d-paymeinstructionsscreen.png)](/user/pages/docs/02.online-payments/04.release-notes/2024-11-01-kzdu2/payme_instructions_screen.png)

* For PayNow, when the shopper pays on a tablet or mobile device, the UI now shows instructions to complete the transaction.

  [![](/images/5/9/7/c/d/597cd73f23b120516e8c992e36c75247304e9551-paynowinstructionsscreen.png)](/user/pages/docs/02.online-payments/04.release-notes/2024-11-01-kzdu2/paynow_instructions_screen.png)

### Fixed

* When creating a production build, the `auto.js` code is no longer tree-shaken by bundlers.
* For integrations using the [address lookup feature](/payment-methods/cards/web-component/#optional-configuration) for cards: the **State or province** field no longer resets when the shopper selects a value for the **Country** field.
* The [Iceland Krona currency (ISK)](/development-resources/currency-codes/) now correctly uses minor units.
* For UPI, the initial value for `isValid` is now **true** only for UPI QR.
* To support earlier iOS versions, the ECMAScript version is now the [2020 version](https://262.ecma-international.org/11.0/index.html).
* For Payconiq, the UI now includes instructions for the QR code, and an unused button label is now removed.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.5.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.5.0
```

## 2024-10-28 — Checkout API

### New

* The [/cardDetails](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/cardDetails) response now includes:

  * `fundingSource`: the funding source of the card, for example **DEBIT**, **CREDIT**, or **PREPAID**.
  * `isCardCommercial`: indicates if the card is a commercial card or a consumer card.

## 2024-10-22 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.71.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

* For ACH, the shopper can now select the **Savings** or **Checking** account type.

## 2024-10-22 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.4.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### New

* All [action types](/online-payments/build-your-integration/advanced-flow/?platform=Web\&integration=Components#additional-action) now trigger the `onActionHandled` callback.
  * For all action types, except for **threeds2**, the original `action` object is passed to the callback.
* For ACH, the shopper can now select the **Savings** or **Checking** account type.

### Improved

* For improved developer experience, we now export the `CustomTranslations` type.
* `document.querySelector` is now called only once, instead of three times.

### Fixed

* `get-process-message.handler.js` no longer causes exceptions. The new optional chaining operator for the `postMessage` handler function now prevents improperly formed events.
* When you call `actions.reject()` in the `beforeSubmit` callback, the UI no longer set the UI in an error state. Previously, it caused a call to `handleFailedResult`, which caused the `onPaymentFailed` callback to be called and set the UI in an error state.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.4.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.4.0
```

| Australia (AU) live                  | `https://checkoutshopper-live-au.cdn.adyen.com`   |

## 2024-10-14 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.7.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* You can now use [Adyen Test Cards Android](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-testcards-android) to autofill test payment method information, to test your integration more quickly.

* For Twint:

  * For Components integrations, you must now use [`TwintComponent`](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-android/blob/5.7.0/docs/payment-methods/TWINT.md#stored-twint-payments) instead of `InstantPaymentComponent`.
  * You can now [store payment details](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-android/blob/5.7.0/docs/payment-methods/TWINT.md#optional-configurations) and [pay with stored payment details](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-android/blob/5.7.0/docs/payment-methods/TWINT.md#stored-twint-payments).

* For French meal vouchers, the following payment method types are now available:

  * Up. Payment method type: **mealVoucher\_FR\_groupeup**.
  * Natixis. Payment method type: **mealVoucher\_FR\_natixis**.
  * Sodexo. Payment method type: **mealVoucher\_FR\_sodexo**.\
    Learn to [configure French meal vouchers](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-android/blob/5.7.0/docs/payment-methods/FRENCH_MEAL_VOUCHER.md).

* For [API-only integrations with encrypted card details](https://docs.adyen.com/payment-methods/cards/custom-card-integration/?tab=candroid_3), you can now use the following classes to validate corresponding fields:

  | Class                       | Description                        |
  | --------------------------- | ---------------------------------- |
  | `CardNumberValidator`       | Validates the card number field.   |
  | `CardExpiryDateValidator`   | Validates the expiry date field.   |
  | `CardSecurityCodeValidator` | Validates the security code field. |

* Support for the following locales:

  | Locale     | Values    |
  | ---------- | --------- |
  | Catalan    | **ca-ES** |
  | Icelandic  | **is-IS** |
  | Bulgarian  | **bg-BG** |
  | Estonian   | **et-EE** |
  | Latvian    | **lv-LV** |
  | Lithuanian | **lt-lT** |

### Improved

* For UPI Intent, if the shopper selects the **Continue** button without selecting an UPI option, an error message now shows.
* For Drop-in, in the navigation bar, the accessibility of the **Back/Close** button is improved.

### Changed

* For Drop-in, headers of preselected stored payment screen and payment methods list screen are updated.

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                                                    | Version        |
  | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------- |
  | [Adyen 3DS2](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.21)                                                           | **2.2.21**     |
  | [Cash App Pay](https://github.com/cashapp/cash-app-pay-android-sdk/releases/tag/v2.5.0)                                                 | **2.5.0**      |
  | [Android Gradle Plugin](https://developer.android.com/build/releases/past-releases/agp-8-5-0-release-notes#android-gradle-plugin-8.5.1) | **8.5.1**      |
  | [AndroidX Fragment](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/fragment#1.8.3)                                             | **1.8.3**      |
  | [AndroidX Activity](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/activity#1.9.2)                                             | **1.9.2**      |
  | [AndroidX Compose Activity](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/activity#1.9.2)                                     | **1.9.2**      |
  | [AndroidX Compose BOM](https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/compose/bom/bom-mapping)                                                | **2024.06.00** |
  | [AndroidX Lifecycle](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/lifecycle#2.8.3)                                           | **2.8.3**      |
  | [AndroidX Lifecycle ViewModel Compose](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/lifecycle#2.8.3)                         | **2.8.3**      |
  | [AndroidX AppCompat](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/appcompat#1.7.0)                                           | **1.7.0**      |

### Fixed

* When parsing JSON objects with explicit null values, JSON deserialization no longer returns the coerced `null` string.

### Deprecated

* The style for payment method list headers. Use the new style instead.

  | Previous                                   | Now                                        |
  | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------ |
  | `AdyenCheckout.TextAppearance.HeaderTitle` | `AdyenCheckout.TextAppearance.HeaderLabel` |

* The `com.adyen.checkout.instant.ActionHandlingMethod` method. Use the new method instead.

  | Previous                                          | Now                                                       |
  | ------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
  | `com.adyen.checkout.instant.ActionHandlingMethod` | `com.adyen.checkout.components.core.ActionHandlingMethod` |

## 2024-10-07 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.14.0

### New

* On iOS 18, if the shopper has two identical stored payment methods, Drop-in/Component no longer crashes when the shopper selects a payment method that's not the default stored one.

## 2024-10-07 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.1.0

### New

* For Drop-in, you can now display custom names for the payment methods in your checkout. Use the [`paymentMethodNames`](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow?platform=Flutter\&integration=Drop-in\&version=1.1.0#optional-configuration) object to set the name for each payment method.
* When a shopper cancels the payment during the 3D Secure 2 flow, the payment result is now **PaymentCancelledByUser**.

### Improved

* For Apple Pay, improved the instantiation of the payment method component.
* For Google Pay, improved configuration parsing.

## 2024-10-07 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.12.0

### Fixed

* On iOS 18, if the shopper has two identical stored payment methods, Drop-in/Component no longer crashes when the shopper selects a payment method that's not the default stored one.

### Improved

* You can now use the `AdyenEncryption` module independently with a reduced size, because it no longer depends on `AdyenCore`.

### New

* Support for [Library Evolution in Swift](https://www.swift.org/blog/library-evolution/).

### Changed

* When you set `isEnabled` = **false**, only [Drop-in/Components analytics](/online-payments/analytics-and-data-tracking#data-we-are-collecting) are not sent to Adyen.

## 2024-10-01 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.3.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* For Pay by Bank payments in the US, the payment form now shows US bank logos.
* For Click to Pay with Visa, custom timeouts now get reported to the Visa SDK.

* For Click to Pay with Visa, when the shopper selects **Enter**, the event no longer propagates to other views.

## 2024-10-01 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.4.1

### Improved

* Removed the unused `getReturnURL` declaration from the SessionHelper module to compile the library correctly when [React Native New Architecture](https://reactnative.dev/docs/the-new-architecture/landing-page) is enabled.

### Fixed

* Bumped [`serve-static`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/serve-static) version to 1.16.2 to address security vulnerability.

## 2024-09-23 — Pay by Link

### Changed

* Reverted the new iDeal payment flow that was released in [2 September, 2024](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Pay%20by%20Link#releaseNote=2024-09-02-pay-by-link).

## 2024-09-23 — Checkout API

### Fixed

* Fixed an issue where payments with the following `paymentMethod.type` would break in some cases:

  * **klarna**
  * **klarna\_account**
  * **klarna\_paynow**
  * **klarna\_b2b**
  * **walley**
  * **walley\_b2b**
  * **ebanking\_FI**

* When [blockedPaymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments#request-blockedPaymentMethods) is set to **scheme**, Bancontact is no longer blocked.

## 2024-09-16 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.69.0

### Improved

* For Drop-in, when loading, payment methods that are not supported are filtered out before the individual payment method Components are created.
* When loading, during the internal setup requests, the `browserInfo` information is included.

### Updating

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.69.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.69.0
```

```
     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

<link rel="stylesheet"
     href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.69.0/adyen.css"
     integrity="sha384-V9kYkdR/YBBSSAvtflZc9EhUrQuedsjqrh/cRgdF7qYElV+Ns39aj92Zz+qVu9aJ'"

```

### Changed

* When you set `analytics.enabled` = **false**, only [Drop-in/Components analytics](/online-payments/analytics-and-data-tracking#data-we-are-collecting) are not sent to Adyen.

## 2024-09-16 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.2.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Checkout%20API\&amp;version%5B0%5D=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend that you use the latest version of Checkout API.\</p>*

### Improved

* When loading, during the internal setup requests, the `browserInfo` information is included.
* For Drop-in, when loading, payment methods that are not supported are filtered out before the individual payment method Components are created.
* For Apple Pay, the `ApplePaySession` property is now optional in the [`Window` interface](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Window).

### Fixed

* For Apple Pay, `ApplePayService` no longer causes reliability issues with [SonarCloud](https://docs.sonarsource.com/sonarcloud/).
* Projects that use [webpack 4](https://v4.webpack.js.org/guides/getting-started/) can now directly import styles from the `adyen.css` file.
* For cards, the **Enter** key now correctly triggers validation and submission. Previously, a regression issue prevented the actions from being triggered.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/6.2.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.2.0
```

| Asia Pacific & Southeast (APSE) live | `https://checkoutshopper-live-apse.cdn.adyen.com` |
| India (IN) live                      | `https://checkoutshopper-live-in.cdn.adyen.com`   |

* When you set `analytics.enabled` = **false**, only [Drop-in/Components analytics](/online-payments/analytics-and-data-tracking#data-we-are-collecting) are not sent to Adyen.

## 2024-09-11 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.4.0

### New

* Support for [Apple Pay](/payment-methods) recurring payments.
* For Drop-in, you can now set `showRemovePaymentMethodButton` to **true** to allow your shoppers to remove their stored payment details.
* iOS Components/Drop-in version: [5.11.0](/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2024-09-09-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.11.0).

### Fixed

* `AdyenAction` is now exported correctly.
* Fixed an issue where `AdyenInstant` and other components were not loading correctly on React Native versions earlier than 73. This fixes the known issue in v2.3.0.

## 2024-09-11 — Flutter Components/Drop-in — Version 1.0.2

### New

* iOS Components/Drop-in version: [5.11.0](/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2024-09-09-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.11.0).

### Improved

* For Android, improved component bottom sheet behavior.
* For cards, improved the component for shopper devices that use large font and display sizes.
* Migrated the Android example to use Flutter Gradle plugins through the Plugin DSL.

## 2024-09-09 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.11.0

### Changed

* The following protocol and classes that were recently made internal `(@_spi(AdyenInternal))` are now public again:

  * `Validator`
  * `CardNumberValidator`
  * `CardExpiryDateValidator`
  * `CardSecurityCodeValidator`
  * `LengthValidator`
  * `NumericStringValidator`

* TWINT SDK version: 8.0.1

### Fixed

* The privacy manifest path in Package.swift now ensures correct Swift Package Manager (SPM) integration.

## 2024-08-07 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.10.0

### New

* Support for the UPI Intent flow, where the shopper can choose a UPI app to pay through. They are redirected to and complete the payment on the selected app.

* For Drop-in with Sessions flow, the shopper can now remove their stored payment methods. To do so:

  * In the `/sessions` request, include `showRemovePaymentMethodButton`: **true**.
  * Set the `StoredPaymentMethodsDelegate` to `session`:
    ```swift
    dropInComponent.StoredPaymentMethodDelegate = self.session
    ```

* Support for the following locales: `bg-BG`,`ca-ES`, `et-EE`,`is-IS`,`lt-LT`,`lv-LV`.

### Fixed

* For cards, when the shopper selects **Cancel** to exit the address input screen, the address fields no longer gets reset.

## 2024-07-24 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 6.0.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes#checkout-api-v69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later. We recommend using the latest Checkout API version.\</p>*

### Breaking Changes

**General**

* Drop-in/Components is no longer supported on Internet Explorer 11.
* If you integrate with npm, we've changed [how you import Drop-in/Components](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/#update-import-statements) to reduce the bundle size.
* If you integrate with embedded scripts, we [changed how we expose the `AdyenCheckout` and Drop-in/Components on the window object](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/?tab=dropin-step2_1#import-using-embedded-scripts).
* We've changed [how you create an instance](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/#update-creating-an-instance) of Drop-in or Component.
* For the `onSubmit()` event handler, you now need to [resolve/reject an `actions` object](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/#onsubmit-and-onadditionaldetails) to continue or halt the payment flow.
* For the `onAdditionalDetails()` event handler, you now need to [resolve/reject an `actions` object](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/#onsubmit-and-onadditionaldetails) to continue or halt the payment flow.
* The `onPaymentCompleted()` event is no longer triggered for failed payments, instead the [`onPaymentFailed()` event is triggered](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6#onpaymentfailed).
* The `showPayButton` parameter now defaults to true.
* The `onValid()` event is removed.
* The [`setStatusAutomatically` property](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow?platform=Web\&integration=Drop-in\&version=5.63.0#drop-in-configuration) is no longer supported. If a payment is successful or if it fails, the component is kept in loading state and merchant must handle it accordingly.
* We've changed how you style Drop-in/Components to give you more fine-grained control by using custom CSS properties. [Update your CSS styles](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6#update-your-styling) accordingly.
* The `countryCode` property is now mandatory. It must be set directly in the `AdyenCheckout` configuration object, or in the [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) request.
* The `showFormInstruction` property is no longer supported.
* When instantiating `AdyenCheckout`, you can no longer set the configuration property 'installmentOptions'. It must be set in the Card Component configuration instead.
* You can no longer set the configuration property `paymentMethodsConfiguration` when initializing `AdyenCheckout`. Instead, set it in the Drop-in configuration.
* The `locale` in your [global configuration object](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow/?platform=Web\&integration=Drop-in\&version=latest#configure) must follow the **xx-YY** format, where **xx** specifies the language, and **YY** specifies the region. Locales that are not formatted correctly will be ignored and fallback to **en-US**. See all [supported locales on our GitHub](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-web/tree/main/packages/server/translations).\
  Exception: For Arabic, the `locale` must be set to **ar**.
* TypeScript types are now imported directly from the root of the package, for example:
  ```js
  import type { CardConfiguration } from '@adyen/adyen-web';
  ```

**Payment method specific**

Express payment methods

* For [Google Pay express](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-component/express-checkout/), the event handler `onPaymentDataChanged` now works only when the Component configuration property `isExpress` is set to **true**.
* For [Apple Pay express](/payment-methods/apple-pay/web-component/express-checkout/), the event handlers `onShippingContactSelected` and `onShippingMethodSelected` now work only when the Component configuration property `isExpress` is set to **true**.
* For [Paypal Express](/payment-methods/paypal/web-component/express-checkout/), the `onShippingChange()` event handler is no longer supported. Migrate to the `onShippingAddressChange()` and `onShippingOptionsChange()` event handlers.
* The `onShopperDetails()` event for PayPal is renamed to `onAuthorized()` and it must be resolved or rejected to finalize the payment.
* For Google Pay, the `isAvailable()` method does not return a boolean flag anymore. Instead, the promise is resolved when the payment is available and rejected otherwise.

Card payment methods

* The Card Component configuration property `showBrandsUnderCardNumber` is no longer supported.
* The `challengeWindowSize` configuration parameter can now be set in the `card` object within `paymentMethodsConfiguration` instead of the `threeDS2` object.
* The Custom Card Component is renamed from `securedfields` to `CustomCard` and created as:
  ```js
  new CustomCard()
  ```

Other payment methods

* For PayPal, the `onCancel()` event handler is no longer supported. To detect when the PayPal lightbox is closed, listen to the `onError` event with error type **CANCEL**.
* For Klarna, the `token` property is replaced with the `authorization_token` from the [/payments/details](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/details) request.
* For Adyen Giving, you now need to integrate with the [Giving Campaign Manager Component](/online-payments/donations/campaign-manager-web-component/#get-details-of-donation-campaigns).
* We no longer support the payment method QiwiWallet.

**Partial payments**

* The `onOrderCreated()` event is [renamed to `onOrderUpdated()`](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6#onordercreated-and-onpaymentmethodsrequest).

### New

* [`onPaymentMethodsRequest()` is a new event](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6#onordercreated-and-onpaymentmethodsrequest), which is triggered when a partial payment is made and the associated order isn't fully paid. Use the even handler to request payment methods for a new payment.
* [`onPaymentFailed()` is a new event](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6#onpaymentfailed), which is triggered when a payment fails. Previously, this event was part of `onPaymentCompleted()`.
* You can now disable the final animation after a successful or failed payment in Drop-in, by setting `disableFinalAnimation` to **true**.
* Shoppers can now confirm the payment by pressing the **Enter** key. If the payment has a validation issue, an error is shown in the Drop-in/Components interface.
  * You can customize, or prevent, the Enter keypress behavior by implementing an `onEnterKeyPressed()` event handler on `AdyenCheckout`.
* Support for six more locales: Catalan (`ca-ES`), Icelandic (`is-IS`), Bulgarian (`bg-BG`), Estonian (`et-EE`), Latvian (`lv-LV`) and Lithuanian (`lt-lT`).
* Support for Apple Pay Order tracking [in Drop-in](/payment-methods/apple-pay/web-drop-in/#optional-apple-pay-wallet-order-tracking) and [in the Apple Pay Component](/payment-methods/apple-pay/web-component/#optional-apple-pay-wallet-order-tracking).
* Support for [tree shaking when integrating through `npm`](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6?tab=dropin-step2_1#npm-reduced-bundle-size).

### Changed

* [`onPaymentCompleted()` now works for](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6#onpaymentcompleted) both Sessions and Advanced flow.
* We have [replaced input field placeholder texts with permanently visible contextual elements](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/#update-placeholder-texts) to enhance the accessibility and UX. You *can* still apply and customize placeholders.
* For Google Pay and Apple Pay, the `onAuthorized` event is now triggered before the `onSubmit` event. The `onAuthorized` event must be resolved in order to proceed with the payment flow.
* We have improved AVS checks for GooglePay and ApplePay by adding the billing address to `state.data` if available during payment method authorization.
* Drop-in no longer uses radio buttons by default.
* The WeChatPay timer now defaults to 30 minutes.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Drop-in/Components Node.js package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web):

```bash
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@6.0.0 --save
```

### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

The base URLs for the script source are different for each environment. Use the one that corresponds to your [live endpoint](/development-resources/live-endpoints#checkout-endpoints):

| Europe (EU) live                     | `https://checkoutshopper-live.cdn.adyen.com`      |
| United States (US) live              | `https://checkoutshopper-live-us.cdn.adyen.com`   |
| Australia (AU) live                  | `https://checkoutshopper-live-au.cdn.adyen.com`   |
| Asia Pacific & Southeast (APSE) live | `https://checkoutshopper-live-apse.cdn.adyen.com` |
| India (IN) live                      | `https://checkoutshopper-live-in.cdn.adyen.com`   |

```bash
     integrity="sha384-GDA6txYXS6ka6zCgDtLmgI80TQMlYST32KjDtdiyFi/k/zp9rXP64qbziIXVOI5h"
     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>
```

## 2024-07-20 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.2.0

### Breaking Changes

* Starting August 31 2024, Google introduces [Target API level requirements for Google Play apps](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/11926878?hl=en). If you use:

  * React Native **v73** or later, you do not need to take any action.
  * React Native **v72**, you have to:
  * * In `android/build.gradle`, update `compileTarget` to **34**, and set `classpath("com.android.tools.build:gradle:8.1.4”)`.
  * * In `android/app/build.gradle`, add `android.buildFeatures.buildConfig` = **true** to enable `buildConfig`.
  * React Native **v71** or earlier:
  * * In `android/build.gradle`, update `compileTarget` to **34**.

### New

* Android Components/Drop-in version: [5.6.0](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Android%20Components%2FDrop-in\&version%5B0%5D=5.6.0#releaseNote=2024-07-09-android-componentsdrop-in-5.6.0).

### Fixed

* Updated the `gradle.build` to fix potential compatibility issues with old and new versions of React Native.

## 2024-06-28 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.1.0

### Fixed

* For Google Pay, [configurations related to the billing and shipping addresses](/payment-methods/google-pay/react-native-drop-in/#optional-configuration) now work correctly.
* On Android, the `Inconsistent JVM-target compatibility` build issue no longer occurs.

### New

* iOS Components/Drop-in version: [5.9.0](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=iOS%20Components%2FDrop-in\&version%5B0%5D=5.9.0#releaseNote=2024-05-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.9.0).
* Android Components/Drop-in version: [5.5.0](/online-payments/release-notes/?title%5B0%5D=Android%20Components%2FDrop-in\&version%5B0%5D=5.5.0).
* The new iDEAL payment flow where the shopper is redirected to the iDEAL payment page to select their bank and authorize the payment.

## 2024-06-21 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.5.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=71">Checkout API v71\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* Support for the UPI Intent flow, where the shopper can choose a UPI app to pay through. They are redirected to and complete the payment on the selected app.
* The new iDEAL payment flow where the shopper is redirected to the iDEAL payment page to select their bank and authorize the payment.

### Improved

* Autofill support for the following:

  * For gift cards, the gift card number and PIN fields.
  * For UPI Virtual Payments, the address field.
  * For payment methods that use them, the address input fields.

* Drop-in navigation:

  * Added the top navigation bar.
  * Disabled the dragging gesture that caused Drop-in to be dismissed.
  * When the shopper navigates from an additional action screen (for example 3D Secure), Drop-in is dismissed.

### Changed

Dependency versions:

| Name                                                                                                       | Version        |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------- |
| [AndroidX Compose BoM](https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/compose/bom/bom-mapping)                   | **2024.05.00** |
| [AndroidX Lifecycle](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/lifecycle#2.8.2)              | **2.8.2**      |
| [Material Design](https://github.com/material-components/material-components-android/releases/tag/1.12.0/) | **1.12.0**     |

### Deprecated

We recommend that you remove the following from your integration, because they no longer have an effect on the iDEAL payment method.

* For `IdealComponent`:

  * `isConfirmationRequired()`
  * `submit()`

* For iDEAL configuration:

  * `setViewType()`
  * `setHideIssuerLogos()`
  * `setSubmitButtonVisible()`

## 2024-06-17 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.0.1

### Fixed

* For [Expo](https://expo.dev/) v47 and later, version conflicts no longer occur.

## 2024-05-31 — React Native Components/Drop-in — Version 2.0.0

### New

* Drop-in now supports the [Sessions flow](/online-payments/build-your-integration/), where you make a single [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) request.
* You can now specify a locale instead of using the shopper device locale.
* Support for [Expo](https://expo.dev/).
* You can now use the `AdyenAction.handle()` method to handle additional actions.
* For Native 3D Secure 2, you can now configure the [`requestorAppUrl`](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-react-native/blob/develop/docs/Configuration.md#3d-security-2).
* For Drop-in on iOS, you can now [configure a custom title](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-react-native/blob/develop/docs/Configuration.md#drop-in) for the pre-selected stored payment method view. 

### Improved

* For [Apple Pay](/payment-methods/apple-pay), you can now configure `shippingContact`, `shippingType`, `shippingMethods`, and `supportedCountries`.
* The `AdyenCheckout` theme is now separated from the `AppTheme`, which makes it easier to integrate with Drop-in.

### Changed

* For Android, you no longer need to have `<service android:name="com.adyenreactnativesdk.component.dropin.AdyenCheckoutService" android:exported="false" />` in your app's manifest file.

### Deprecated

* For Android, `adyenReactNativeRedirectScheme` is deprecated, you can use any [intent filter](https://developer.android.com/guide/components/intents-filters). When it is required, you get a `returnUrl` in `onSubmit.data`. To get the `returnUrl`, you can also use the `await AdyenDropIn.getReturnURL()` function.

### Breaking Changes

* For Android, your app theme must have [Material Components](https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/views/theming/look-and-feel) as `parent` to work with payment methods that use the **InstantPaymentComponent**, for example:
  ```
  <style name="AppTheme" parent="Theme.MaterialComponents.DayNight.NoActionBar"> 
  ```

## 2024-05-30 — Pay by Link

### Improved

* For payment links with only one payment method that does not require additional shopper input, the shopper no longer needs to select an additional button to pay.

## 2024-05-30 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 5.4.0

### New

* For external redirects, you can now [customize the colors of the toolbar and navigation bar](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-android/blob/main/docs/UI_CUSTOMIZATION.md#styling-custom-tabs) shown in [Custom Tabs](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/android/custom-tabs).

* TWINT is now supported with a native flow, and you no longer need to redirect shoppers through the browser. To use the redirect flow, set the following configuration:

  ```kotlin
  CheckoutConfiguration(
      environment = environment,
      clientKey = clientKey,
      ..
  ) {
      // Optionally pass the payment method type to configure it for the specific payment method.
     instantPayment(PaymentMethodTypes.TWINT) {
         setActionHandlingMethod(ActionHandlingMethod.PREFER_WEB)
     }
  }
  ```

### Fixed

* Fixed some memory leaks.
* In case of a debug build, Drop-in no longer overrides the log level.
* For cards, when a shopper does not select an address, the address lookup function now shows a validation error.
* Actions no longer crash when your app uses obfuscation.
* When handling a 3D Secure 2 challenge using Checkout API v66 or earlier, Drop-in no longer throws an error.
* If the app process unexpectedly terminates when handling actions, the state is now restored and you can proceed with the payment flow.
* For [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions), fixed an issue where the `setEnableRemovingStoredPaymentMethods` flag in the [Drop-in configuration](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow/?platform=Android\&integration=Drop-in\&version=5.4.0#3-optional-add-a-configuration-object) was ignored.

### Changed

* The phone number input field in the payment form now shows ISO codes instead of flags.

* The UI elements that were previously labelled **Country** are now **Country/Region**.

* Dependency versions:

  | Name                                                                                                         | Version        |
  | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------- |
  | [Adyen 3DS2](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.18)                                | **2.2.18**     |
  | [Android Gradle plugin](https://developer.android.com/build/releases/gradle-plugin)                          | **8.3.2**      |
  | [AndroidX Browser](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/browser#1.8.0)                    | **1.8.0**      |
  | [AndroidX Compose Activity](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/activity#1.9.0)          | **1.9.0**      |
  | [AndroidX Compose BoM](https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/compose/bom/bom-mapping)                     | **2024.04.01** |
