The new [`onActionHandled`](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow?platform=Web\&integration=Components#optional-configuration) callback informs you when an action, for example a QR code or 3D Secure authentication screen, is shown to the shopper. It returns data that contains the type of component that shows the action to the shopper and a description of the action.

## 2023-01-30 — Checkout API — Version 70

Follow the [migration guide](/online-payments/migrate-to-checkout-api-v70) to upgrade your integration.

### Breaking Changes

* For the [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) or [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) request to [store payment details](/online-payments/tokenization) or make a payment with stored payment details:\
  You now must include the [recurringProcessingModel](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/70/post/payments#request-recurringProcessingModel) parameter.

* For the [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) request to [store payment details](/payment-methods/cards/web-drop-in#create-a-token):\
  You now must include the [recurringProcessingModel](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/70/post/payments#request-recurringProcessingModel) parameter.

* For the [/paymentMethods/balance](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods/balance) request to check a gift card balance:\
  You now must include the [`amount`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/70/post/paymentMethods/balance#request-amount) parameter.

* For [Pix](/payment-methods/pix), the [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) response no longer includes `action.url`. You must [use `action.qrCodeData` to render the QR code](/payment-methods/pix/api-only#present-qr-code).

### Changed

* You must now make a [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) or [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) request to store payment details. You can no longer configure it from the Customer Area.

* For the [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) request:

  * The [storePaymentMethod](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions#request-storePaymentMethod) parameter. Use `storePaymentMethodMode` instead.
  * If the required [reference](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/70/post/sessions#request-reference) parameter isn't included, you now get a validation error.

* For [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) and [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) requests including line items for custom risk rules:\
  You can now [use the `lineItems` parameter instead of `additionalData.riskdata.basket`](/online-payments/migrate-to-checkout-api-v70#include-line-items-for-risk-rules).

### New

* The `/storedPaymentMethods` endpoint:

  * Make a **GET** request to list the stored payment details for a shopper, if there are any available.
  * Make a **DELETE** request to disable stored payment details to stop charging a shopper with the particular recurring detail ID.

* For [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) and `/paymentLinks` requests:\
  You can now include the `storePaymentMethodMode` parameter to set when to store the shopper's payment details.

* For the [/paymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods) request:\
  When you include the `shopperReference` parameter, stored payment details in the response now contain the `supportedRecurringProcessingModel` parameter.

* For specific industries, such as hospitality, you can specify a reason when making a merchant-initiated transaction with stored payment details. In the [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) request, include the `industryUsage` parameter to specify a reason for the payment. Possible values:

  * **delayedCharge**.
  * **noShow**.
  * **installment**.

### Removed

In the Customer Area, on the **Payments** > **Checkout settings** page:

* Turning on the **enableRecurring** toggle no longer adds the required parameters to store payment details in payment requests.
* Turning on the **enablePayout** toggle no longer adds the required parameters to store payment details for payouts.

### Fixed

* For [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) and [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) requests to make payments with stored payment details:\
  When you include `shopperInteraction`: **ContAuth** and `recurringProcessingModel`: **CardOnFile**, the transactions now always get processed as **CardOnFile** payments.

* If you contacted our [Support Team](https://ca-test.adyen.com/ca/ca/contactUs/support.shtml?form=other) to enable filtering payment methods based on your store, when making a [/paymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods) or [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) request including [store](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/70/post/paymentMethods#request-store):\
  The response now includes only payment methods available for the specified store.

* For [/paymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods) and [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) requests including [allowedPaymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/70/post/paymentMethods#request-allowedPaymentMethods) with **scheme** and **card** in the array:\
  The response no longer includes wallet payment methods.

## 2023-01-30 — Pay by Link

### Breaking Changes

For the [/paymentLinks](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/70/post/paymentLinks) request to [create a payment link that stores the shopper's payment details](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/api#tokenize-your-shoppers-payment-details):\
You must include the [recurringProcessingModel](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/70/post/payments#request-recurringProcessingModel) parameter.

### New

When creating a payment link in the Customer Area, you can enable storing the shopper's payment details.\
In your Customer Area, go to **Payments** > **Payment link settings** and select the toggle to enable.

* Apple Pay now shows the full list of networks supported by the shopper's iOS device.

## 2023-01-03 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.31.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* For integrations using the [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) endpoint, you now receive the `sessionResult` property after the payment flow finishes.
* For Google Pay, the [`buttonRootNode` property](https://developers.google.com/pay/api/web/reference/request-objects#ButtonOptions) is now supported.
* For the Card Component on iOS devices in the Safari browser, when an input field is in focus, you can now enable the keyboard arrows for all versions of the browser by setting the new `disableIOSArrowKeys` to **false**.

### Improved

* The sorting logic after detecting card brands now happens on Adyen's backend servers.

* For ACH:

  * The bank account number input field in the payment for no longer shows a placeholder.
  * When the shopper enters a bank account number, it is no longer formatted with a space between every four digits.

* For the cards, when the `billingAddressMode` property is set to **partial** and the `country` property is set, there's now validation for the postal code that the shopper enters.

### Fixed

* For OXXO, the payment button no longer shows when `showPaymentButton` is set to **false**.
* For payment methods with an additional action, configured properties for the payment method now persist after the handling the action. Previously, configured properties were lost after handling the action.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.31.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.31.0 --save
```

### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

```
<script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.31.0/adyen.js"
     integrity="sha384-EUmEyEC7yl7Vx3zywNlWNZUAmfObzuL5ucB3XwL8pqqHRiBKmAFaNgKtx+eQUq1M"
     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

<link rel="stylesheet"
     href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.31.0/adyen.css"
     integrity="sha384-2eA2bcYBLr7anAJ65YYoCathDWR2D6+44dnv/woRgdzi8R4rphr6zJVj+ZJKZSBV"
     crossorigin="anonymous">
```

### Known Issues

For PayNow, the QR code expires after 15 minutes. It should expire after 3 minutes. This issue is fixed in v5.31.3.

## 2022-12-23 — Checkout API

### New: general

We now accept payments made with Ukrainian cards that have been expired for up to three months, in line with the National Bank of Ukraine's recommendations.

### Improved: general

Shopper-facing error pages now display the HTTP error code when applicable and include an updated graphic.

## 2022-12-13 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.9.1

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Fixed

* For cards, when the shopper enters a card number and a dual branded card is detected, a brand is no longer selected by default.
* Drop-in no longer crashes when navigating back after the shopper removes all stored payment methods.

## 2022-12-12 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.30.1

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Fixed

* For cards, the `onBinLookup` event now includes the `issuingCountryCode` property. Previously, the event didn't include information about the issuing country code.

For PayNow, the QR code expires after 15 minutes. It should expire after 3 minutes. This issue is fixed in v5.31.3.

## 2022-12-05 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.30.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* Payment method: DuitNow.

* For cards:

  * The **Expiry date** field now includes an icon showing the location of the expiry date.
  * In the **CVC/CVV** field, you can now set digits to appear as dot icons by setting the new `maskSecurityCode` property to **true**.

### Changed

* To [enable the instant payment button for Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-drop-in#instant-payment-button-configuration), you can now set `instantPaymentTypes` to either **googlepay** or **paywithgoogle**.
* The `checkoutAttemptId` field for [analytics and data tracking](/online-payments/analytics-and-data-tracking) is now in the `paymentMethod` object.

### Improved

In the payment methods list, the items in the list now have the **radio** role for accessibility features.

### Fixed

* For Pix, when `showPayButton` is set to **false**, the payment form now appears when the shopper selects Pix from the payment methods list.
* For Klarna using the `/sessions` endpoint, you no longer have to implement the `onAdditionaDetails` callback. Previously, you had to implement the `onAdditionalDetails` callback to make a request to the correct endpoint.
* When you create the 3D Secure 2 Component with `createFromAction`, the component no longer crashes if you make more than one transaction of the same type.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.30.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.30.0 --save
```

```
     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

<link rel="stylesheet"
     href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.30.0/adyen.css"
     integrity="sha384-wNJf9kOfnzg6TcZUN6ysrsTbnTJtuwoDIgAWPr/uRy83KzYIQaf1zzr3MsbCdeUE"
     crossorigin="anonymous">
```

### Known Issues

For PayNow, the QR code expires after 15 minutes. It should expire after 3 minutes. This issue is fixed in v5.31.3.

## 2022-11-28 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.10.2

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### Fixed

* When the shopper submits a payment, the billing address that the shopper entered is now submitted. Previously, the submitted value was **nil** if:

  * there was no input in the **State/Province** field.
  * there was no input in the **Apartment/Suite** field.

* The Drop-in/Component now detects when a shopper enters an invalid email address in the payment form. Previously, sometimes the Drop-in/Component detected invalid email addresses as valid.

* When the shopper enters a card number in the payment form, and a dual branded card is detected, we no longer automatically select a brand.

## 2022-11-16 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.9.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* For cards, in `AddressConfiguration.PostalCode` mode, you can now [specify if the postal code field required](/payment-methods/cards/android-component#address-field-configuration).
* For BCMC, you can now [specify if the cardholder name field is required](/payment-methods/bancontact/bancontact-mobile/android-component#optional-configuration).
* After the card brand is detected and the shopper enters the full card number in the card number input field, focus automatically moves to the next input field.

### Improved

* Upgraded the 3D Secure 2 SDK version to [v2.2.10](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.10). This causes a [known issue](#known-issue-4-9-0).
* For a card number to be valid, its minimum required length is now 12 digits. Previously, the minimum was 8 digits.

### Fixed

* Configuration changes no longer dismiss Drop-in. Previously, some configuration changes dismissed Drop-in.
* Drop-in can now be initialized with only stored payment methods. Previously, no payment methods were shown if only stored payment methods were available.

### Changed

For cards, if you currently use `AddressConfiguration.PostalCode` mode, you must update it for **4.9.0**:

| 4.8.0 or earlier                                            | 4.9.0                                                         |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `.setAddressConfiguration(AddressConfiguration.PostalCode)` | `.setAddressConfiguration(AddressConfiguration.PostalCode())` |

### Known Issues

[]()\
If your project uses [Google `play-services-location` library](https://play.google.com/sdks/details/com-google-android-gms-play-services-location) version 20.0.0 or earlier, it causes a runtime exception because of an incompatibility with the 3D Secure 2 SDK. You must upgrade the library to version 21.0.0 or later to avoid the runtime exception.

## 2022-11-14 — Pay by Link

### New

When creating a payment link through the Customer Area, you can now specify if you want to manually capture the payment.

## 2022-11-09 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.29.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* Payment methods:

  * PayNow
  * PromptPay

### Fixed

* For PayPal, GooglePay, and AmazonPay, payments no longer crash because of `Script`.
* When a shopper makes gift card partial payments, and the second payment method fails, the corresponding callback gets triggered with an error. Previously, the callback was not triggered.

## 2022-11-01 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 5.0.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes#checkout-api-v69">Checkout API v69\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v5.4.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=5.4.0) or later for compatibility.

### New

* Using the [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) endpoint, you can integrate Drop-in and Components with a single API call.\
  If you already integrated using the [/paymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods), [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments), and [/payments/details](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/details) endpoints, you can continue using this back end setup in version 5.0.0.

* When using the [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions) endpoint, the new `AdyenSession` object handles all actions after the payment session is created.

* For Drop-in you can now customize the payment method title or subtitle in the payment methods list. Use the new function `overrideDisplayInformation(ofPaymentMethod:with:)` in `PaymentMethods`.

* `APIContext.init(environment: AnyAPIEnvironment, clientKey: String)` now throws exception if the client key is invalid.

* Analytics feature turned on by default. Find out [what we track and how you can configure it](/online-payments/analytics-and-data-tracking).\
  `finalBIN` that provides the BIN when the shopper selects the **Pay** button.

* The `PaymentMethodType` enum includes supported payment methods. This replaces using strings for payment method types.

* For Drop-in, you can now configure the Boleto payment method using the `boleto` property.

* Payment methods:

  * Atome.
  * Online banking Poland.
  * Online banking Czech Republic.

* For ACH Direct Debit payments, the shopper can now save their payment details.

* For cards:

  * `CardComponentDelegate.didChangeBIN(:component:)` now provides the 8-digit BIN when the card number is more than 16 digits.
  * `CardComponentDelegate.didSubmit(lastFour:finalBIN:component)` now has the parameter

* The Apple Pay Component now supports shipping options.

* Support for [DocC documentation](https://adyen.github.io/adyen-ios/5.0.0/documentation/adyen/).

* Support for delegated authentication.

### Fixed

* You can now configure the 3D Secure 2 Component with `threeDSRequestorAppURL`.

### Changed

The `Observer` is protocal has been renamed to `AdyenObserver`.

### Breaking Changes

Follow the steps in the [migration guide](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-ios-5-0-0) if you are upgrading your integration from an earlier version.

## 2022-10-31 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.28.3

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Changed

Drop-in and Components now use [Preact v10.11.2](https://github.com/preactjs/preact/releases/tag/10.11.2).

### Fixed

The Amazon Pay payment method now loads for the US locale when the shopper is in the US. Previously it loaded the Great Britain locale.

### Deprecated

For Amazon Pay, the default property `region`. Use `configuration.region` instead.

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes#checkout-api-v67">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### Fixed

* The `ViewControllerPresenter` SwiftUI helper is now compatible with iOS 16.
* For the stored cards, shoppers can now only enter 4 digits in the CVC input field if the card brand is American Express. Previously 4 digits were accepted for other card brands also.
* The Google Pay payment method is now blocked and is no longer shown.

## 2022-10-24 — Pay by Link

When creating payment links through the Customer Area, it's now possible to add installment options when the shopper country/region is Colombia, Peru, or Ecuador.

## 2022-10-19 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.10.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes#checkout-api-v67">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### New

For 3D Secure 2.2.0, the `threeDSRequestorAppURL` parameter is automatically populated with the `returnUrl` value from the `/payments` request.

### Removed

The `URL` extension. It caused conflict with the code in some integrations.

### Changed

The properties in `RedirectDetails` are now public.

### Fixed

A successful QR code payment now always triggers a callback.

## 2022-10-03 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.27.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

Payment method: [Open banking](/payment-methods/open-banking)

### Improved

The `mount` function can now remount a Component after it has been unmounted. Previously, only `remount` could remount a Component.

### Fixed

* For Google Pay, payment submission errors now trigger the `onError` callback instead of throwing an exception.
* On mobile, redirecting the shopper to another app no longer triggers the `onError` callback with an `AdyenCheckoutError`.
* For UPI, changing the payment mode to **QR code** now triggers the `onChange` callback. Previously, only changing the payment mode to **VPA** triggered the callback.

### Updating

## 2022-09-27 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.8.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=67">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

Payment method: Online banking Poland

### Improved

Updated the 3D Secure SDK to [v2.2.8](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.8).

### Fixed

* Drop-in now terminates without crashing when the shopper is redirected back to your app.
* The 3D Secure 2 SDK no longer crashes on Android 13.

## 2022-09-26 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.26.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Fixed

* Fixed the known issue from [v5.25.0](/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2022-09-19-web-componentsdrop-in-5.25.0) where the Stored Card Component UI showed an error code instead of the error message when there was a validation error.
* On Drop-in, moving the focus on a payment method no longer automatically expands it. To expand a payment method, the user must slect by clicking, tapping, or pressing a button.
* Added missing alt text translations for icons used for input field validation feedback.

### New

* The `checkoutAttemptId` is now stored as part of your browser's `sessionStorage` for 15 min.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.26.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.26.0 --save
```

### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

## 2022-09-19 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.25.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Improved

For the Card Component, input field errors now include the name of the field where it occurred.

### Changed

The UPI Component now shows a selector for **Virtual Payment Address** and **QR code** above the input field, instead of a different button for each, so that you can customize the **Continue** button. Use the new `defaultMode` configuration parameter to specify if **Virtual Payment Address** or **QR code** is selected by default.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.25.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.25.0 --save
```

### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

```

     integrity="sha384-dr5oyw53MXiRb8jiuNS5357DFMBKLYFNJ8uMgatbtu18R16CXdTz7jx9IJDZolyO"
     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

<link rel="stylesheet"
     href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.25.0/adyen.css"
     integrity="sha384-EWxYZbuFOr+TBHe/ugu0v3NOulSLFDx8Diy1Mb2WJk1TNzTJJHAuwiwW3gq6btNx"
     crossorigin="anonymous">
```

### Fixed

For input fields, the icons that indicate if an input is valid or invalid now include corresponding `alt` text, which is used by screen readers.

### New

Payment method: [Online banking Poland](/payment-methods/online-banking-poland).

### Known Issues

If there's a validation error in the Stored Card Component, the UI shows the error code instead of the error message. Fixed in [v5.26.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=5.26.0)

## 2022-09-19 — Checkout API

### Fixed

When you [enable receiving the `fraudResult` parameter](/risk-management/handle-risk-rule-notifications#enable-risk-results-in-the-api-response-and-webhooks) in the [/payments/details](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/details) response, you now receive it for 3D Secure 2 transactions. Previously, `fraudResult` wasn't returned for 3D Secure 2 transactions.

## 2022-09-13 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.24.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

For ACH Direct Debit payments, the shopper can now choose to save their payment details. Set `enableStoreDetails` to **true** in the payment method configuration to enable the checkbox.

### Fixed

When the shopper selects a checkbox, the check mark now renders completely. Previously, only a part of the check mark rendered.

## 2022-09-13 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.9.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=70">Checkout API v70\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### Deprecated

The `finalizeIfNeeded(with: Bool)` method. Use `finalizeIfNeeded(with success: Bool, completion: (() -> Void)?)` instead.

### New

Successful Apple Pay payments are now finalized with `finalizeIfNeeded(with success: Bool, completion: (() -> Void)?)`, which increases conversion rates.

## 2022-08-23 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.23.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

* For cards, the cardholder name field now has an autocomplete attribute that labels its purpose.

## 2022-08-08 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.21.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* For the Gift cards Component, you can now show a custom button when the shopper chooses to pay part of the amount with a gift card and the remaining amount with another payment method.
* Support for [credit card installments](/payment-methods/cards/credit-card-installments).

### Fixed

* If you do not support American Express cards, the shopper can no longer enter a CVC that is more than 3 digits.
* The UPI Component now handles the timeout error for UPI Collect payments.
* For the SEPA bank transfers, setting the `showPayButton` to **false** now hides only the payment button.

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

Supported language: Portuguese. Use the `shopperLocale`: **pt-PT**.

### Improved

For the Card Component, the input fields in the payment form now use [JSON Web Encryption](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7516) for security. To [test your Card integration](/payment-methods/cards/web-component#test-and-go-live), you now must use either a local or `https` domain.

For Bancontact card payments, when the shopper enters the card number and the brands are detected by the Component, the brands no longer remain detected if you remove the card number.

## 2022-07-06 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.8.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=67">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### Improved

* For the Card Component, use the new `billingAddressRequirementPolicy` parameter in the configuration object to specify card brands for which the billing address field is optional.

### Fixed

* For Drop-in, when you make a partial payment with a gift card and select another payment method, the payment button now shows the remaining amount.
* For Drop-in, you can now use Apple Pay to complete a partial gift card payment.
* For the Apple Pay Component, the `PaymentData.amount` passed from the SDK to the `didSubmit` callback is no longer nil. This fixes the issue introduced in [v4.0.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.0.0).

### Deprecated

The URL extension. This shouldn't affect your integration because this is an internal code change.

### Removed

In the `RegionRepository` class, the functions `getSubRegions` and `getCountries` are removed. This shouldn't affect your integration because this is an internal code change.

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

Add your signed JSON Web Token (JWT) to your Google Pay configuration using [`configuration.authJwt`](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-component#merchant-info). This allows you to use your Google merchant ID with multiple domains.

## 2022-06-13 — Checkout API

### New

Added support for partial authorization. This allows shoppers to pay for their goods with another card, if the balance of their initial card is less than the value of the goods.

### Fixed

Fixed issue where the `/payments` and `/payments/details` responses incorrectly showed BCMC cards as Mastercard or Visa. The payments were correctly handled as BCMC, but the reponses had the incorrect `paymentMethod.brand`.

## 2022-06-13 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.16.2

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Fixed

* If an action is rejected in the `beforeSubmit` callback, the state of the Component no longer remains `loading`.
* For donations, amounts are now shown with decimals. Previously, amounts were rounded up to the closest integer.
* The 3D Secure 2 Component now throws a challenge error when [`transStatus`](/online-payments/3d-secure/api-reference#threeds2result) cannot be retrieved.
* Fixed an issue where in some situations, if a shopper corrected an invalid card number, it was still marked as invalid.

## 2022-05-24 — Checkout API — Version 69

This API version is supported by our Java, NodeJS, PHP, Python, Go, and .NET libraries. We are working on updating the Ruby library.\
You can find all the [Adyen libraries on GitHub](https://github.com/Adyen/).

### Breaking Changes

**POST** `/payments`

* The `fraudResult.results` list now no longer contains the `FraudCheckResult` for each list item. Each list item now only contains the values of the individual risk checks. See [code samples](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/upgrade-to-checkout-api-v69#removed-fraudcheckresult).

**POST** `/paymentMethods`

* The format of the [`expiryYear`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/69/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-storedPaymentMethods-expiryYear) returned for stored cards is now the last two digits of the year.

### New

**POST** `/sessions`

* [authenticationData](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/69/post/sessions#request-authenticationData) object in the request.

**POST** `/payments`

* [authenticationData](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/69/post/payments#request-authenticationData) object in the request.
* [paymentMethod](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/69/post/payments#responses-200-paymentMethod) object in the reponse.

**POST** `/payments/details`

* [authenticationData](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/69/post/payments/details#request-authenticationData) object in the request.
* [paymentMethod](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/69/post/payments/details#responses-200-paymentMethod) object in the reponse.

### Deprecated

Fields related to 3D Secure authentication are now grouped under the new `authenticationData` object. The old 3D Secure authentication fields have been deprecated.

See [field mapping and code samples](/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/upgrade-to-checkout-api-v69#3d-secure-authentication-fields).

## 2022-05-23 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.16.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

Analytics feature turned on by default. Find out [what we track and how you can configure it](/online-payments/analytics-and-data-tracking).

For [MB WAY](/payment-methods/mb-way/web-component), added a country code prefix to the phone number input field.

For [wallets India](/payment-methods/wallets-india), the payment method type is now: **wallet\_IN**.

## 2022-05-11 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.6.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=67">Checkout API v67\</a>.\</p>*

### New

* Use `CardConfiguration.Builder.setAddressConfiguration(AddressConfiguration)` to enable and configure the billing address form in your Card Component.

### Deprecated

* `Environment.LIVE`. Use the name of your live environments instead. For example, use `Environment.EUROPE` if your live environment is `https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com`. You can find that value in your [live Customer Area](https://ca-live.adyen.com/) > **Developers** > **API URLs**.
* `CardConfiguration.Builder.setAddressVisibility(AddressVisibility)`. Use `CardConfiguration.Builder.setAddressConfiguration(AddressConfiguration)` instead.

### Fixed

* Fixed issue with the soft keyboard navigation not working in some cases for the Card Component.
* All fields now use the locale in your configuration. The device locale is being ignored.
* Google Pay no longer crashes when the Coponent or the payment method list is initialized.
* For `ModelObject` classes, there are no more issues related to obfuscation .
* Fixed an issue where the remove stored payment method button would disappear.
* Fixed an issue where removed stored payment methods would appear again in the UI after rotating the screen, even though they had been removed.

## 2022-05-09 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.15.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

For gift cards on Drop-in, the shopper can now cancel a transaction after making a partial payment with a gift card.

### Fixed

* For the Card Component on iOS devices in the Safari browser, when an input field is in focus, the keyboard arrows are now disabled for all versions of the browser. Previously, the keyboard arrows only functioned correctly for some versions.
* Integrations using TypeScript now compile without an error. This fixes the known issue introduced in [v5.14.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=5.14.0).

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* Payment method: [Atome](/payment-methods/atome).

## 2022-03-14 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.11.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

For cards, supported brands are no longer shown above the card number field when the shopper selects to pay by card. You can now show them under the card number field by setting the property `showBrandsUnderCardNumber` to **true**. In the Drop-in payment method list, next to the Card payment method, this also shows a maximum of 4 supported brands instead of all.

Starting from [v5.12.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=5.12.0), the default value for this property is **true**.

### Fixed

Drop-in and Components no longer crash for some [Content Security Policy](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Headers/Content-Security-Policy) header values.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.11.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.11.0 --save
```

```
     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

     href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.11.0/adyen.css"
     integrity="sha384-JNgZxvSbTomiYdB3jwg1MoRvg+3YBa/ihE38uDKCeKBK8Srg5DvJY9mgQvUFqJYn"
     crossorigin="anonymous">
```

## 2022-03-08 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.5.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=67">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

* Payment method: gift cards.

* New required methods for the gift card flow:

  * `checkBalance`: calls `/paymentMethods/balance` and `sendBalanceResult` to return the result.
  * `createOrder`: calls `/orders` and `sendOrderResult` to return the result.
  * `cancelOrder`: calls `/orders/cancel`. The result is returned in `sendResult`.

* Support for right-to-left layout.

* Added translations for Arabic (international).

* You can pass a custom `Bundle` to `DropInService` using `DropInConfiguration.Builder.setAdditionalDataForDropInService`. Retrieve this bundle by calling `DropInService.getAdditionalData`.

* The Google Pay environment now also follows the Adyen environment when separately calling `GooglePayConfiguration.Builder.setEnvironment`. You can call `GooglePayConfiguration.Builder.setGooglePayEnvironment` to override this default behaviour.

* On Drop-in, you can now allow shoppers to remove stored cards. To do this, override `DropInService.removeStoredPaymentMethod` and use `sendRecurringResult` to return the result.

### Improved

* Drop-in is no longer dismissed when tapping outside of the bottom sheet.
* Upgraded the 3D Secure 2 SDK version to [v2.2.6](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.6).

### Fixed

* Fixed [handling redirects using Custom Tabs](https://developers.google.com/web/updates/2020/07/custom-tabs-android-11) as required by the [privacy changes for package visibility in Android 11](https://developer.android.com/about/versions/11/privacy/package-visibility).
* Drop-in no longer crashes when the shopper locale doesn't have an ISO3Country.
* When resuming Drop-in after the activity has been destroyed in the background, the Component screen no longer crashes.
* In the Card Component, CVC and expiry date fields are no longer being validated when empty.
* Added missing translation strings.

## 2022-03-07 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.10.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Improved

* If the environment and type of client key do not match, you now get an error.
* The error message for when a 3D Secure 2 challenge occurs specifies what went wrong.


```
     integrity="sha384-LoKEanRPljHoEsT5o+grBn8hgVzoPevwGvRd+gOp/2Xgc4Jx2FQkx29092SKDdeY"
     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

     href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.10.0/adyen.css"

```

### Deprecated

Payment method types **billdesk\_online** and **payu\_IN\_nb** for [Online banking India](/payment-methods/online-banking-india). Use **onlinebanking\_IN** instead.

### Changed

For [Online banking India](/payment-methods/online-banking-india), the payment method type is now **onlinebanking\_IN**.

### New

For the Card Component, the [`onFieldValid`](/payment-methods/cards/web-component#on-field-valid) callback now returns the `issuerBin` (first 8 digits of the card) prop if the card number is 16 or more digits.

## 2022-03-07 — Checkout API

### Improved

* You can now use the `/donations` endpoint on all versions of the API.

### Fixed

* If Amazon Pay is configured as a blocked payment method, it's no longer returned in the `/paymentMethods` response.

## 2022-03-02 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.7.1

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67#releaseNote=2021-03-01-api-67">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### Fixed

* You can now use more than one client key for your integration, for example, if you need different credentials for some merchant accounts.
* Fixed the known issue from [v4.5.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.5.0) where Cartes Bancaires did not work on Apple Pay.

For non-reusable payment links, if a card payment is refused, the shopper can try to pay again for a maximum of 5 times.


### Changed

For card payments in installments, if there's a single installment, Drop-in doesn't return the [`installments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/payments#request-installments) object.

## 2022-02-11 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.7.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67#releaseNote=2021-03-01-api-67">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### New

* New supported payment method: [ACH Direct Debit](/payment-methods/ach-direct-debit).
* For an API-only integration, you can now use the `BankDetailsEncryptor` to encrypt bank details (account number and routing number). This is required for ACH.
* For the Card Component, you can configure the countries/regions in the billing address drop-down menu using [`billingAddressCountryCodes`](https://adyen.github.io/adyen-ios/5.0.0/documentation/adyen/).

### Changed

* Moved the WeChat binary from the `AdyenWeChatPay` module to an external repository. If you're using WeChat Pay with [Carthage](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-ios#carthage), add `AdyenWeChatPayInternal` to your project. If you're using WeChat Pay with CocoaPods and Swift Package Manager, the change happens automatically as part of the update.

### Improved

* For the Card Component, the focus moves to the next field when the entered card number reaches the expected length.
* If testing Apple Pay using the Simulator, you get an `invalidToken` error. The error description now explains that you must use a device instead.
* Increased the reliability of app-to-app redirect detection by increasing the delay for the `BrowserComponent`.
* Update the WeChat Pay Component to use [WeChat SDK 1.9.2](https://developers.weixin.qq.com/doc/oplatform/en/Downloads/iOS_Resource.html).

### Fixed

* Apple Pay Component no longer calls `didFail` for a successful payment. This used to happen if you didn't dismiss the Apple Pay view immediately after calling `finalizeIfNeeded(with: success)`.
* Text fields, except the card number and CVC, now allow the system default input options.

## 2022-02-07 — Checkout API

### Fixed

Fixed an issue where payment requests with a billing address had the `stateOrProvince` field populated with **ZZ**.

## 2022-02-07 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.7.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Fixed

* If there's an error during the 3D Secure 2 authentication flow, the **Pay** button now returns to the default state from the loading state.

### New

* In addition to gift card logos, you can now also configure custom names for gift cards.

### Updating

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.7.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.7.0 --save
```

## 2022-02-02 — Checkout API

### Fixed

Fixed an issue with Prosodie and Illicado gift cards where the balance check request always returned a **Success** result code.

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=68">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

When you get a network error, [`error.message`](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow?platform=Web\&integration=Components) now contains the message from the API response instead of a generic one.

When [`setStatusAutomatically`](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow?platform=Web\&integration=Components#optional-configuration) is set to `true`, an overlay now prevents shoppers from interacting with payment method fields after pressing the **Pay** button.

## 2022-01-17 — Pay by Link

### Improved

When you create a payment link in the Customer Area and have one physical store, you can now clear the **Store** field if you don't want the payment link to be associated with that store.

## 2022-01-11 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.6.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Improved

* The new property, [`SRConfig`](/payment-methods/cards/web-drop-in#screen-reader-config), lets you configure screen reader behaviour when there are errors in the Card Component fields. This property doesn't affect how error messages are rendered in the checkout form.

### Fixed

* Fixed the known issue with character input for the card holder field. This issue affected the Card Component when used with web browsers on Android devices and is present in [v5.4.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=5.4.0) and [v5.5.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=5.5.0).


## 2022-01-10 — Pay by Link

### Improved

If you get an error when creating a payment link in the Customer Area, you now get a PSP reference number in the error message. Adyen can use it to help you find out the cause of the error.

### Fixed

Fixed an issue where, when creating a payment link in the Customer Area, more than one radio button could be selected in the **Link validity** section.

## 2022-01-06 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.6.1

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### Fixed

* Values entered in the card number and CVC fields can no longer be copied or shared.
* For stored card payments, the CVC field value is now cleared when the shopper cancels or an error happens.

## 2021-12-20 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.5.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

The PayPal Component now renders a PayLater button in countries/regions where it is available. If you do not want the PayLater button to be rendered, you can use [`blockPayPalPayLaterButton: true`](/payment-methods/paypal/web-component#paypal-paylater).

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.5.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.5.0 --save
```

```

     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

<link rel="stylesheet"
     href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.5.0/adyen.css"
     integrity="sha384-47lm6XSs4AdvQN9BdRTZykpp82IALHlxMtM5p378Nsg3O3nGoBB86N0d7GXgjrA3"
     crossorigin="anonymous">
```

### Known Issues

* Shoppers using web browsers on Android devices cannot type into the card holder name field. [Fixed in v5.6.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=5.6.0).
* When using Internet Explorer 11, card input fields do not appear and work correctly. Fixed in [v5.6.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&tab=npm-recommended-_2022-01-18-n9wc_1#releaseNote=2022-01-18-web-componentsdrop-in-5.6.1).

## 2021-12-20 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.6.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### New

* BACS Direct Debit is now supported through a native flow.
* On Drop-in, you can configure if you want to show the preselected stored payment method using [`allowPreselectedPaymentView`](https://adyen.github.io/adyen-ios/5.0.0/documentation/adyen/).

### Fixed

* Fix `NSLayoutConstraint` errors in the debug log.

## 2021-12-20 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.4.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=api\&amp;version=67">Checkout API v67\</a>.\</p>*

### New

* Payment method: BACS Direct Debit.

### Fixed

* The Google Pay environment is no longer being incorrectly set to `WalletConstants.ENVIRONMENT_PRODUCTION`.

### Deprecated

* `DropInServiceResult.Action(actionJSON: String)` is deprecated. Use `DropInServiceResult.Action(action: com.adyen.checkout.components.model.payments.response.Action)` instead. To serialize your JSON response string, use `com.adyen.checkout.components.model.payments.response.Action.SERIALIZER`.

## 2021-12-14 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.4.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Changed

We changed how Drop-in and Components handle browser compatibility, which means polyfills no longer polute the global scope. This change doesn't affect the browser support of your Drop-in or Components integration.

### Fixed

* Fixed the [known issue from v5.2.2](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=5.2.2) where PayPal didn't load if you integrated using the `/sessions` endpoint.
* Fixed an issue where PayPal was not loading when re-mounting with a different type of currency.
* Fixed issues with entering Japanese characters in text input fields.
* Drop-in and Components no longer crash when imported on a server-side context, for example if using Angular Universal, Nuxt.js, or Next.js.

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.4.0):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.4.0 --save
```

### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

```
<script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.4.0/adyen.js"
     integrity="sha384-TrjObe7+xENaI4cONDkap2qTzoJIfTaC79+mJuANZ5Y1IxLMvqpPKcmgl3IrqCk3"
     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

<link rel="stylesheet"
     href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.4.0/adyen.css"
     integrity="sha384-47lm6XSs4AdvQN9BdRTZykpp82IALHlxMtM5p378Nsg3O3nGoBB86N0d7GXgjrA3"
     crossorigin="anonymous">
```

### Known Issues

* Shoppers using web browsers on Android devices cannot type into the card holder name field. [Fixed in v5.6.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=5.6.0).
* When using Internet Explorer 11, card input fields do not appear and work correctly. Fixed in [v5.6.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&tab=npm-recommended-_2022-01-18-n9wc_1#releaseNote=2022-01-18-web-componentsdrop-in-5.6.1).

## 2021-12-13 — Pay by Link

### Fixed

Fixed an issue where the [email notification](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/customer-area#email-updates) for a 3D Secure payment could be missing the **payment method**.

## 2021-12-07 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.5.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### New

* On Drop-in, the [`allowsSkippingPaymentList`](https://adyen.github.io/adyen-ios/5.0.0/documentation/adyen/) flag lets you skip the screen with the list of payment methods, if there is only one payment method available to the shopper. For example, if card is the only available payment method, the shopper will be taken directly to the card details input screen.
* On Drop-in, you can now customize the screen with the Apple Pay button using `DropInComponent.Style.applePay`.

### Improved

We added support for the following [card networks in Apple Pay](/payment-methods/apple-pay#supported-networks):

* Cartes Bancaires (`cartesBancaires`) in France.
* MADA (`mada`) in the Middle East.

### Fixed

* Card brand detection for dual-branded cards is now based on the first 11 digits of the card number.

### Known Issues

* Cartes Bancaires does not work with Apple Pay. [Fixed in v4.7.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.7.1).

## 2021-12-06 — Pay by Link

### Improved

On the **Payment links** page, you can now:

* Filter payment links by **Payment link ID**.
* Add a column for **Shopper email**.

## 2021-12-02 — Pay by Link

### New

Added support for [Arabic](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/api#language).

### Improved

Improved validation for **Shopper reference** when creating a payment link in the Customer Area.

## 2021-12-02 — Checkout API

### Fixed

We fixed an issue where iDEAL payments could be refused if the **POST** `/payments` request included a `store`.

## 2021-11-25 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.3.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### New

For Drop-in, you can now render the [Apple Pay](/payment-methods/apple-pay/web-drop-in#optional-configuration) and [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-drop-in#optional-configuration) buttons at the top of the payment method list.

### Improved

* The icons in the payment method list UI have more padding in the focus state.

* When using Internet Explorer 11, card input fields do not appear and work correctly. Fixed in [v5.6.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&tab=npm-recommended-_2022-01-18-n9wc_1#releaseNote=2022-01-18-web-componentsdrop-in-5.6.1).

### Fixed

* Fixed an issue where if you integrated using the `/sessions` endpoint and have configured `onAdditionalDetails` on the front end, the front end was automatically submitting additional payment details. This means that submitting additonal payment details from your server using the `/payments/details` call no longer fails.

## 2021-11-15 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.2.1

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later.\</p>*

### Fixed

* Accessibility improvements:

  * Screen readers no longer read out card brand logos or the CVC icon, which was exposing unnecesary information about decorative images.
  * Card brand logos are no longer focusable using the keyboard, which was stopping users on unactionable elements.
  * You can remove the iframe `title` for the [Custom Card Component](/payment-methods/cards/custom-card-integration) by replacing it with `role="presentation"`.

* Fixed an issue where on Firefox, users couldn't use the **Tab** key to move between Card Component fields.

* Entering card numbers that contain all the same digit, like **0000 0000 0000 0000 0000**, now produces an **Invalid card brand** error on the front end.

### Removed

The deprecated Internet Explorer 11 attributes `scrolling` and `frameborder`.

### Known Issues

When using Internet Explorer 11, card input fields don't appear and work correctly. Fixed in [v5.6.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&tab=npm-recommended-_2022-01-18-n9wc_1#releaseNote=2022-01-18-web-componentsdrop-in-5.6.1).

### Updating

### Tab: npm (recommended)

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.2.1):

```sh
npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.2.1 --save
```

### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

```
<script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.2.1/adyen.js"
     integrity="sha384-zvgjXeNFYcp8Q7t3r3fnPKU77VAn6RA4ZDZnP6KtPAmDtJPz5f1DRV+1Lsjau8j0"
     crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

<link rel="stylesheet"
     href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.2.1/adyen.css"
     integrity="sha384-gaEp/efBcplH05NvdYnrdMcaKBrhlK/jau5tB/MkhdOsY7sz6mUnu5fa26lNzt+i"
     crossorigin="anonymous">
```

## 2021-11-15 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.9.1

*\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### Fixed

* The encrypted token returned by `CardEncryptor.Card.encryptedToToken()` now also encodes the time stamp for when the token was created.

## 2021-11-15 — Pay by Link

### Improved

We've added ways to customize the list of payment links, for example to show and hide fields, or rearrange columns.

## 2021-11-10 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.9.0

*\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### New

* You can now use `CardEncryptor.Card.encryptedToToken()` to encrypt all card details as a single token.

### Improved

* Upgraded the 3D Secure 2 SDK version to [v2.2.4.](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-ios/releases/tag/2.2.4).
* The `CardDetails` object now contains the version of the 3D Secure SDK in `threeDS2SDKVersion`.

## 2021-11-10 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.4.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### New

* For dual-branded cards, the Card Component now renders both brands, and allows the cardholder to choose the brand they want to pay with.
* You can now prefill shopper information for card payments. You can prefill the cardholder name, billing address, postal code and social security number, by injecting it when creating the component.
* On Drop-in, you can now allow shoppers to remove stored cards. Implement [`StoredPaymentMethodsDelegate`](https://adyen.github.io/adyen-ios/5.0.0/documentation/adyen/) and set\
  [`paymentMethodListConfiguration.allowDisablingStoredPaymentMethods`](https://adyen.github.io/adyen-ios/5.0.0/documentation/adyen/) to **true** in the Drop-in configuration.
* For the Card Component UI, once the card brand is detected, the supported card brand logos become more transparent to make them less prominent.

### Improved

* Upgraded the 3D Secure 2 SDK version to [v2.2.4](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-ios/releases/tag/2.2.4).

### Fixed

* Shoppers can no longer enter invalid characters in the checkout form.
* Fixed an issue with the Drop-in slide-in animation.
* If there's a network error when polling for the status of a payment with `action.type`: **await**, Drop-in/Components now does another check before returning a failure response. This applies to payment methods where the shopper needs to go to another app to complete the payment, for example BLIK and MB WAY.

## 2021-11-09 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.3.0

`Environment.TEST` is now the default for initializing `DropInConfiguration`. Set it to [a live environment value](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow?platform=Android\&integration=Drop-in#test-and-go-live) for release builds.

### Deprecated

* `saveState` and `restoreState` methods in `action` components. All components and providers now use `SavedStateHandle`.
* `requiresConfiguration` method in `action` component providers. All components require configurations so this method always returns **true**.

### New

* Added **LIVE** as an [environment configuration](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow?platform=Android\&integration=Drop-in#test-and-go-live), and it is the same as **EUROPE**.
* The Card Component now supports installments.
* Drop-in or the Component now returns [`fundingSource`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-paymentMethods-fundingSource) if it is present in the `/paymentMethods` response.
* You can now skip the payment methods screen if there is a single available payment method by using `DropInConfiguration.Builder.setSkipListWhenSinglePaymentMethod`. This does not apply to Google Pay, PayPal, WeChat Pay or payment methods that require a redirect.

### Improved

* When the shopper enters the number for an unsupported card, the Card Component returns an error saying the brand is not supported.
* Updated the 3D Secure SDK to [v.2.2.5](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.5).

### Fixed

* Features that are only available in debug builds are now working correctly.
* The Card Component no longer crashes if you are using it without the 3D Secure library.
* For Google Pay, Drop-in and Components now handle cancellation and failure callbacks on initialization.

## 2021-11-02 — Checkout API

### Improved

* The **POST** `/paymentMethods` repsonse now contains the [`brands`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-paymentMethods-brands) array for Bancontact.

## 2021-11-01 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.2.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later. \</p>*

### New

For [PayPal](/payment-methods/paypal), you can include a `cspNonce` to add a [Content Security Policy (CSP) nonce](https://developer.paypal.com/sdk/js/configuration/#link-cspnonce) if you use this on your site.

### Improved

* For Japanese convenience stores (`econtext_stores` and `econtext_seven_eleven`) you can now [hide the input fields for shopper name, email, and telephone number](/payment-methods/econtext-konbini/web-component#optional-configuration).
* For [Bancontact card](/payment-methods/bancontact/bancontact-card), you can now override the supported brands by including a `[brands]` array.
* Drop-in, the Card Component, and the Custom Card Component can now handle cards with an optional expiry date. An optional expiry date is marked as optional in the UI, and is not required for a card to pass the validation checks.

### Updating

Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.2.0):

## 2021-10-18 — Pay by Link

### Improved

* If a shopper accepts the Terms and Conditions and refreshes the page, we no longer show the modal for accepting Terms and Conditions.
* When uploading an image to a theme, we now show a loading state in the image upload component.

### Fixed

We fixed an issue where the payment link could have default validity, even though you had specified a custom validity when creating the payment link in the Customer Area.

## 2021-10-13 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.3.0

*\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a>.\</p>*

If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

### New

* Doku, Affirm, MB WAY and Japanese conveninence store payments now support prefilling the shopper information in the payment form. You can prefill shopper infromation by injecting it when creating the component.

* Added translations for the warning text the shopper sees when they enter a card brand you do not support.

* For shoppers who do not have cards set up on Apple Pay, use `allowOnboarding` to either:

  * Allow shoppers to set up Apple Pay by going through the Apple Pay onboarding.
  * Not show Apple Pay in the checkout form.

### Improved

* The Boleto Component now allows shoppers to edit the prefilled billing address.

### Fixed

* Fixed an issue with form views freezing when updating the layout.

## 2021-10-13 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.6

*\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*
