## 2021-10-06 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 5.0.0

*\<p>This release requires \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=68\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v68\</a> or later. \</p>*

If upgrading from v3.23.0 or earlier, have a look at the [migration guide](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-web-4-0-0).

### Known Issues

* Payments using Apple Pay, Google Pay, or PayPal do not work for [Components integrations](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow?platform=Web\&integration=Components). The issue is fixed in [v5.1.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=5.1.0).

### Deprecated

The following props from `AdyenCheckout` configuration:

* `allowPaymentMethods`
* `removePaymentMethods`

To customize which payment methods are available for the shopper, include [`allowedPaymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/paymentMethods#request-allowedPaymentMethods) or [`blockedPaymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentMethods#request-blockedPaymentMethods) in your `/paymentMethods` request.

### Breaking Changes

* The `onError` callback now returns an object for every Component. The object contains more details about the error. For more information, refer to [Handle Drop-in errors](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow?platform=Web\&integration=Drop-in#initialize-the-payment-session) or [Handle Component errors](/online-payments/components-web#error-handling).

* The creation of `AdyenCheckout` is now asynchronous:

  ### Tab: v5.0.0 or later

  ```js
      const checkout = await AdyenCheckout(configuration); 
  ```

  ### Tab: before v5.0.0

  ```js
      const checkout = new AdyenCheckout(configuration); 
  ```

  ### New

  Using the `/sessions` endpoint, you can integrate Drop-in and Components with a single API call.

  If you already integrated using the `/paymentMethods`, `/payments`, and `/payments/details` endpoints, you can continue using this back end setup in version 5.0.0.

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/5.0.0):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@5.0.0 --save
  ```

  ### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.0.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-kcKKvS6qZbXycrUw31OJ2/2Hz8A8FTsV9anjvpyQc/IWR1SmkFUw7w7F/t5S3qtA"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/5.0.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-0IvbHDeulbhdg1tMDeFeGlmjiYoVT6YsbfAMKFU2lFd6YKUVk0Hgivcmva3j6mkK"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2021-10-06 — Checkout API — Version 68

  This API version is supported by our Java, NodeJS, PHP, Python, Ruby, Go, and .NET libraries.\
  You can find all the [Adyen libraries on GitHub](https://github.com/Adyen).

  ### Breaking Changes

  * **POST** `/paymentMethods`

    * The [`details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-paymentMethods-details) array has been removed.
    * The list of issuers for payment methods like iDEAL or Dotpay, is now in the [`paymentMethods.issuers`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-paymentMethods-issuers) array.
    * For cards, if the `paymentMethods.brands` array has a single element, the value of the `paymentMethods.name` is now the brand name of the available card brand, instead of **Credit Card**.

  * **POST** `/payments`
    * [`checkoutAttemptId`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/payments#request-checkoutAttemptId) replaces `conversionId`.

  * **POST** `/paymentLinks`
    * `amount.value` fields now must be integers. Values like **10.5** now produce a validation error.

  * **GET** `paymentLinks/{linkId}`
    * New `status` value, **paymentPending** for payment methods that have a pending state. Previously, such states had the **completed status**.

  * For payment link resources, [`storePaymentMethodMode`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentLinks#request-storePaymentMethod) replaces [`storePaymentMethod`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentLinks#request-storePaymentMethod) from previous versions. The allowed values for `storedPaymentMethodMode` are:

    * **disabled** (default), the shopper's payment details are not stored.
    * **enabled** and `shopperReference` is provided, the shopper's payment details are stored.
    * **askForConsent**, the shopper can choose in the Pay by Link UI if they want their payment details to be stored.

  ### New

  * **POST** [/sessions](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/sessions): creates a payment session for Web Drop-in and Web Components integrations.

  * **POST** `/paymentMethods`
    * For payment methods with issuer lists, each issuer object now has a [`disabled`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-paymentMethods-issuers-disabled) field indicating if the issuer is available.

  * **POST** `/payments`

    * Responses with `action.type`: **qrCode** now contain the [`expiresAt`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/payments#responses-200-action-CheckoutQrCodeAction-expiresAt) field which has the expiry date in [ISO8601 format](https://www.iso.org/iso-8601-date-and-time-format.html).
    * In your request, you can now send all the fields from the [EMV 3D Secure 2.1.0 specification](https://www.emvco.com/emv-technologies/3-d-secure/).
    * [`mpiData.tokenAuthenticationVerificationValue`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/payments#request-mpiData-tokenAuthenticationVerificationValue)

  * **POST** `/orders`
    * The initial amount of the order is always returned in the [`amount`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/orders#responses-200-amount) object.

  ### Fixed

  * **POST** `/paymentLinks`
    * Fixed an issue where the payments page didn't show a phone number field when configured as part of [`requiredShopperFields`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentLinks#request-requiredShopperFields).

  ### Deprecated

  * **POST** `/payments` and **POST** `/donations` requests:

    * [`homePhone`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/payments#request-accountInfo-homePhone)
    * [`mobilePhone`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/payments#request-accountInfo-mobilePhone)
    * [`workPhone`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/payments#request-accountInfo-workPhone)
    * [`deliveryEmail`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/payments#request-merchantRiskIndicator-deliveryEmail)
    * [`challengeIndicator`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/68/post/payments#request-threeDS2RequestData-challengeIndicator)

  ### Changed

  * **POST** `/payments`
    * All address fields are now optional.

  ## 2021-10-05 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.9.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Improved

  * We added several accessibility improvements for Drop-in and Components.
  * For payment methods with [multiple payment method variants](/development-resources/paymentmethodvariant), such as Klarna or Dragonpay, we fixed an issue where the Component could be created with incorrect payment method data. This issue only occurred when using Components, not when using Drop-in.

  ### New

  For [Pix](/payment-methods/pix), you can configure the Drop-in or the Component to collect shopper's name and the CPF/CNPJ (a unique identifier similar to a social security number), by including `personalDetailsRequired`.

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/4.9.0):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@4.9.0 --save
  ```

  ### Tab: Embed scriptand stylesheet

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.9.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-aEL1fltFqDd33ItS8N+aAdd44ida67AQctv9h57pBGjNJ8E2xxbX/CVALJqO8/aM"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.9.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-0IvbHDeulbhdg1tMDeFeGlmjiYoVT6YsbfAMKFU2lFd6YKUVk0Hgivcmva3j6mkK"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ### Known Issues

  A known issue exists in version 4.9.0 where the Card Component, when created with the type **card**, fails to match the corresponding **scheme** type returned in the /paymentMethods response. This prevents the brands array from the API response from being automatically propagated to the Card Component.

  **Impact**: The list of supported card brands is not displayed automatically, requiring manual intervention.

  **Resolution**\
  To resolve this issue, you have three options:

  * Update (recommended) your integration to version 5.6.0 or later to have the brands array populate automatically.
  * If you must remain on version 4.9.0, manually add the brands array to your Card Component configuration.
  * Initialize the component by creating it as 'scheme' instead of 'card'.

  ## 2021-10-04 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  When creating a theme and uploading a background image with an unsupported format, you will now get a validation error.

  ### Fixed

  If you have uploaded a brand logo, this is now used as the favicon.

  ## 2021-09-29 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.2.0

  *\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### New

  * New supported payment method: [Multibanco](/payment-methods/multibanco).
  * The Card Component now allows you to accept credit card payments in installments using [`InstallmentConfiguration`](https://adyen.github.io/adyen-ios/5.0.0/documentation/adyen/).
  * Shoppers can now dismiss pickers using the new **Done** button.

  ### Changed

  * If you're using Objective-C, we renamed `TextField` to `ADYTextField` to avoid naming conflicts.

  ### Improved

  * The Encryption Component now uses [JSON Web Encryption (JWE)](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7516) with RSA OAEP 256 and AES CBC 256 with HMAC SHA 512. You do not need to make any changes to your integration.
  * The Card Component now shows an error message to the shopper if the card brand they are using is not supported.

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed a UI bug where only part of the **Pay** button was visible.
  * Fixed an issue with `socialSecurityNumber` where setting it to **show** would still hide the field based on the card's BIN.

  ## 2021-09-27 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  * Upgraded to [Web Components 4.8.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=4.8.0#releaseNote=2021-09-20-web-componentsdrop-in-4.8.0).

  ## 2021-09-27 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  Fixed an issue where `girocard_applepay` didn't appear in the `brands` array for Apple Pay in the [/paymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods) response.

  ## 2021-09-22 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.2.0

  *\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a> or later. \</p>*

  ### New

  * For dual-branded cards, the Card Component now renders both brands, and allows the cardholder to choose the brand they want to pay with.\
    This makes the Component compliant with European regulatory guidelines for accepting dual-branded cards (EU IFR Regulation 2015/751 article 8).
  * For Bancontact, you can now show a toggle for saving the card details for later payments. To do this, call `BcmcConfiguration.Builder.setShowStorePaymentField(true)`.
  * Added support for removing Observers attached to Components.
  * Added support for the [Activity Result API](https://developer.android.com/training/basics/intents/result).

  ### Improved

  * The supported brands for Google Pay are now automatically configured based on your account settings, so you no longer need to specify these using `setAllowedCardNetworks`.
  * For the Card Component, the `paymentComponentState.data.paymentMethod` now includes the `threeDS2SdkVersion`.

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue where Drop-in could crash when trying to render stored payment methods.
  * We fixed an issue where Drop-in would not render Bancontact unless you included a `BcmcConfiguration` object.
  * For Google Pay, we no longer pass the `TotalPrice` if the `TotalPriceStatus` is set to `NOT_CURRENTLY_KNOWN`, in line with the [Google Pay specifications](https://developers.google.com/pay/api/web/reference/request-objects).

  ## 2021-09-20 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  * If a shopper refreshes the page after an unsuccessful payment attempt, the delivery address form is no longer hidden.
  * For reusable payment links, the delivery address used for the first payment is no longer being stored for the following payments.

  ## 2021-09-20 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.8.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Fixed

  * For payment methods with multiple [payment method variants](/development-resources/paymentmethodvariant), such as Klarna or Dragonpay, we fixed an issue where the Component could be created for the incorrect variant. This issue only occurred when using Components, not when using Drop-in.
  * Fixed an issue where the Card Component state was not correctly updated in case you entered correct card details, and then pasted a full unsupported card number.

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/4.8.0):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@4.8.0 --save
  ```

  ### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.8.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-JM4Li2UogXxSpxmM8Xoj1CeRTyzkcoEaU7cTJlK+34aOZ3k8W+eTVrrvW8kpbphp"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.8.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-abv8co6wyDCexfM8Iv1sH5xYfTh9wAO5gzwVj0aLZkIOw7dQFEV5ZDwSrywJ7CYf"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ### Improved

  Accessibility improvement: All shopper input fields now have a `name` property. This makes sure their `<label>` elements get a `for` attribute, and that the shopper input is given an `id` that matches the value of the `for` attribute.

  ## 2021-09-13 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  Fixed an issue where JCB cards enabled for Apple Pay would show up as part of the `paymentMethod.brands` array instead of the `brands` array for Apple Pay. This means Web Drop-in and Components would render the Apple Pay logo as part of the Card Component.

  ## 2021-09-06 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.7.5

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you're using [native web components](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Web_Components), we fixed an issue where opening a dropdown list in Adyen's Web Components or Drop-in would cause it to close it back again immediately.

  ## 2021-09-06 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.1.0

  *\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a>.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### New

  * New supported payment methods: Affirm, OXXO.

  * The Card Component now has support for:

    * Korean card payments through KCP.
    * Brazillian card payments that require the social security number.
    * UK addresses.

  * Shoppers can now add vouchers to Apple Wallet.

  * Get the last 4 digits of the entered card number using [`didSubmit(lastFour:component:)`](https://adyen.github.io/adyen-ios/5.0.0/documentation/adyen/).

  ### Improved

  * If a shopper enters card data and then closes the payment screen, the component now clears the values for improved security.
  * The Card Component now runs validation on optional CVC fields if the shopper enters a value.
  * Disabled Luhn checks for private label cards (also known as white label cards), because the check fails even if the card number is valid.
  * The card number field now has a numeric keypad for input.
  * The Boleto Component has improved validation for social security numbers.
  * There are smoother transitions between Drop-in screens.
  * Redesigned the Drop-in UI for iPad so that it is responsive to screen size and feels native to the iPad.
  * Redesigned the UI for voucher payment methods to make it easy for shoppers to find the call to action.
  * Added a [**README.md** file](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-ios/tree/develop/Demo) for the demo projects.
  * Updated the 3D Secure SDK to [v2.2.3](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-ios/releases/tag/2.2.3).
  * Moved the networking layer into a separate framework as a dependency.

  ### Fixed

  * You can now use CocoaPods on M1 Mac devices.
  * Fixed a bug where card brand logos were not showing up if the shopper deleted the initial card number and entred a value again.
  * Single digit house numbers no longer fail validation.

  ## 2021-09-06 — Checkout API

  ### New

  **POST** [/paymentLinks](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/paymentLinks)

  * `themeId` field to customize the appearance of the payment page when creating a payment link. This field is available in [Checkout API v67](/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&integration_type=api) and later.

  ### Fixed

  All lists of countries/regions are now ordered alphabetically for each translation.

  ## 2021-09-06 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  * Pay by Link now has its own section in the Customer Area main navigation.

  * Create and choose themes for the payment page when using Pay by Link:

    * [In the Customer Area](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/customer-area#customize)
    * [Through the API](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/api#themes)

  ### Improved

  * You can now have numbers in the card holder name.

  ### Fixed

  We reverted the [improvement from 23 Aug 2021](/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2021-08-23-pay-by-link) because it was causing problems for some payment methods.

  ## 2021-08-30 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.7.4

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Fixed

  * The `isAvailable` method for Apple Pay has a shorter execution time, since it no longer invokes `canMakePayments` twice.
  * We fixed an issue where an input in an error state was not properly validated in case it lost focus.

  ### Improved

  * If it is not possible to fetch the language of the browser, we now populate the `browserInfo.language` field with a default value of `en`.
  * When showing that input is valid or invalid, address input fields now behave in the same way as all other input fields.

  ## 2021-08-26 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.1.1

  *\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a>.\</p>*

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed an issue on the Card Component where address fields were still shown even if `AddressVisibility` was set to **NONE**.

  ## 2021-08-23 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  If a shopper refreshes the page after entering payment details for a voucher or a redirect payment method, they now have to choose whether to continue with the payment, or to start over. This helps to prevent shoppers from making accidental duplicate payments.

  ## 2021-08-23 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where a [splits](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments/\(paymentPspReference\)/captures#request-splits) array included in the [`/payments/{paymentPspReference}/captures`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/\(paymentPspReference\)/captures) request was not processed correctly.

  ## 2021-08-18 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.1.0

  *\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a>.\</p>*

  ### New

  * The Card Component now has support for:

    * Korean card payments through KCP.
    * Brazillian card payments that require the social security number.
    * The postal code input field.

  * On Drop-in, you can now take shoppers to the list of all available payment methods instead of only showing their stored cards. To do this, call `DropInConfiguration.Builder.showPreselectedStoredPaymentMethod(false)`.

  * Support for Android 12.

  ### Improved

  * Configuration objects now do runtime validation to make sure your client key matches the environment it is being used on.
  * Disabled Luhn checks for private label cards (also known as white label cards), because the check fails even if the card number is valid.
  * Updated the 3D Secure SDK to [v2.2.4](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.4).

  ### Changed

  * The focus no longer automatically switches to the next input field after the card number has been filled in.

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed an issue where card brand logos weren't loading if there were multiple logos of the same type, for example when the shopper had multiple stored cards of the same brand.
  * If the Android operating system stops your app before the payment flow is completed, this no longer causes Drop-in to fail.

  ## 2021-08-16 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  * We improved the error messages for when a user creating a payment link in the Customer Area doesn't have the required permissions.

  ## 2021-08-16 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  * **Finnish e-banking** now appears as **Verkkopankkimaksu** in the front end when the locale is set to **fi-FI**.

  ### Fixed

  * For iDEAL payments, sending emoji in the [productTitle](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments#request-additionalData-AdditionalDataRisk-riskdata-basket-item_itemNr_-productTitle) no longer causes an error during the redirect.

  ## 2021-08-09 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  * A `merchantReference` that doesn't fit into a single line is now broken into several lines, so it will no longer exceed the payment form border.
  * We improved the message shown to the shopper after completing a payment that has a status of [**Payment pending**](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/customer-area).

  ## 2021-08-09 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  For redirect 3D Secure payments, the [`amount`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/details#responses-200-amount) object in the [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/details) response is now returned in the same way as for all other payments.

  ## 2021-08-02 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.7.3

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Improved

  The Amazon Pay Component now supports [Amazon Pay multi-currency](https://pay.amazon.eu/help/5BDCWHCUC27485L). This feature will be automatically available to you if your Amazon Pay merchant account is in the EU or UK region.

  ### Fixed

  * For card payments, the `holderName` field no longer shows a validation error if empty and configured as optional.
  * Fixed an issue with the white Apple Pay button styling.

  ## 2021-07-26 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  For in-store Pay by Link, we fixed an issue where the result icon on the confirmation page wasn't being rendered.

  ## 2021-07-26 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  For native 3D Secure 2 payments, the [`amount`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/details#responses-200-amount) object in the [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/details) response is now returned in the same way as for all other payments.

  ## 2021-07-19 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  * Payment links created through the Customer Area are now correctly generating recurring payment tokens.
  * Fixed an issue where installments were not available for stored cards.

  ## 2021-07-19 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.7.2

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ## 2021-07-12 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  * If you're entering shopper details manually when creating a payment link in the Customer Area, you can now also add the shopper's first name, last name, and telephone number.

  ### Fixed

  * If you are using the same merchant account for your online payments integration as well as Pay by Link, Apple Pay only appears as an available payment method for Pay by Link if you are using [Adyen's Apple Pay certificate](/payment-methods/apple-pay/web-drop-in#before-you-begin) for your online payments integration.
  * For voucher and bank transfer payments, we added back the **Continue** button which takes the shopper back to your website.
  * When creating a payment link in the Customer Area, you no longer get an error when entering an Australian shopper address.

  ### Known Issues

  Recurring payment tokens are not generated correctly for payment links created through the Customer Area. This is fixed in the [19 July 2020 release](/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2021-07-19-pay-by-link).

  ## 2021-07-12 — Checkout API

  ### New

  * Splitting funds for Adyen for Platform accounts is now possible when doing [refunds](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/\(paymentPspReference\)/refunds#request-splits) and [amount updates](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/\(paymentPspReference\)/amountUpdates#responses-200-splits).

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed an issue where the `/payments/details` response did not contain the 3D Secure 2 fields in the `additionalData` object.
  * Website cross-origin isolation using [COOP and COEP](https://web.dev/coop-coep) is now supported for [Web Components 3.4.0](/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=3.4.0#releaseNote=2020-04-01-ios-componentsdrop-in-3.4.0) and above, as well as all assets on `checkoutshopper`.

  ## 2021-07-08 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 4.0.0

  *\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a>. \</p>*

  For guidance on upgrading to this version, have a look at the [migration guide](/online-payments/android/migrate-to-android-4-0-0).

  ### Breaking Changes

  * All Components, including action Components, now require a configuration object.
  * All configuration objects now require a [client key](/development-resources/client-side-authentication). For the `cardConfiguration` and `bcmcConfiguration` objects, the client key replaces the Client Encryption Public Key used in earlier versions.
  * If using `ResultHandlerIntent`, you now need to pass this in `startPayment` instead of `DropInConfiguration`.
  * The `handleResultIntent` in the `WeChatPayActionComponent` has been replaced by the `handleIntent` method.
  * The `handleRedirectResponse` in the `RedirectComponent` has been replaced by the `handleIntent` method.
  * Refactored `CallResult` to `DropInServiceResult`.
  * Refactored module structure so that each payment method has only one module: the `card-base` and `card-ui` modules have been merged into a single `card` module, and similarly for other payment methods.
  * Refactored the `cse` module, renaming the `Card` class and the `CardEncrypter class`. For more information, refer to our [custom card integration guide](/payment-methods/cards/custom-card-integration?tab=android_3).
  * Renamed package `com.adyen.checkout.base` to `com.adyen.checkout.components`.
  * Renamed package `com.adyen.checkout.base.component` to `com.adyen.checkout.components.base`.

  ### Removed

  * We removed all methods that were marked as deprecated in earlier versions.
  * The `SimplifiedDropInService` has been removed. We recommend that you handle the [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) response using the `DropInService`.
  * For [MB WAY](/payment-methods/mb-way), we removed the `shopperEmail` field because it is no longer required.
  * We removed the `WeChatPayComponent`. If you want to check whether the WeChat app is available, you can do so by calling [`WeChatPayProvider.isAvailable`](/payment-methods/wechat-pay/wechat-pay-in-app-payments/android-component#start-a-payment-with-wechat-pay).
  * We removed `PaymentComponentProvider.isAvailable`. Payment method providers that require an availability check (currently [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay/android-component) and [WeChat Pay](/payment-methods/wechat-pay/wechat-pay-in-app-payments/android-component#start-a-payment-with-wechat-pay)), will now implement `PaymentMethodAvailabilityCheck` instead.

  ### New

  * The `Adyen3DS2Component` supports the [new 3D Secure 2 flow](/online-payments/3d-secure/native-3ds2/android-component#handle-redirect) introduced in [Checkout API v67](/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&integration_type=api). The old flow is still supported if you are using an API version earlier than v67. The `Adyen3DS2Component` implements `IntentHandlingComponent`, and has a `handleIntent` method for [handling 3D Secure 1 redirects](/online-payments/3d-secure/native-3ds2/android-component#handle-redirect).

  * * For [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay), the payment method type is now **googlepay**.

  * The `QRCodeComponent` handles all payment methods with `action.type`: **qrCode**, including [Bancontact mobile](/payment-methods/bancontact/bancontact-mobile/android-component), [Swish](/payment-methods/swish), and [Pix](/payment-methods/pix). The `QRCodeComponent` implements `IntentHandlingComponent`, and has a `handleIntent` method.

  * New supported payment method: [BLIK](/payment-methods/blik).

  * We added support for the following shopper locales: `cs-rCZ`, `el-rGR`, `hr-rHR`, `hu-rHU`, `ro-rRO`, `sk-rSK`, `sl-rSI`.

  * To hide the CVC field, you can now include the `hideCvc` or the `hideCvcStoredCard` flag in the `cardConfiguration`object. If `hideCvcStoredCard` is set to **true** and the shopper pays with a stored card that doesn't require a CVC, they will see a **Pay** button immediately after selecting the payment method.

  * The `CardComponent` now returns the BIN and last 4 digits of the card number in the `CardComponentState`.

  * `DropInService` now has the `onPaymentsCallRequested` and `onDetailsCallRequested` methods for asynchronous handling of the API responses. When handling the API responses synchronously, you can use the `onPaymentsCallRequested` method to access a non-serialized version of the `PaymentComponent` JSON at the moment when the shopper submits the payment.

  * `ActionComponentProvider` has the following new methods:

    * `canHandleAction`: checks if the Component can handle the specified action type.
    * `requiresView`: indicates whether a view is required to handle this action.
    * `getSupportedActionTypes`: returns the list of supported action types.

  ### Improved

  * We improved card brand recognition in the `CardComponent`.
  * For cards that do not require a CVC, the CVC field will now be marked as optional.
  * We updated the UI for phone number input for [MB WAY](/payment-methods/mb-way).
  * We updated the 3D Secure SDK to version [2.2.0](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.2.2).
  * For the custom [card integration with encryption](/payment-methods/cards/custom-card-integration?tab=android_3), we now validate the Client Encryption Public Key.
  * Drop-in now has a default `GooglePayConfiguration` and a `CardConfiguration` object, so it is no longer required to set these configurations in `DropInConfiguration.Builder`.
  * You can now see the GooglePay SDK dependency, so you no longer need to explicitly declare it to access Google Pay specific code.
  * The default [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay/android-component) environment now automatically follows the Adyen environment.

  ### Changed

  * New releases are now published to [Maven Central](https://repo1.maven.org/maven2/com/adyen/checkout/).
  * We migrated from the Android Support Library to AndroidX.
  * The minimum supported Android API version is now [21](https://developer.android.com/reference/android/os/Build.VERSION_CODES#LOLLIPOP).
  * For the [`GooglePayConfiguration.Builder`](/payment-methods/google-pay/android-component#show-google-pay-in-your-payment-form), you no longer need to pass a `merchantAccount`. Instead, you need to pass a client key, as for all other configuration objects.
  * `GooglePayComponent` is now an `ActivityResultHandlingComponent`. No methods are affected by this change.
  * For debug builds, you are now allowed to take a screenshot of the Card Component.
  * `DropInService` is now a regular bound `Service` instead of a `JobIntentService`.
  * For CI builds and example app without values, `example.local.gradle` has been renamed to `default.local.gradle`.

  ### Fixed

  * Drop-in now renders all available payment methods at the same time.
  * Intent results are now also handled in case `DropInActivity` got destroyed.

  ## 2021-07-05 — Pay by Link

  ### Changed

  * Changed the date format in the Customer Area payment links overview to be more readable.

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed an issue where the `merchantReference` would not be correctly returned in the `/payments/details` response during 3D Secure transactions.
  * Downloading the payment links CSV file from the Customer Area now works on Safari.

  ## 2021-06-30 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 4.0.0

  *\<p>We recommend you use \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&amp;integration\_type=api">Checkout API v67\</a>.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### Breaking Changes

  * Removed support for iOS 10.0.
  * The new minimum required TLS version is 1.2.
  * iOS Components has a [new module structure](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-ios-4-0-0#new-module-structure).
  * Each [component initialization](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-ios-4-0-0#component-initialization) requires an instance of `apiContext`, which specifies the client key and the environment.
  * Changed [how the end of a component flow is handled](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-ios-4-0-0#end-of-component-flow).
  * Renamed the [payment method configuration objects](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-ios-4-0-0#payment-method-configuration).
  * In the `Adyen` module, the `Amount` object now adopts the [`Codable`](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swift/codable) protocol.
  * Moved the `payment` property from `PresentableComponent` to `PaymentComponent`.
  * Specifying the country code is now required for the `Payment` object.
  * Moved the `Amount` property outside of the `Payment` object.
  * The `didCancel` method from `DropInComponentDelegate` now returns `PaymentComponent` instead of `PresentableComponent`.
  * Moved card configuration to the [`CardComponent.Configuration`](https://adyen.github.io/adyen-ios/5.0.0/documentation/adyen/) object.
  * Moved Apple Pay configuration to the `ApplePayComponent.Configuration` object.
  * Moved the `payment` parameter to the `ApplePayComponent.Configuration` constructor.
  * Changed how you [specify supported Apple Pay card networks](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-ios-4-0-0#apple-pay-supported-card-networks).
  * The Apple Pay token is now a base64-encoded string. This replaces the plain text JSON format.
  * To proccess voucher payments your app's **Info.plist** must contain `NSPhotoLibraryAddUsageDescription`.
  * Renamed `DropInActionComponent` to `AdyenActionComponent` and moved it to [`AdyenActions` module](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-ios-4-0-0#new-module-structure). This allows you to use the component outside of `AdyenDropIn`.
  * Renamed `AdyenActionComponent.perform()` to `AdyenActionComponent.handle()` for consistency with other action components.
  * To present the redirect component UI, you now need to set `RedirectComponent.presentationDelegate`.
  * The styles for all action components are now grouped under `ActionComponentStyle`.

  Have a look at the [migration guide](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-ios-4-0-0) for details about the more complex breaking changes.

  ### Removed

  * [Deprecated classes, functions, properties](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-ios-4-0-0#remove-deprecated).
  * `disableCloseButton` from `present(component: PresentableComponent)` in `PresentationDelegate`.
  * `cancelHandler` from the `ApplePayComponent`.
  * `cancelCallback` from the Apple Pay component. Use the `didFail(with error: Error, from component: PaymentComponent)` function with `ComponentError.cancelled` instead.

  ### Changed

  * `paymentData` is now optional in `RedirectAction`, `ActionComponentData` and `RedirectComponent`. This is to support the removal of `paymentData` starting [Checkout API v67](/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&integration_type=api).

  ### New

  * The `ThreeDS2Component` supports the new 3D Secure flow from [Checkout API v67](/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&integration_type=api). The old flow is still supported if you are using an API version earlier than v67.
  * `VoucherComponent` handles actions for voucher payment flows, for example Doku Alfamart.
  * `QRCodeComponent` to handle payment methods that use QR codes, like PIX.
  * `DropInComponent` now has the `cardComponentDelegate` property.
  * `DropInComponentDelegate` now has the `didOpenExternalApplication(component:)` callback which notifies you about redirects to an external app.
  * `DropInDelegate.didSubmit` now has a `PaymentMethod` object to allow you to identify which payment method is being submitted.
  * `didComplete(from component: DropInComponent)` method for `DropInComponentDelegate` and `ActionComponentDelegate`.
  * Support for right-to-left layout.
  * Arabic (international) is now a supported language.
  * Adds support for stored payment details for BLIK.
  * You can configure button border color and width using `ButtonStyle`.

  Payment method support:

  * Pix, `PixComponent`

  * Boleto, `BoletoComponent`

  * Indonesian bank transfers and cash payments at convenience stores through `DokuComponent`.

  * Japanese voucher payments through econtext:

    * At Seven Eleven stores, `SevenElevenComponent`.
    * At other convenience stores, `EContextStoreComponent`.
    * Using ATM machines, `EContextATMComponent`.
    * Online, `EContextOnlineComponent`.

  ### Improved

  * Prevent leaking API keys when URL responses are cached.
  * Added CVC length validation for the stored cards component.
  * You can now use `CardEncryptor` to encrypt individual card fields, for example CVC or expiry month.
  * Added [assertion validation](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swift/1541112-assert) for country code and currency code.
  * Improved accuracy for card brand detection.
  * Added billing address fields to the card component.
  * `GiftCardComponent` now has native support for full and partial payments using gift cards.
  * Uses a continous curve by default for rounding corners.
  * You can now control the position of the title and **Cancel** button for DropIn.
  * You can now add a custom image to the **Cancel** button.
  * Added a configuration screen to the [UIKit and SwiftUI demo apps](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-ios/tree/develop/Demo) so you can change configuration fields during runtime.

  ### Fixed

  * The `MBWayComponent` now shows Portugal and Spain in the phone prefix dropdown list of phone extensions.
  * Fixed a bug where gift card payment methods had a missing logo.
  * Fixes localization warning when using Swift Package Manager.
  * For Swish redirects, you now get all the query parameters in `returnUrlQueryString`.
  * Fixed an issue where users couldn't scroll properly through a form.

  ### Known Issues

  For the Apple Pay Component, the `` `PaymentData.amount `` passed from the SDK to the `didSubmit` callback is nil. Fixed in [v4.8.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.8.0).

  ## 2021-06-28 — Pay by Link

  ### Changed

  In the currency dropdown, we're now using the correct **GHS** currency code for the Ghanian cedi.

  ### Fixed

  Fixed an issue in the Customer Area where you couldn't change the order for some payment methods.

  ## 2021-06-28 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  Card data decryption failures now return the correct error **174 - Unable to decrypt data**, instead of **175 - Unable to parse JSON data**.

  ## 2021-06-28 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.7.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Improved

  * The **Cardholder** field now accepts all characters, except digits.

  * We improved accessibility for dropdown menus:

    * You can now close a dropdown menu using the **Esc** key.
    * Pressing **Tab** on a open dropdown moves the focus to the next element after closing the dropdown.

  ### Fixed

  For Google Pay, canceling a payment no longer logs an `unhandled promise` error in the console.

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/4.7.1):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@4.7.1 --save
  ```

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.7.1/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-+6sganTOdD6V0LmuV93Rv4yPqf9Pt/2hDohL1SZ0tnac+qK1kvlsAJPnrFFZsMaK"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.7.1/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-dkJjySvUD62j8VuK62Z0VF1uIsoa+APxWLDHpTjBRwQ95VxNl7oaUvCL+5WXG8lh"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2021-06-23 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  You can now filter and download a CSV of the payment links you created. You can filter by:

  * Merchant reference
  * Status of the payment link
  * How the link was created
  * The user who created the link
  * Creation date
  * Link type

  When filtering by links generated through the API, you get all the links created using Adyen's EU data center. We are working on adding the other data centers.

  ## 2021-06-21 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  We improved the error message that you get when including an invalid `redirectResult` in the [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/details) request.

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where shoppers couldn't add a gift card payment to an existing order.

  ## 2021-06-21 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  * When [entering multiple email addresses for receiving payment updates](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/customer-area#email-updates), you can now separate them by commas.
  * We improved the error message that you get when trying to get a payment link for a combination of country/region and currency for which no payment methods are available.

  ### New

  * Pay by Link is now using [Checkout API v67](/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&integration_type=api) and [Web Components 4.7.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web#releaseNote=2021-06-14-web-componentsdrop-in-4.7.0).
  * The address form in the Customer Area now supports addresses in Australia.
  * Shoppers in Australia can now select a **State** when entering their billing or delivery address on the payment page.

  ## 2021-06-14 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.7.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Changed

  When `onSubmit` is triggered, Component/Drop-in status is now automatically set to `'loading'`. If you want to overwrite the default behavior, set the [`setStatusAutomatically` prop](/online-payments/components-web#configure) to **false**.

  ### Improved

  * Added support for Cartes Bancaires in the Apple Pay Component.
  * We optimized the address form layout for addresses in Australia.

  ```
  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.7.0/adyen.css"

       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2021-06-14 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  If the user creating a payment link in the Customer Area doesn't have the right [permissions](/account/user-roles#online-payments), they now see an error explaining this.

  ## 2021-06-14 — Checkout API

  ### New

  Checkout API v67 and above now supports the [3D Secure authentication-only flow](/online-payments/3d-secure/standalone-authentication).

  ### Fixed

  In Checkout API v64 and below, the [`/paymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentMethods) response now has `supportsRecurring`: **true** for cards.

  Note that `supportsRecurring` was [removed in Checkout API v65](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=api\&version=65).

  ## 2021-06-08 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  We improved the positioning of the **Continue** button shown on a mobile screen after the shopper completes the purchase.

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where the [payment update email](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/customer-area#email-updates) did not contain the billing and the delivery address, even though the shopper had provided these.

  ## 2021-06-08 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  When the shopper cancels a [Bancontact mobile](/payment-methods/bancontact/bancontact-mobile/web-component) payment, you will now receive a `resultCode` **Cancelled** instead of `resultCode`: **Unknown** as before.

  ## 2021-06-03 — Checkout API

  ### New

  You can now do your captures, refunds, cancellations, reversals, and asynchronous authorisation adjustments using the Checkout API.

  Have a look at the new endpoints in API Explorer:

  * [`/payments/{paymentPspReference}/captures`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/\(paymentPspReference\)/captures)
  * [`/payments/{paymentPspReference}/amountUpdates`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/\(paymentPspReference\)/amountUpdates)
  * [`/payments/{paymentPspReference}/refunds`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/\(paymentPspReference\)/refunds)
  * [`/payments/{paymentPspReference}/reversals`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/\(paymentPspReference\)/reversals)
  * [`/payments/{paymentPspReference}/cancels`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments/\(paymentPspReference\)/cancels)
  * [`/cancels`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/cancels)

  ## 2021-06-01 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.3

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * Android Components are now available in [Maven Central](https://mvnrepository.com/artifact/com.adyen.checkout).
  * You can now configure the Card Component to show an input field for the postal code.
  * We updated the 3D Secure 2 SDK to version [2.1.0-rc09](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-android/releases/tag/2.1.0-rc09).

  ### Fixed

  * The Google Pay logo has been updated to according to the [Google Pay brand guidelines](https://developers.google.com/pay/api/android/guides/brand-guidelines#google-pay-logo-mark).
  * For apps targeting compatibility with Android 10, we fixed an issue where collecting the 3D Secure fingerprint could sometimes fail with an exception.

  ## 2021-05-31 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.6.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ## 2021-05-31 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  For the 3D Secure 1 flow in v67, the fraud result is now correctly returned in the [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments/details) response.

  ## 2021-05-25 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  Fixed an issue affecting v67, where the `pspReference` was missing from the `/payments/details` response for Zip and Afterpay Touch.

  ## 2021-05-18 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  The link creation page in the Customer Area now has a:

  * Address form specific to country/region.
  * Search option for the currency selection field.

  ### Fixed

  Fixed an issue that caused some Blik payments to be reported as failed, even though they were successful.

  ## 2021-05-18 — Checkout API

  ### New

  Added support for [cross-origin isolated websites](https://web.dev/coop-coep).

  ## 2021-05-11 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.5

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### Improved

  * The Card Component has better card type detection for Diners cards.
  * Added a postal code field to the Card Component.

  ### Fixed

  * Drop-in now shows the Apple Pay button according to Apple's [Human Interface Guidelines](https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/apple-pay).
  * The BCMC Component no longer shows the additional large title with the component's name on Drop-in.

  ## 2021-05-10 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.5.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * You can now customize the icon for each payment method, by including the `icon` property in the Component configuration. For example, this is how you can customize the Google Pay icon on Drop-in:

  ```js
      {
        paymentMethodsConfiguration: {
         paywithgoogle: {
            icon: 'https://...'
         }
      }
  ```

  * For cards, you can now customize the icon of the card brand, by including the `icon` property in [`brandsConfiguration`](/payment-methods/cards/web-component#component-configuration). For example, this is how you can customize the icon for Visa on Drop-in:

  ```js
  {
    paymentMethodsConfiguration: {
     card: {
        brandsConfiguration: { 
          visa: {
            icon: 'https://...'
          }
        }
     }
  }
  ```

  ### Changed

  The maximum length of a gift card number is now 32 digits, instead of 22 digits as before.

  ### Fixed

  For Amex, the hint for where the shopper can find the CVC no longer shows two locations.

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/4.5.0):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@4.5.0 --save
  ```

  ### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.5.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-Co94gRjtPsf3110lIIB8CaogV5Khwg8lcSh4fK5r1gzfWZHxaqpBXwEFjaFGcLaj"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.5.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-8EGo5meqBqlQ4MFf3nbQYD/onCuTfclYfNl3a5uQ1swwv0XXcTkda75dvjlYbZI8"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2021-05-10 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where shoppers could not use a gift card that didn't have enough balance for the full payment.

  ## 2021-05-10 — Checkout API

  ### New

  For iDEAL payments in v66 and earlier, you will now get a validation error when making a [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/post/payments/details) request with a `redirectResult` and `paymentData` belonging to different payments.

  ### Fixed

  For the 3D Secure redirect flow in v67, we fixed an issue where the `merchantReference` could be missing from the [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments/details) response.

  ## 2021-05-03 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  New supported payment method: Apple Pay

  ## 2021-05-03 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.4.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * Support for right-to-left languages though the HTML `dir` attribute.
  * Added Arabic (international) to our [supported languages](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow/?platform=Web\&integration=Drop-in#optional-configuration).

  ### Fixed

  * For Google Pay, `onSubmit` now correctly calls Drop-in or the Google Pay Component, depending on your integration.
  * For payments partially covered using a gift card, the shopper can now see a message about the amount they need to pay in addition to the gift card amount.

  ## 2021-04-26 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  Fixed an issue where adding a gift card payment to an order could result in a validation error if the request was made using one of our plugins.

  ## 2021-04-19 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.3.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Known Issues

  The Card Component does not work on Internet Explorer 11. This is [fixed in Web Components 4.3.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=4.3.1).

  ### New

  For cards, you can now include [`minimumExpiryDate`](/payment-methods/cards/web-component#minimum-expiry) in the Component configuration. This will prevent shoppers from paying with a card that expires before the specified date.

  ### Improved

  We've animated the final status shown by Drop-in (`success` or `error`).

  ### Fixed

  * When using [Google Pay Drop-in integration](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-drop-in) with your own custom button, the `onSubmit` event no longer triggers twice.
  * For [Amazon Pay](/payment-methods/amazon-pay/web-component), we fixed an issue where the Component did not pass the [`publicKeyId`](/payment-methods/amazon-pay/web-component#step-3) to the call that updates the checkout session state.
  * When changing the country/region in the address form, the optional and required fields are now being reset based on the new country.
  * For the Bank Transfer Component, the `showEmailAddress` parameter is now working as expected.
  * Fields that are prefilled (by including a `data` object in the Component configuration) are now validated as expected.

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/4.3.0):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@4.3.0 --save
  ```

  ### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
       integrity="sha384-cNkiBPQRGouJfbstYuSccx2XkGe3RB28iYrjge6rLIDtex7fk5+3/E9f4EZ34fxE"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.3.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-5CDvDZiVPuf+3ZID0lh0aaUHAeky3/ACF1YAKzPbn3GEmzWgO53gP6stiYHWIdpB"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2021-04-19 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue where the list of payment links in the Customer Area would return no results when using the search bar.
  * When creating a payment link in the Customer Area, searching in the **Store** drop-down now works as expected.

  ## 2021-04-19 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue where the [PayPal Component](/payment-methods/paypal/web-component) could fail when doing a zero-auth transaction.
  * For [Amazon Pay](/payment-methods/amazon-pay), we fixed an issue where a redirect 3D Secure flow was incorrectly triggered instead of the native 3D Secure flow.

  ## 2021-04-19 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  For the native 3D Secure 2 flow in Checkout API v67 and above, we fixed an issue where the `merchantReference` was not returned in the [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments/details) response.

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Fixed

  * For [Boleto Bancário](/payment-methods/boleto-bancario), the payment form now shows the **Country** field (as read-only). This fixes an issue where payments would fail because `state.data` was missing a `billingAddress.country`.

  ## 2021-04-12 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  Inactive [payment links shown in the Customer Area](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/customer-area#view-links) can now have a **Payment pending** status, indicating that the final result of the payment is not yet known. Previously, such payments had a status of **Completed**.

  ### Fixed

  All users creating in-store payment links through the Customer Area can now select the store for which to create the payment link.

  ## 2021-04-06 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  When making an `/applePay/sessions` request, you no longer receive an error when specifying a `domainName` with a slash (`/`) at the end.

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where a [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments) response with status [500](https://httpstatuses.com/500) (Internal Server Error) could include an incorrect `pspReference` value.

  ## 2021-04-06 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  When creating a payment link in the [Customer Area](https://ca-test.adyen.com/), you can now specify fields that the shopper should fill in before completing the payment, for example name, email, or address.

  ## 2021-04-01 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.2.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Known Issues

  For [Boleto Bancário](/payment-methods/boleto-bancario), the `state.data` is missing the `billingAddress.country`, causing the payment to fail. This issue is fixed in [Web Components 4.2.2](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=4.2.2).

  ### Fixed

  * The cardholder name field no longer loses focus when the shopper typing in the field.
  * The **Pay** button is now also disabled for keyboard navigation when the Component is in the `loading` state. This prevents accidental double payments if the shopper uses the keyboard to submit the payment form.

  ## 2021-03-29 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.2.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Known Issues

  * The cardholder name field loses focus when typing in the field. This issue is fixed in [Web Components 4.2.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=4.2.1).
  * For [Boleto Bancário](/payment-methods/boleto-bancario), the `state.data` is missing the `billingAddress.country`, causing the payment to fail. This issue is fixed in [Web Components 4.2.2](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=4.2.2).

  ### New

  * For Google Pay, Drop-in and Components now return a `browserInfo` object. If you need to handle 3D Secure through Google Pay, pass this object inside `browserInfo` when making the [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments) request.
  * You can now render the cardholder name before the card number using `positionHolderNameOnTop`: **true**.
  * To make card payments in Brazil, the social security number is often required. You can use [`socialSecurityNumberMode`](/payment-methods/cards/web-component#optional-configuration) to render a social security number field in your payment form.

  ### Improved

  We improved the accuracy of card brand detection.

  ### Fixed

  Calling `component.data` no longer fails if the component is not fully rendered. This issue came up for components that have a list of issuers, for example iDEAL or Dotpay.

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/4.2.0):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@4.2.0 --save
  ```

  ### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```

       integrity="sha384-SGA+BK9i1sG5N4BTCgRH6EGbopUK8WG/azn/TeIHYeBEXmEaB+NT+410Z9b1ii7Z"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.2.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-oT6lIQpTr+nOu+yFBPn8mSMkNQID9wuEoTw8lmg2bcrFoDu/Ae8DhJVj+T5cUmsM"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2021-03-22 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  Fixed an issue where shoppers paying using the inline Klarna widget integration were unable to complete payments.

  ## 2021-03-22 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  For 3D Secure redirects, the loading page now has text informing the shopper that the process might take a few minutes to complete.

  ## 2021-03-15 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  For [Checkout API v67](/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=67\&integration_type=api) and above, making a [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments/details) request with a URL-encoded `redirectResult` no longer results in a validation error.

  ## 2021-03-09 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.1.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Known Issues

  For [Boleto Bancário](/payment-methods/boleto-bancario), the `state.data` is missing the `billingAddress.country`, causing the payment to fail. This issue is fixed in [Web Components 4.2.2](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=4.2.2).

  ### New

  * We added new Apple Pay button types. For a full list of supported Apple Pay button types, refer to [Button configuration](/payment-methods/apple-pay/web-component).
  * For Google Pay, you can now use the `buttonSizeMode` and `buttonLocale` configuration properties. For more information, refer to [Button options](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-component#button-options).
  * Stored cards can now be configured separately from other cards, by including a `storedCard` object in the `paymentMethodsConfiguration` object.

  ### Changed

  The `placeholder` values for card fields are now only read from [translation fields](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow/?platform=Web\&integration=Drop-in#optional-configuration). Configuring `placeholder` values inside the Card Component is no longer supported.

  ### Fixed

  * We added a missing CSS file export in the npm package.
  * We fixed an issue where the `promise` dependency could be missing when using the ES format bundle.
  * When pasting a dual-branded card number over a previously entered card number, the detected card brand is now being rendered correctly.
  * When paying in IQR (Iraqi Dinar), the amount on the **Pay** button is now rendered correctly.

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/4.1.0):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@4.1.0 --save
  ```

  ### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

