  ```
       integrity="sha384-3tEepwhhMcyxgIbL3HBe3I59BpSMNyKoNrbKWARYH1tJ7K7K6NdTDqOltKlwiVsH"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.1.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-+CPzBNZVkBXu4uXDECnVuVQ24Kl8vWrR61UzuuuUj5IBEP//BQ0G0KDNfz2iPcvJ"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2021-03-08 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  Fixed an issue where canceling an Affirm payment on the website returned a **Payment successful!** message instead of canceling the payment.

  ## 2021-03-05 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.4

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### Improved

  For gift card payments, the Drop-in now shows a branded icon for each available gift card.

  ## 2021-03-01 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  * Fixes an issue where the shopper would be unable to enter their card details if Pay by Link was configured to only accept a single card brand.

  ## 2021-03-01 — Checkout API — Version 67

  *\<p>The changes to the native 3D Secure 2 integration require at least \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=web\&amp;version=3.22.2">Web Components v3.22.2\</a> or \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes?integration\_type=ios\&amp;version=4.0.0">iOS Components v4.0.0\</a> or \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=4.0.0\&amp;integration\_type=android">Android Components v4.0.0\</a>. \</p>*

  ### Breaking Changes

  * **POST** [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments)

    * For native 3D Secure 2 authentication, the response now contains `action.type`: **threeDS2**. This replaces the **threeDS2Fingerprint** and **threeDS2Challenge** parameters used in earlier versions.
    * For redirect 3D Secure authentication, the payment is now authorized after the [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments) request, when the shopper completes the authentication on the issuer's site. In previous versions, the payment was only authorized after the [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments/details) request.

  * **POST** [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments/details)

    * For native 3D Secure 2 authentication, the response can no longer contain an `action` object. This means that native 3D Secure 2 payments no longer require multiple `/payments/details` requests.

    * For redirect 3D Secure authentication, the [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments/details) request is no longer required to authorize the payment, only to verify the payment result. As with other redirect payment methods, the payment is already authorized before the shopper is redirected back to your website.

      To know the payment result even when the shopper did not return to your website, listen for the [AUTHORISATION webhook](/development-resources/webhooks/webhook-types#webhook-structure).

    * For redirect 3D Secure authentication, the shopper is now redirected back to you from the issuer with an HTTP GET instead of HTTP POST. The `returnURL` is appended with a `redirectResult`.

    * For redirect 3D Secure authentication, you now need to submit the `redirectResult` appended to the `returnUrl` when the shopper is redirected back to you. This replaces the `MD` and the `PaRes` parameters used in previous versions, and aligns the flow for 3D Secure redirects with all other redirects.

  ### Known Issues

  The changes in this version have broken the [Klarna widget integration](/payment-methods/klarna/api-only#the-klarna-widget). We are working on fixing this. In the mean time, we recommend that you don't update to this version if you're using the Klarna widget.

  ### Removed

  * **POST** [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments)

    * We removed the following fields that were marked as deprecated in [v64](/online-payments/release-notes/?version%5B0%5D=64\&integration_type=api):

      * `details`
      * `paymentData`
      * `redirect`
      * `authentication`
      * `outputDetails`

  ### New

  * **POST** [`/paymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentMethods)

    * When using SEPA payments with stored details, the `storedPaymentMethods` field in the response now contains an object with `type`: **sepadirectdebit**:

    ```
    "storedPaymentMethods" : [
          {
             "ownerName" : "John Doe",
             "iban" : "GB33BUKB20201555555555",
             "id" : "9916068117659492",
             "name" : "SEPA Direct Debit",
             "supportedShopperInteractions" : [
                "ContAuth"
             ],
             "type" : "sepadirectdebit"
          }
    ]
    ```

  * **POST** [`/paymentLinks`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentLinks)

    * [`requiredShopperFields`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentLinks#request-requiredShopperFields) to specify fields that the shopper needs to fill in before completing the payment.
    * [`themeId`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentLinks#request-themeId) field to cusomize the appearance of the payment page when creating a payment link.

  ### Improved

  * **POST** [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments)
    * For payment methods with `action.type`: **redirect**, the response no longer contains `paymentData`.
  * **POST** [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/payments/details)
    * For payment methods with `action.type`: **redirect**, you no longer need to submit `paymentData`.
  * **POST** [`/paymentLinks`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/67/post/paymentLinks)
    * Sending a [`shopperReference`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/post/paymentLinks#request-shopperReference) of less than three characters now results in a validation error.

  ## 2021-03-01 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  * Recurring BACS Direct Debit payments no longer generate an error in the test environment.

  ## 2021-02-25 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 4.0.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> Deprecated \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version will be \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> on \<strong>August 1, 2026\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v4">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  For more guidance on upgrading to this version, have a look at the [migration guide](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-web-4-0-0).

  ### Breaking Changes

  * The origin key is no longer supported. If you haven't already, [switch to using the client key](/development-resources/client-side-authentication/migrate-from-origin-key-to-client-key) for client-side authentication.
  * Configuration at the component level [overrides all other configuration](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-web-4-0-0#components-configuration-hierarchy).
  * Drop-in now only accepts [props related to itself](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-web-4-0-0#dropin-configuration).
  * We removed some [deprecated props](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-web-4-0-0#props-removed).
  * You must now configure aria labels [using translation fields](/online-payments/build-your-integration/migrate-to-web-4-0-0#aria-labels-in-transaltion-fields).

  ### Known Issues

  For [Boleto Bancário](/payment-methods/boleto-bancario), the `state.data` is missing the `billingAddress.country`, causing the payment to fail. This issue is fixed in [Web Components 4.2.2](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=4.2.2).

  ### New

  * For npm installations, we now also release ES and CommonJS (CJS) format bundles. This is in addition to the Universal Module Definition (UMD) format bundle released with previous versions.

  * For [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay), the payment method type is now **googlepay**.

  * You can now configure action components by [action type](/online-payments/build-your-integration/advanced-flow/?platform=Web\&integration=Components#additional-action) inside `paymentMethodsConfiguration`. For example, to change the default window size for a 3D Secure challenge:

    ```js
    const checkout = new AdyenCheckout({
        paymentMethodsConfiguration: {
            threeDS2: {
                challengeWindowSize: '05' // '02' is the default size
            }
        }
    });
    ```

  ### Improved

  * The `onChange` event now also returns error and validation information. The `state.valid` object returns a boolean for each field, and `state.errors` can be used to log the state of your form. You should only use the `onError` event for non-validaton errors, for example network errors.
  * Drop-down menus now have search functionality which filters results as you type. This applies, for example, for the list of issuers for iDEAL payments, or the lists of countries, states, or provinces for payment methods that require these.
  * If an issuing bank is offline, it appears greyed out to the shopper and they cannot select it from the dropdown menu. This applies, for example, to Dotpay.

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/4.0.0):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@4.0.0 --save
  ```

  ### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.0.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-tLVjL642U7emY5SakHpLTJK/+V1Lwg75DV44coCF//dRCpT7lmAlp2uYiKg5Al3g"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/4.0.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-dJirTUnCXKPhSVrqqL9ZxAssgCxxyquiIgX1piD13kzrUg3Lr9+Kb+Y9CYEXnHLN"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2021-02-08 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  When redirecting to your *Terms & Conditions*, we now add the [`shopperLocale`](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/api#language) to the *Terms & Conditions* URL. For example, for Spanish you'll get: `https://terms-and-conditions-url.com/?locale=es-ES`. This allows you to change the language of the *Terms & Conditions* based on the shopper's language.

  ## 2021-02-03 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.21.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Fixed

  The default sizing of the 3D Secure challenge window in previous versions could sometimes lead to issues such as the shopper not being able to scroll down the 3D Secure 2 challenge page. This has now been fixed:

  * The default size of the 3D Secure 2 challenge window is now **02** (390px x 400px) instead of **05**.
  * If you configure the challenge window size to be **05**, we no longer force a 16:9 aspect ratio, so that the window size is now 100% x 100% as expected.

  ### Updating

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/3.21.1):


  ```
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.21.1/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-KM3xJKzswGJ0xqiPCOHrWUtn0i0LHqEngauvYxSfy0eRtqcImL7ArxjV2HVgeRJ/"
  ```

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ## 2021-01-14 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.3

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed a bug where custom localization was not being applied to the `AwaitComponent`.

  ## 2021-01-11 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  Making a [BLIK](/payment-methods/blik) payment with saved payment details no longer results in an error.

  ## 2021-01-11 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.20.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * New supported payment method: [Amazon Pay](/payment-methods/amazon-pay/web-component). This payment method is only available on Components, not on Drop-in.

  * For cards, you can now use the `onBinLookup` callback. This is only available on Drop-in and Components, not on the Custom Card Component.

    The `onBinLookup` callback was improved in version [3.21.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=3.21.0). We recommend updating to this version if you want to use it.

  ### Improved

  * The supported brands for [Apple Pay](/payment-methods/apple-pay) and [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay) are now automatically configured based on your account settings, so you no longer need to specify these in the `supportedNetworks` (for Apple Pay) or in `allowedCardNetworks` (for Google Pay).
  * We added support for new [BIN](/get-started-with-adyen/adyen-glossary/#bank-identification-number-bin)s on the [Bancontact card Component](/payment-methods/bancontact/bancontact-card/web-component).
  * Accessibility improvement: we've added translations for the `aria` attributes on the [Card Component](/payment-methods/cards/web-component) and the [Custom Card Component](/payment-methods/cards/custom-card-integration).

  ### Fixed

  * You no longer receive API validation errors when making valid payment requests for voucher payment methods such as [Indonesian bank transfers](/payment-methods/doku/component), [Japanese convenience stores](/payment-methods/econtext-konbini/web-component), or Philippines convenience stores (Dragonpay).

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/3.20.0):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@3.20.0 --save
  ```

  ### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.20.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-UNqekQqUbhwebnism+MhmqRqTuLtMCz7O/dMCjBuMZEoj61mhjM84R+jZDB2BIEg"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.20.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-jXPa2gofpP6MkO/994dAS/W4pn9MgcK9IOebe5jKpnRmzMAvnxBspvqQVpU3jjIH"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2021-01-11 — Pay by Link

  ### Changed

  The maximum validity time for a payment link is now 70 days instead of 30 days.

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where Pay by Link would fail to load on Internet Explorer 11.

  ## 2021-01-05 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue where the **Copy link** button was not working as expected on some devices or browsers.
  * 3D Secure authentication no longer fails if the payment is routed from 3D Secure 2 to 3D Secure 1, for example in case of a technical error in the 3D Secure 2 flow.

  ## 2021-01-05 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  * We've added a button that the shopper can select in case they are not automatically redirected back to your `returnUrl`: *Click here if you are not redirected*.

  ## 2020-12-23 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.2

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### Fixed

  * Removed a bug in the Apple Pay Component where `delegate.didFail()` was sometimes called for a successful transaction.
  * The Apple Pay icon in the Drop-in payment methods list is now compliant with Apple guidelines. This means any customizations in `ListItemStyle` are not applied to the Apple Pay icon.

  ## 2020-12-15 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * New supported payment method: [BLIK](/payment-methods/blik).

  ## 2020-12-14 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.19.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * Support for Boleto Bancário through PrimeiroPay using a redirect payment flow. If using PrimeiroPay, you need to [handle `action.type`: **redirect**](/online-payments/build-your-integration/advanced-flow/?platform=Web\&integration=Components#additional-action).
  * Support for bank transfers through an additional action payment flow. The [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/post/payments) request returns `action.type`: **bankTransfer**.

  ### Changed

  * BACS payments now have an [additional action payment flow](/payment-methods#payment-flow). This means you now need to [handle `action.type`: **voucher**](/online-payments/build-your-integration/advanced-flow/?platform=Web\&integration=Components#additional-action).

  ### Improved

  * The Gift Card Component flow introduced in [Web Components 3.17.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=3.17.0) now has more specific error messages.
  * If you do not specify the [`countryCode`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods#request-countryCode) for [buy now, pay later payment methods](/payment-methods), the Component will show a drop-down menu where the shopper chooses the country/region.
  * The Apple Pay Component now has an `onClick` event, making it consistent with Google Pay and PayPal.

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed an issue where `state.data` had values for `holder.name` filled automatically when `holder.name` was disabled.
  * For Java library users, we fixed an issue where initialization would fail if `paymentMethodsResponse` contained any **null** values.
  * The `onDisableStoredPaymentMethod` Drop-in event now passes the correct `storedPaymentMethodId` parameter.
  * If you are using the [Custom Card Component](/payment-methods/cards/custom-card-integration) with separate date fields without providing translations for [placeholder values](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow/?platform=Web\&integration=Drop-in#optional-configuration), the Component will use the default translations for the placeholders.
  * For dual branded cards, we fixed the error where brand selection didn't work, meaning the shopper could only pay with the default card brand.
  * Fixed an issue with the [PayPal Component](/payment-methods/paypal/web-component), where setting a invalid color on PayPal Credit would cause an error. The Component now defaults to valid PayPal colors.
  * Fixed minor styling issues for the QiwiWallet Component.

  ### Updating

  ### Tab: npm (recommended)

  Install this version of the [Adyen Web Node package](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@adyen/adyen-web/v/3.19.0):

  ```sh
  npm install @adyen/adyen-web@3.19.0 --save
  ```

  ### Tab: Embed script and stylesheet

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.19.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-taz+R7EBgv7Zhi89Jt8k00cAmOCtxDK586pbjJhl+owvWp7aipA2nawhG8VmPoVP"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.19.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-i7r6Qx/VBv4AYkHxh2Ah3fZd/+4scf5x72ZX6X1rrsxoPrvyr9lGOwacyysZDLWJ"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2020-12-14 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where a [`/paymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/paymentMethods) request with `amount`:**0** would sometimes return an empty response.

  ## 2020-12-08 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### New

  * For stored cards, you can now show or hide the CVC field separately from the card form. To do this, use `PaymentMethodsConfiguration.CardConfiguration.stored.showsSecurityCodeField`.

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed an issue where the public `delegate` property of the `UILabel` could clash with code elsewhere in your integration.

  ## 2020-12-07 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  When creating a payment link in the Customer Area, the **Shopper locale** and the **Link type** are now prefilled with last values that you previously selected.

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where `additionalData.paymentLinkId` was sometimes missing from the **AUTHORISATION** webhook.

  ## 2020-11-30 — Checkout API — Version 66

  ### New

  * **POST** [`/paymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/paymentMethods)

    * For [Apple Pay](/payment-methods/apple-pay/api-only#build-your-payment-form-for-apple-pay) and [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay/api-only#build-your-payment-form-for-google-pay), the response now contains the [`brands`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/paymentMethods#responses-200-paymentMethods-brands) array.
    * If you enabled Boleto Bancário, the response now contains `paymentMethod.type`: **primeiropay\_boleto** for Boleto Bancário payments through PrimeroPay.
    * New payment method: bank transfer, with `paymentMethod.type`: **bankTransfer\_IBAN**.

  * **POST** [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/post/payments)

    * If you enabled Boleto Bancário, you can now include `paymentMethod.type`: **primeiropay\_boleto** to make a payment with Boleto Bancário through PrimeroPay.

    * For bank transfers:

      * The request has `paymentMethod.type`: **bankTransfer\_IBAN**.
      * The response has `action.type`: **bankTransfer** and `action.paymentMethodType`: **bankTransfer\_IBAN**.

  ### Improved

  * **POST** [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/post/payments)

    * For redirects, except 3D Secure redirects, the response now contains `details.key`: **redirectResult** instead of `details.key`: **payload**.
    * Sending a [`shopperReference`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/post/payments#request-shopperReference) of less than three characters now results in a validation error:
      ```
      {
      "status": 422,
      "errorCode": "216",
      "message": "The shopper reference must be at least 3 characters long.",
      "errorType": "validation",
      "pspReference": "R8QTPCQ8HXSKGK82"
      }
      ```

  * **POST** [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/post/payments/details)

    * For redirects, except 3D Secure redirects, [`redirectResult`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/post/payments/details#request-details-redirectResult) is now the only way to specify the redirect result in the request, replacing [`payload`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/post/payments/details#request-details-payload).

  * **GET** [`/paymentLinks/{linkId}`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/66/get/paymentLinks/\(linkId\))

    * In the response, the `status` field now returns **completed** instead of **paid** to avoid confusion for payment methods that require an additional action.

  ## 2020-11-26 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  * [Affirm](/payment-methods/affirm) is now also supported when creating payment links through the Customer Area.
  * We added the support for the following [shopper locales](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/api#language): Czech, Greek, Hungarian, Croatian, Romanian, Slovak, and Slovenian.

  ### Improved

  When creating payment links through the Customer Area, specifying a [**Shopper reference**](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/customer-area#create) of less than three characters now results in a validation error.

  ## 2020-11-26 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  The error messages for client key issues now contain more information about the exact cause of the problem.

  ### Fixed

  When using saved payment details to make a payment, the payment no longer fails if you don't specify a `paymentMethod.type`.

  ## 2020-11-24 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.0

  *\<p>This release requires \<strong>Checkout API v52 or later\</strong>.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### New

  * Support for [Swift Package Manager](https://swiftpackageregistry.com/Adyen/adyen-ios).
  * We now support SwiftUI applications. Have a look at our [example SwiftUI App](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-ios/tree/develop/Demo/SwiftUI) on GitHub.
  * New supported payment method: [BLIK](/payment-methods/blik). For more information see the [BLIKComponent class reference](https://adyen.github.io/adyen-ios/5.0.0/documentation/adyen/).
  * Updated the 3D Secure SDK to [v2.2.1](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-ios/releases/tag/2.2.1).
  * Optional parameter `LocalizationParameters.bundle` that you can use to override the default translation strings.

  ### Deprecated

  We are deprecating the following classes and parameters:

  * Header section: `showsLargeTitle` and `FormHeaderItem`.
  * Footer section: `FormFooterItem`, `FormFooterItemView` and `FormFooterStyle`. Use `FormButtonItem` and `FormLabelItem` instead.

  ### Improved

  * Xcode 12 support for CocoaPods and Carthage.

  * The card encryption code has been rewritten in Swift.

  * You can now hide the CVC field for stored card payments. To do this in:

    * Drop-in, set `PaymentMethodsConfiguration.card.showsSecurityCodeField` to **false**.
    * Components, set `CardComponent.showsSecurityCodeField` to **false**.

  * We removed the footer note from `SEPADirectDebitComponent` to bring it in line with other Adyen frontend libraries.

  * When you are doing [zero-value auth](/get-started-with-adyen/adyen-glossary/#zero-value-auth), the submit button text is now **Confirm preauthorization**.

  ### Fixed

  * Dismissing `ApplePayComponent` now correctly invokes the callback.
  * Canceling the web view using a swipe down now calls `RedirectComponent.delegate`.
  * All card types now appear as an icon once you start typing a card number and the card type is detected.
  * We fixed an issue where `AdyenWeChatPay.xcscheme` wasn't being tracked to build `AdyenWeChatPay.framework` on Carthage.

  ## 2020-11-18 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.7.3

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an Android Drop-in exception that occurred when generating a generic Configuration object with an empty `clientKey`.

  ## 2020-11-18 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.18.2

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  For dual-branded cards, the Card Component now renders both brands, and allows the cardholder to choose the brand they want to pay with.\
  This makes the Component compliant with European regulatory guidelines for accepting dual-branded cards (EU IFR Regulation 2015/751 article 8).

  ### Fixed

  The BIN lookup will no longer fail if no `brands` have been provided in the [Card Component](/payment-methods/cards/web-component) configuration.


  ## 2020-11-11 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.18.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Improved

  * Both these issues are fixed in 3.18.2.

  ## 2020-11-09 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.18.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ## 2020-11-06 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.17.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Fixed

  * The Apple Pay Drop-in and Component can now use the `configuration` object from the `/paymentMethods` response, so you no longer need to specify `merchantName` or `merchantIdentifier` when you configure Drop-in or the Apple Pay Component.
  * For giropay, the `showPayButton` configuration is now used as expected.

  ```
       integrity="sha384-gbQyzqV1xX+snFEMrubEm0IpmYSufBjUnHzTaJZ+dcWCyxl0j94IZhnfC2gbxgeu"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2020-11-06 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.17.2

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Changed

  The are some changes in how the payment method details for SEPA Direct Debit and Apple Pay are passed by Drop-in/Components in the `state.data.paymentMethod` object:

  * Apple Pay now outputs `applePayToken` instead of `applepay.token`
  * SEPA Direct Debit now outputs `iban` and `ownerName` instead of `sepa.ibanNumber` and `sepa.ownerName`

  ### Improved

  * For the Gift Card Component, we made some improvements to the flow.

  ### Known Issues

  * Dual branded cards cause a validation issue in the card component.
  * The BIN lookup will fail if no `brands` have been provided in the [Card Component](/payment-methods/cards/web-component) configuration. To avoid this, specify at least one supported card type in the `brands` field.
  * Both these issues are fixed in 3.18.2.

  ### Updating

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.17.2/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-9+nLpCVhoDOcPA/0Ebl0pTB55CIWp+XEMxjyivaRhvFc/Unajqo+Q/7+8I6+MtEO"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.17.2/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-cZIV5piH3KzCM9VwntcX2yQ9zLS8xvo9f0pld8RJe7mUBO2GcsgEmXkc78rH/UA3"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2020-11-05 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where making a non-EUR gift card payment with the [`order`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/post/payments) object was not working as expected.

  ## 2020-11-05 — Pay by Link

  ### Improved

  If no payment methods are available for the `amount` and `countryCode` specified in the payment link, the shopper will now see an error.

  ### Fixed

  The `amount` specified in a payment link is now correctly validated against the limit set for your account.

  ## 2020-11-03 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.7.2

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * You can now hide the CVC field when accepting payments with cards and stored card details using the [`CardComponent`](/payment-methods/cards/android-component). To do this, set the `hideCvc` and `hideCvcStoredCard` flags to **true** in the `CardConfiguration` object.

  ### Fixed

  * When accepting payments with stored [Bancontact (BCMC) card details](/payment-methods/bancontact/bancontact-card/android-component), the Component no longer requires a CVC.

  ## 2020-10-28 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.7.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Improved

  * We added translations for [MB WAY](/payment-methods/mb-way) and for the AwaitComponent.

  ## 2020-10-26 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.17.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  We have redesigned the flow for [gift cards](/payment-methods/gift-cards).

  * The Gift Card Component now calls an `onBalanceCheck` event when the shopper selects the **Redeem** button in the Component. You can use this event to trigger making the [`/paymentMethods/balance`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods/balance) request from your server. Pass the response to the Component so that it can present the gift card balance in the UI.
  * The Gift Card Component now also calls an `onOrderRequest` event when the gift card balance is not enough to complete the payment. You'll need to use this event to make an [`/orders`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/later/post/orders) request from your server. The [`/orders`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/later/post/orders) endpoint allows you to combine the gift card with other payment methods to make [partial payments](/online-payments/partial-payments).

  ### Known Issues

  The new gift card flow does not show errors when a balance check fails. In scenarios where the payment amount is lower than the gift card's balance, the Component is unable to call the `submit()` function. These behaviors are improved in version [3.17.2](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=3.17.2).

  ### Updating

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.17.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-G9jkTAyCOIVdksXbtrNgdur2DyRi85ujYLXbqe5AvvH23rN21d7xiU7cRIqvsEaH"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.17.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-gbQyzqV1xX+snFEMrubEm0IpmYSufBjUnHzTaJZ+dcWCyxl0j94IZhnfC2gbxgeu"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2020-10-22 — Checkout API

  ### New

  * **POST /payments**: When making [partial payments](/online-payments/partial-payments) with an [`order`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/payments#request-order) object and without an `amount`, the API now automatically sets the amount to either the gift card balance or the [`remainingAmount`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/orders#responses-200-remainingAmount), whichever is lower.

  ### Fixed

  * **POST /payments**: Fixed an issue that caused [updating stored card details](/online-payments/account-updater/batch-account-updater#card-details-updated) using an `alias` to fail.

  ## 2020-10-19 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.16.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Improved

  * When configuring the [Google Pay Component](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-component), you can now include include the [`transactionInfo`](https://developers.google.com/pay/api/web/reference/request-objects#TransactionInfo) parameter.
  * The [Apple Pay Component](/payment-methods/apple-pay/web-component) now uses the `configuration` object from the `/paymentMethods` response, so you no longer need to specify `merchantName` or `merchantIdentifier` when you configure the Component.
  * We now make the `onValidateMerchant` call by default for the [Apple Pay Component](/payment-methods/apple-pay/web-component). However, you can also choose to make the call manually, in which case your `onValidateMerchant` call overrides the default.
  * We improved the sourcemaps for the `adyen.js` files.

  ### Fixed

  * The warning: `paymentMethodsResponse was provided but no payment methods were found.` will now only be shown if you provide a `paymentMethodsResponse` object without any valid payment methods.

  ### Known Issues

  * The Apple Pay Component was not getting the expected configuration values from the `/paymentMethods` response, so you still have to specify the values in the `configuration` object. This issue is fixed in version [3.17.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=3.17.1).

  ### Updating

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ## 2020-10-14 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.15.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ## 2020-10-12 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  * If using the [Klarna widget](/payment-methods/klarna/api-only#the-klarna-widget), you no longer get an error when making a [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/payments/details) request.

  ## 2020-10-12 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue with the PayPal authorisation-only flow.
  * **Pay by Link API**: When you specified an array of [`allowedPaymentMethods`](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link/create-payment-links/api#specific-transactions), it was still possible to make payments with card types that were not specified in this array (even though they were not rendered in the UI). We have fixed this, so that it is no longer possible to make payments with card types not specified in the `allowedPaymentMethods` array.

  ## 2020-10-12 — Checkout API — Version 65

  ### New

  Checkout API v65 is now available in [API Explorer](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/overview).

  * **All endpoints**
    * When a request fails due to validation errors, the response now returns the `pspReference` in the response body.

  * **POST [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/post/payments)**

    * You can now send the following additional parameters when providing [risk-related information](/risk-management).

      * [`riskData.profileReference`](/risk-management/create-and-use-risk-profiles#dynamically-assign-a-risk-profile-to-a-payment)
      * [`riskData.fraudOffset`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/post/payments#request-riskData-fraudOffset)
      * [`riskData.customFields`](/risk-management/configure-manual-risk/configure-custom-risk-rules#create-custom-fields)

    * When making a payment with a gift card, the API now requires the `paymentMethod.brand`.

  * **POST [`/paymentLinks`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/post/paymentLinks)**
    * You can now use the [`riskData`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/post/paymentLinks#request-riskData) object to send risk-related settings.

  * **POST [`/paymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/post/paymentMethods)**
    * For gift cards, the response now contains the gift card brand in the [`brand`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-paymentMethods-brand) parameter.

  * **POST [`/paymentMethods/balance`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods/balance)**
    * If the gift card has a limit for how much balance can be used in a single transaction, the response now contains this limit in the [`transactionLimit`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/post/paymentMethods/balance#responses-200-transactionLimit) field.

  ### Changed

  * **POST [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/post/payments)**
    * BACS payments now have a [voucher payment flow](/online-payments/api-only#additional-action). This means the response now contains `action.type`: **voucher** instead of **redirect**.

  ### Removed

  * **POST [`/paymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/65/post/paymentMethods)**

    * The response no longer contains the following parameters:

      * `supportsRecurring`
      * `oneClickPaymentMethods`: Use [`storedPaymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-storedPaymentMethods) instead.
      * `groups`: Use [`brands`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-paymentMethods) instead.

  ## 2020-10-09 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.15.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * We introduced a new method for Web Drop-in, `closeActivePaymentMethod`. This lets you close a selected payment method, for example if you want to reset Drop-in.
  * Support for [installment options](/payment-methods/cards/web-component#optional-configuration) for card payments in Japan.

  ### Improved

  * We improved the Google Pay Component:

    * `existingPaymentMethodRequired` is now set to **false** by default.
    * Increased the `isAvailable` timeout to accommodate higher latencies.
    * Removed the timeout for loading the Google Pay Web SDK file automatically so the Google Pay script can load properly.

  * Swapped the order for the `shopperEmail` and `telephoneNumber` input fields in the personal details component. The email appears first, followed by the telepohone number.

  ### Changed

  * Removed the `shopperEmail` field from the MB WAY Component because it is no longer required by the payment method.

  ### Fixed

  * The `formatData` field for the ACH payment method is now properly checking the format, and the margin is fixed when no holder name is shown.
  * When a shopper pastes a value into the card number field, we now correctly trigger the BIN lookup, clear the error and allow the shopper to make the payment.

  ### Updating

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.15.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-wRJEmCkwtDr3QqIA1UqInI8kmB6XerK8zxfIhP0bSk34lK/kyvE9bSewbrleIQwo"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.15.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-4ppagynsoxB4LNdDTdpX1No72EGOMm13+Y89hg+nz+dAIAWchOBTEnLL7u/hi6eM"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2020-10-07 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.7.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### New

  * New supported payment method: [MB WAY](/payment-methods/mb-way), `MBWayComponent`.
  * Added the `AwaitComponent` to handle the **await** action type for payment methods like MB WAY.
  * Added the [client key](/development-resources/client-side-authentication#availability-compatibility) to remove the need for configuring the `cardPublicKey` in your integration. The client key fetches your client encryption public key automatically from your Customer Area.
  * Enabled programmatic dismissal for the `RedirectComponent` using the `dismiss(animated:completion:)` method.
  * Added a closure callback to the `ApplePayComponent` which will be called when the user closes the payment sheet.
  * Added support to exclude card brands from the `ApplePayComponent`.
  * Support for JCB cards in the `ApplePayComponent`.
  * Enabled customisation for the presentation style of the `RedirectComponent`by adding `modalPresentationStyle` to `RedirectComponentStyle`.
  * Added `didCancel(component:from:)` to the `DropInComponentDelegate` to call back client code when a managed component is dismissed by the shopper.
  * Updated the 3D Secure SDK to [v2.1.0-rc6](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-3ds2-ios/releases/tag/2.1.0-rc.6).

  ### Improved

  * More accurate card brand detection for the `CardComponent` using the new client key.

  ### Known Issues

  * The `clientKey` must be set in instances of `CardComponent` and `BCMCComponent` even when you use the initializer that receives the client key.

  ## 2020-10-05 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where single-use [payment links](/unified-commerce/pay-by-link) were creating multiple payments.

  ## 2020-09-25 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.14.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Improved

  * It is now possible to set the aria-label `iframeTitle` on the card fields.

  ### Fixed

  * Full-width characters such as Japanese Katakana characters are now validated correctly.
  * We increased the time-out for loading device fingerprinting files, to prevent `df-timeout` errors from the `onChange` event when loading large device fingerprinting files.
  * Fixed an issue that occurred in some situations with prefilled data when redirecting.
  * Fixed an issue where the PayPal Component was not passing the correct component reference to the `onCancel` and `onError` events.
  * Clicking on the icon for a dual-branded card to select the card brand is working as expected again.

  ## 2020-09-25 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.13.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed issues with Google Pay and Drop-in:

    * Google Pay can no longer trigger the submit flow twice on the Drop-in.
    * Safari now correctly resolves the initial `isReadyToPay` promise on Drop-in when automatically loading the Google Pay API JavaScript library.

  ### Updating

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), use the same SRI hashes below.

  ## 2020-09-21 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  **Pay by Link API**: When making a [`/paymentLinks`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/paymentLinks) request, you can now include the [`deliverAt`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/paymentLinks#request-deliverAt) parameter.

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed the Finnish translations for the terms and conditions prompt.
  * **Pay by Link API**: We fixed an issue where payments would fail when you provided a delivery address but no billing address.

  ## 2020-09-14 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  For African eWallets and eBanking, the `shopperEmail`, `shopperName`, and the `telephoneNumber` are no longer required parameters for the [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments) request.

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue in [v64](/online-payments/release-notes#releaseNote=2020-08-09-checkout-api-64) where valid fields could trigger a validation error.

  ## 2020-09-10 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.7.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * New supported payment method: [MB WAY](/payment-methods/mb-way).
  * AwaitComponent to handle `action.type`: **await**.
  * Added support for [`ClientKey`](/development-resources/client-side-authentication) which will replace `PublicKey` for client-side authentication in future versions.

  ## 2020-09-07 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  If the final status of the payment can not yet be determined, the shopper now sees a message of **Pending** instead of **Something went wrong**.

  ## 2020-09-02 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.13.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Improved

  * Added field validation for:

    * `telephoneNumber` in E-Context payments.
    * `dateOfBirth` in [buy now, pay later payment methods](/payment-methods).

  * For SEPA Direct Debit payments, the IBAN and name fields can now be filled in automatically. To do this, pass `ibanNumber` and `ownerName` to `paymentMethodsConfiguration`.

  * Apple Pay now uses the highest version supported by the shopper's device by default. You can override this setting in your Apple Pay configuration.

  * The [Google Pay Component](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-component) now uses the `configuration` object from the `/paymentMethods` response, so you no longer need to specify `merchantIdentifier` when configuring the Component.

  * Accessibility improvements:

    * If there is no aria configuration object, the component creates one with the default `iframeTitle` and `error` properties.
    * If an `iframeTitle` or `error` property is missing from the configuration object you defined, they are added automatically.
    * If no placeholder configuration object exists, one is created with a default and translated value.
    * For errors, the components use `aria-live="polite"`. For example, if an error comes up when filling in the form, the screen reader will read the error text after it finishes reading the current text.
    * Screen readers no longer read a description for the payment method icons which are decorative. Instead, they read the text announcing the payment method.

  * The Web Drop-in integration now has a 1 second timeout for the `isAvailable` filter. This means that if a payment method fails to load, Web Drop-in continues to load the form for all other payment methods. The end result will not contain an error, it will be the Drop-in form without the payment method that failed to load.

  ### Fixed

  * The PayPal Component no longer returns an error when calling `unmount()`.
  * The aria label `iframeTitle` works correctly for the card and custom card integrations.

  ### Updating

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.13.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-cMH19I9HPj31iL3b/lcBcpsqbieCGSLyNef+RzjS7g3h5DhP2BI6j68/ogKSQCAh"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
       href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.13.0/adyen.css"
       integrity="sha384-AtxcD/Ax9ZRBLJ63s/bwCMrfe/mXWt4TF7F+Vltoxo0WgAwWjVNDsfyMAgY+9nBi"
       crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2020-08-12 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.11.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * The Google Pay Component now automatically loads the Google Pay script if the Component doesn't detect the script in your front end. In previous versions, you had to handle this in your front end, making sure that you load the Google Pay API JavaScript library before the Adyen JS script.

  ### Improved

  * Components for payment methods that use QR codes (such as Swish or WeChat Pay) now also calls `onAdditionalDetails` when the Component receives an error.
  * When using the Swish Component, the timeout for status checks is now increased to 15 minutes. The timeout used to be 3 minutes.

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed the loading state for multiple Components (such as BLIK and Multibanco). In earlier versions, some Components didn't have a loading state so the Components weren't displaying the spinner while initializing.

  * We made the following fixes for the Card Component:

    * The Card Component now allows focus on optional security code fields.
    * We fixed the logic when validating extra fields required for AVS.
    * We fixed the `valid` state of `ExpirationDate`.

  ### Updating

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), use the same SRI hashes below.

  ## 2020-08-09 — Checkout API — Version 64

  ### New

  Checkout API v64 is now available in [API Explorer](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/overview).

  * **All endpoints**

    * If an API request contains a field that is not recognized, or if the format is not valid, we now return an error message with error code `702` instead of dropping the field. The error message contains information about why the validation failed, for example:
      ```json
      {
        "status": 400,
        "errorCode": "702",
        "message": "Structure of PaymentRequest contains the following unmapped fields exampleErrorField",
        "errorType": "validation"
      }
      ```

  * **POST [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments)**

    * For Boleto Bancário, when a `shopperEmail` is provided, we now send an email with the Boleto code to the shopper.

  * **POST [`/paymentLinks`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/paymentLinks)**

    * In the request, you can now specify [`installmentOptions.plans`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/paymentLinks#request-installmentOptions-plans) and [`installmentOptions.preselectedValue`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/paymentLinks#request-installmentOptions-preselectedValue).

  ### Improved

  * **POST [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments)**
    * For Bancontact mobile and WeChat Pay, when making a request with `paymentMethod.type` **bcmc\_mobile\_QR** or **wechatpayQR**, the response now returns `<span translate="no">**Pending**</span>` instead of `<span translate="no">**PresentToShopper**</span>` result code. This result code is now consistent with other QR code payment methods such as BLIK and Swish.

  ### Changed

  * **POST [`/paymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/paymentMethods)**

    * The response for Amazon Pay, Apple Pay, Google Pay, and PayPal now contains a `configuration` object. Example for Google Pay and Apple Pay:
      ```json
      {
       "paymentMethods":[
         {
           "configuration":{
             "merchantId":"0123456789"
           },
           "name":"Google Pay",
           "type":"paywithgoogle"
         },
         {
           "configuration": {
             "merchantDisplayName": "Merchant Name",
             "merchantIdentifier": "1000"
           },
           "name": "Apple Pay",
           "type": "applepay"
         }
       ]
      }
      ```

  * **POST [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments)**

  ### Deprecated

  * **POST [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments__section_resParams)**

    * In the response, we have marked a number of fields as deprecated. Although they are deprecated, these fields have not yet been removed from the response and you can still use them.

      In a future version of the API, the deprecated fields will be removed and replaced by fields in the [`action`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments#responses-200-action) object. We recommend that you start using the fields in the [`action`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments#responses-200-action) object in the response.

      This applies to the following fields:

      | Response field          | Solution                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
      | ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
      | `authentication`        | Use the [`action`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments#responses-200-action) object instead to get the values to be used in further calls to the [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments/details) endpoint. Applies to 3D Secure 2 payments. |
      | `details [InputDetail]` | Use the [`action`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments#responses-200-action) object instead to get all the fields needed to submit in the [`/payments/details`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments/details) call.                                             |
      | `outputDetails`         | Use the [`action`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments#responses-200-action) object instead to get the details that will be presented to the shopper.                                                                                                                                    |
      | `paymentData`           | Use `action.Paymentdata` instead.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
      | `redirect`              | Use the [`action`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/64/post/payments#responses-200-action) object instead to get information about the redirect URL for payment flows that require a redirect.                                                                                                         |

  ## 2020-08-09 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  * New supported payment method: [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay).
  * We added support for the PayPal authorisation-only flow.

  ## 2020-07-28 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.11.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  You can only use this new feature if you are using [`clientKey`](/development-resources/client-side-authentication) for your client-side authentication. If you are still using `originKey`, refer to our [migration guide](/development-resources/client-side-authentication/migrate-from-origin-key-to-client-key).

  Unsupported cards are now *blocked* on the frontend. When a shopper provides an unsupported card type, the Component disables the payment form, and shows an **Unsupported card** error message.

  In previous versions, you'd only find out if a shopper provided an unsupported card type when you receive an `errorCode: 905` in the `/payments` response. In this version, the Component triggers an `onError` callback after it detects that the card type is unsupported. The callback also returns a `detectedBrands` array that indicates what type of card the shopper used.

  ### Improved

  * The [Bancontact card](/payment-methods/bancontact/bancontact-card/web-component) `brand` property now defaults to `bcmc`.

  ### Fixed

  * Apple Pay now uses the `configuration.merchantName` property as the default label for the total line item if the `totalPriceLabel` is not provided.

  ### Updating

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
       integrity="sha384-sR3tFyFWM09aya/mpkzB0fV4t+g6b4i64izXXy+lSSTmrgL/9/cdNFURV5T9gUbI"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
      href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.11.0/adyen.css"
      integrity="sha384-8ofgICZZ/k5cC5N7xegqFZOA73H9RQ7H13439JfAZW8Gj3qjuKL2isaTD3GMIhDE"
      crossorigin="anonymous">  
  ```

  ## 2020-07-23 — Checkout API — Version 53

  ### New

  Checkout API v53 is now available in [API Explorer](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/53/overview).

  * **POST [`/paymentLinks`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/53/post/paymentLinks)**
    * The response now returns the payment link `id` along with all other parameters sent in the request. In previous versions, the response only contained the URL, expiry date, reference, and amount.

  * **POST [`/payments`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/53/post/payments)**

    * When making a request with `paymentMethod.type` **bcmc\_mobile**, the response now returns both `url` and `qrCodeData`. This allows you to offer both QR code and app redirect to your shoppers.
    * We now return an error if the `origin` in the request contains more than 80 characters or if the URL is invalid.

  ### Improved

  * **POST [`/paymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/53/post/paymentMethods)**

    * For giropay, the `bic` is no longer returned in the response. If you are using Web Drop-in or Components, the front end will no longer require a BIC. When shoppers select to pay with giropay, they are now redirected to giropay's website where they can provide their BIC.
    * For Bancontact mobile, the response now only returns `bcmc_mobile`. This new payment method type combines the `bcmc_mobile_QR` and `bcmc_mobile_app` types from previous versions of the API.
    * If transaction rules for payment methods are set up for your account, these are now evaluated when you send a `/paymentMethods` request.

  * **POST [`/paymentLinks`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/53/post/paymentLinks)**
    * If a request is successful, the response now returns **HTTP 201** instead of an **HTTP 200** result code.

  ## 2020-07-16 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.10.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ## 2020-07-15 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.10.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * Added support for `callbackIntents` on the Google Pay component. This allows for updating the price dynamically when certain information changes. Refer to [PaymentDataCallbacks](https://developers.google.com/pay/api/web/reference/request-objects#PaymentDataCallbacks).

  ### Improved

  * Added support for plurals and string interpolation on translations.
  * We now generate one set of SRI hashes, so now you can use the same SRI hash regardless of where you are loading Adyen JS and CSS files from—[test or live](/development-resources/live-endpoints#checkout-endpoints), including from live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet).

  ### Fixed

  * With Web Drop-in the `dropin.submit()` is once again working with Google Pay.
  * Fixed an issue in Web Drop-in where selecting "Pay" on stored cards didn't trigger the `onSubmit()` callback due to the `billingAddressRequired` property.
  * Chrome autofill on iOS now works as expected, and setting the value of `placeholder` to `null` no longer throws an error.

  ### Updating

  If you implemented SRI in test or with live [region-specific URLs](/online-payments/web-best-practices#embed-script-and-stylesheet), you can now use the same SRI hashes below.

  ```
  <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.10.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-ghytckjc6RtqM4eKYE4LBJRtqZWcxPv/sGamiebH2dY3tyiQYUOZnPJ0s27de7vB"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
      href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.10.0/adyen.css"
      integrity="sha384-8ofgICZZ/k5cC5N7xegqFZOA73H9RQ7H13439JfAZW8Gj3qjuKL2isaTD3GMIhDE"
      crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2020-07-09 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  * Webhook events for Pay by Link payments now include the `paymentLinkId`.

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed an issue where in some cases split payment data were not retained with iDEAL payments.

  ## 2020-07-09 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  * When risk data are included in the payments request, this no longer results in an invalid signature calculation.

  ## 2020-07-02 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue where a non-reusable payment link can still be reused after paying with Klarna.

  ## 2020-06-30 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.6.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### New

  * You can now choose to show or hide the security code field, and we added a delegate for `onBrand` and `onBin` to the Card Component.
  * `AnyCardPaymentMethod` now contains `CardFundingSource`.
  * When the app goes to the background, sensitive form components are blurred in the UI.
  * You can now customize rounded corners, and we added a customized `tintColor`and `separator`.
  * We introduced a new issue type **Assistance needed** on GitHub.
  * We have enabled dynamic font adjustments. When iPhone users change the font size in their settings, our SDK now supports that.

  ### Improved

  * We now accept card expiry dates up to 30 years in the future when we validate card data.
  * We made the `AmountFormatter` public and added a helper function to convert major unit double values into minor unit `Int` values.
  * We enabled a loading state for the `PaymentMethodListComponent`.
  * We improved warnings, tests coverage, documentation and translation.

  ### Fixed

  * While a payment is in progress, you cannot cancel a payment using Drop-in and the **open all** button is disabled.
  * We adjusted the preselected payment method component height for iOS 10 and fixed bug with the iOS 10 `PreselectedComponent`.
  * We fixed the background for `ListItemView`, focusing on `CVC` field and navigation glitches for half-screen presentation.

  ### Breaking Changes

  * `barTintColor` and `barBackgroundColor` in `NavigationStyle` have been deprecated.
  * We currently present `Redirect` from the top most `viewController` on `keyWindow` and we deprecated `RedirectComponent.presenterViewController`.

  ## 2020-06-29 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.9.5

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Fixed

  * When using the Component to collect billing address, countries/regions with state datasets now get correctly validated when the `state` field value changes.

  ### Improved

  * Components for payment methods where you receive an `await` action type (such as [MBWAY](/payment-methods/mb-way) and BLIK) now always calls `onAdditionalDetails` when the Component receives an error.

  * The `storePaymentMethod` field is now sent in the `state.data` object as long as `enableStoreDetails` is set to **true** (the ***Save details for next time*** checkbox is shown in the UI). If the shopper doesn't select the checkbox, the `state.data.storePaymentMethod` field will have a value of **false**.

  ## 2020-06-25 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue where if you upload a logo for your payment page, the favicon did not reflect the logo. Now you can see the logo on the payment page and in your browser's favicon.
  * We resolved an issue where payment links can still be used even if you sent `reusable`: **false** in your request.

  ## 2020-06-23 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.9.4

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Fixed

  * Fixed an issue regarding validation of the billing address in the Card Component where a pre-filled country/region with no states available still waits for a state or province field to be provided.
  * Fixed an issue where the Card Component state does not update the address data until the country/region is changed.
  * Fixed an issue where MBWay Component accepted different names for `shopperEmail` and `telephoneNumber` parameters in the request.
  * Fixed an issue regarding validation when a `namePrefix` is passed to the `personalDetails` shopper input fields.
