  ## 2020-06-18 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  Ratepay Direct Debits payments using Austrian (AT) IBANs is now working.

  ## 2020-06-18 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  You can now see the [`/paymentLinks`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentLinks) endpoint in your [Customer Area](https://ca-live.adyen.com/), under **Accounts** > **API URLs**.

  ### Improved

  Pay by Link now only shows one option for WeChat Pay in the payment method selection list.

  ## 2020-06-11 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  The `paymentLinkId` is now sent in the **AUTHORISATION** webhook. You can use this to reconcile payments to payment links.

  ## 2020-06-02 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.9.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * New supported payment method: BLIK.
  * [Client-side authentication using the client key](/development-resources/client-side-authentication). The client key allows for more flexible and efficient origin management than the [origin key](/development-resources/how-to-get-an-origin-key). If you are using origin keys, see how to [migrate to using the client key](/development-resources/client-side-authentication/migrate-from-origin-key-to-client-key).

  ### Improved

  * We improved the UI for the [Bancontact mobile](/payment-methods/bancontact/bancontact-mobile) Pay button.
  * Added [new localization fields](/online-payments/build-your-integration/sessions-flow/?platform=Web\&integration=Drop-in#optional-configuration) for BLIK and improved localization fields for credit cards.

  ### Fixed

  * Double-clicking a [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay) button started a session twice, resulting in an error. We fixed this so you can now double-click a Google Pay button without initiating a new session.
  * The 3D Secure 2 Component no longer tries to parse invalid `postMessages`.
  * We fixed an issue where the CVC field for saved Maestro cards was required even when it is optional.
  * When a shopper entered a credit card number using copy and paste or auto-fill, the `onBinValue` callback triggered by the [Custom Card Component](/payment-methods/cards/custom-card-integration/#show-the-available-cards-in-your-payment-form) provided an encrypted BIN number. Now, the `onBinValue` callback provides the actual BIN number, no matter how the card number is filled in.

  ## 2020-05-28 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  * `onBinValue` now works when a card number is pasted into the field. For more information, see [Web Components 3.9.0](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=web\&version=3.9.0).

  ## 2020-05-21 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  * We improved the [`/paymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods) response when getting a list of available payment methods in Sweden. This means that if you are using Drop-in or Components and send a [`/paymentMethods`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/latest/post/paymentMethods) request with `countryCode`: **SE**, you'll see the following UI improvements in line with [Swedish regulations](/online-payments/lagen-om-betaltjanster).

    * The payment method title has been changed to *Card*.
    * From API version v49 and later: If you support Maestro, the icon for Maestro is shown first.

  ## 2020-05-21 — Pay by Link

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue where a non-reusable payment link can be used to pay multiple times when using Internet Explorer. To fix this issue, we added a no-cache header to the Pay by Link request.

  ## 2020-05-14 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  * If a [Klarna](/payment-methods/klarna) payment request is missing invoice lines, you will now receive the following error message: **No InvoiceLines provided**.

  ## 2020-05-11 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.1

  ### Improved

  * Masking on date inputs is now supported on browsers that do not have native support for date inputs (such as Safari and Internet Explorer).
  * Age validation is now supported in the `Date of birth` field on the [Oney3x4x](/payment-methods/oney) Component.
  * [Installment options for credit cards](/payment-methods/cards/web-component#optional-configuration) are now hidden when the `amount` is set to **0**.

  ### Fixed

  * Resolved a [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay) issue where double-clicking the Google Pay button would initiate two separate payments.
  * Resolved a [Google Pay](/payment-methods/google-pay) issue where Drop-in would, in some cases, open up a new payment sheet when selecting **Continue** on the Google Pay sheet.

  ## 2020-05-07 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  * Optional shopper information sent in an Oxxo payment request will now show up in the **Offers** tab in your Customer Area.

  ## 2020-05-05 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.6.6

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * We added a **Common issues** section on the GitHub [README](https://github.com/Adyen/adyen-android/blob/master/README.md#common-issues).
  * The new `action` type `sdk` replaces the `action` type `wechatpaySDK`.

  ### Fixed

  The WeChatPay Component now correctly triggers the observer automatically.

  ## 2020-04-30 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  * New supported payment methods: Buy now, pay later payment methods such as [Klarna](/payment-methods/klarna) and [Zip](/payment-methods/zip).
  * [Credit card installments](/payment-methods/cards/credit-card-installments)
  * When using Adyen for Platforms, you can now add [split instructions](/classic-platforms/processing-payments#providing-split-instructions) to payment links.

  ### Improved

  * The redirect URL for [Oney 3x4x](/payment-methods/oney) payments has been shortened.

  ## 2020-04-30 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  * We improved the reliability of 3D Secure 1 authentications after partial payments with [gift cards](/payment-methods/gift-cards).

  ## 2020-04-30 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.6.5

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  The 3D Secure 2 SDK was updated to version `2.1.0-rc08`.

  ### Improved

  * Whenever `CardView` is present, we add a `FLAG_SECURE` to the window.
  * We now accept dates up to 30 years in the future when we validate card data.

  ### Fixed

  * We adressed a localization issue and now use `LocaleUtil.fromLanguageTag()` to parse user locale.
  * Fixed a typo in CUP regex validation.

  ## 2020-04-27 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.8.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * [Credit card installments](/payment-methods/cards/credit-card-installments) are now supported on the [Card Component](/payment-methods/cards/web-component) and [Drop-in](/payment-methods/cards/web-drop-in).

  ### Improved

  * `allowCreditCards` configuration is now available on the [Google Pay Component](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-component#configure) and [Drop-in](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-drop-in#configure).
  * The PayU NetBanking Component and the PayU CashCard Component now provide drop-down issuer lists.

  ## 2020-04-23 — Pay by Link

  ### Changed

  * The `countryCode` is no longer a required parameter for the [`/paymentLinks`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/paymentLinks) request.

  ### Fixed

  * The shopper can no longer make multiple payments when a single-use payment link is open in multiple tabs.

  ## 2020-04-23 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue where the 3D Secure 2 flow could break when receiving an unsupported `screen.colorDepth` value on Google Chrome v83 or v84.
  * We fixed an issue where `metadata` was not being sent back in the webhook event.

  ## 2020-04-20 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.7.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Changed

  * Drop-in/Components no longer support the native Klarna integration. Klarna is now only supported through the [redirect integration](/payment-methods/klarna).

  ### New

  * New supported payment methods: [Oney 3x4x](/payment-methods/oney), [MB WAY](/payment-methods/mb-way), RatePAY.

  ### Improved

  * The Card Component/Drop-in now show a visual hint for the location of the CVC/CVV on the card.
  * We added VPay to the card brands recognized by the Card Component/Drop-in.

  ### Fixed

  * A saved card is no longer breaking if the card type is not recognized.
  * When using Internet Explorer 11, the card number can no longer cause the browser to freeze.

  ## 2020-04-16 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.5.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### New

  * The `browserInfo` is now included in the `PaymentComponentData` returned by Drop-in/Component.
  * We added UI customization options to the preselected payment screen rendered by Drop-in.
  * Inline validation for text entry is now supported for the Card Component, the SEPA Direct Debit Component, and others.

  ### Fixed

  * The Redirect Component can now deal with a native app redirect.
  * We fixed an issue where the Apple Pay Component would freeze in the payment screen rendered by Drop-in.
  * We fixed an issue where the keyboard would cover the UI.

  ## 2020-04-13 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.6.3

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  * We fixed an issue where Elo and Hipercard cards were not validating correctly in the [Card](/payment-methods/cards) and [Custom Card](/payment-methods/cards/custom-card-integration) Components.

  ## 2020-04-09 — Checkout API

  ### Fixed

  Before this version, the `/paymentMethods` response would return **wechatpayMiniProgram** and **wechatpaySDK** if `channel`:**Web**. These payment methods are now only when `channel`:**Android** or `channel`:**iOS**.

  ## 2020-04-03 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.4.1

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### Fixed

  The `CardSecurityCodeValidator` and `CardSecurityCodeFormatter` are now public.

  ## 2020-04-02 — Pay by Link

  ### New

  * By default, payment links can now only be used to make one successful payment. To create a reusable link, you can override the default behavior when creating the payment link.
  * You can now view previously created payment links in a dashboard in your Customer Area (only for payment links created through the Customer Area).
  * You'll receive an email when a payment link has successfully been paid for (only for payment links created through the Customer Area).

  ### Improved

  The payment link URL has been shortened.

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue that didn't allow creating payment links if you only had one store ID under your merchant account.

  ## 2020-04-01 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.4.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### New

  * New supported payment methods: [WeChat Pay](/payment-methods/wechat-pay/wechat-pay-in-app-payments/ios), Qiwi wallet
  * Drop-in has a new, half-screen presentation style.
  * The Card Component shows a visual hint for the location of the CVC/CVV on the card.
  * Support for website cross-origin isolation using [COOP and COEP](https://web.dev/coop-coep).

  ### Improved

  * Drop-in shows the first available stored payment method prominently.
  * We improved the card brand recognition by the Card Component.
  * `PaymentMethodDetails` now allows encoding without access to the concrete type.
  * You can now use the Apple Pay Component to collect the billing and delivery address of the shopper.

  ### Fixed

  * The Card Component now only allows card expiry dates from 3 months in the past to 15 years in the future.
  * The Card Component no longer crashes when receiving non-numerical input.

  ## 2020-03-30 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.6.2

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  Added a new Component for making donations, using [Adyen Giving](/online-payments/donations).

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue where the [Custom Card Component](/payment-methods/cards/custom-card-integration) would throw an error if no `onError` event was set.
  * We fixed an issue where the [Custom Card Component](/payment-methods/cards/custom-card-integration) would return an invalid object in the `onError` callback.

  ## 2020-03-23 — Checkout API

  ### New

  To force a card transaction to use a debit funding source, you can now include in the `/payments` request: `paymentMethod.fundingSource`: **debit** .

  ### Fixed

  * The `/paymentMethods` response now returns [ACH](/payment-methods/ach-direct-debit) also when the request includes `amount.value`: **0**.
  * The `deviceFingerprint` is now correctly propagated for all payment methods.

  ## 2020-03-12 — Checkout API

  ### Improved

  When [updating stored payment details](/online-payments/tokenization/managing-tokens#update-stored-details), you can now send in an unencrypted expiration date.

  ## 2020-03-02 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.6.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ## 2020-02-21 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.6.4

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  The 3D Secure 2 SDK was updated to version `2.1.0-rc07`.

  ### Fixed

  We removed a warning about string formatting that you might have seen when compiling the app.

  ## 2020-02-11 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.6.3

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  Drop-in/Components will now forcefully try to get localized strings from the `ShopperLocale` set in the configuration object.

  ### Improved

  If you don't provide a custom array of supported card brands in the `cardConfiguration` object, CardComponent initialization will now get it from the `PaymentMethod` object, instead of using the default values as before.

  ### Fixed

  * We fixed an issue with 19 digit card numbers.
  * We added missing translated strings.
  * We fixed a synchronization issue that could trigger an OOB exception in `CardType` detection.
  * We added proper return type for some Configuration Builder methods.

  ## 2020-01-27 — Web Components/Drop-in — Version 3.5.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/migrate-to-web-v6/from-v3">upgrade to v6.0.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### New

  * Support for Subresource Integrity (SRI) hashes

    You can now use [SRI hashes](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Security/Subresource_Integrity) to ensure that the files you are loading from Adyen have not been manipulated or tampered with by malicious actors.

    To use the SRI hash, you need to add an `integrity` attribute when specifying `<script>` or `<link>` elements. Browsers will also check for the `crossorigin` attribute to ensure that the origin allows [Cross-Origin Resource Sharing (CORS)](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/CORS). If a browser detects that the file's hash does not match the specified hash, the browser will not load the resource. To know which browsers support SRI, refer to this [browser compatibility list](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Security/Subresource_Integrity#Browser_compatibility).

    For example, here is how you specify version 3.5.0 `adyen.js` and `adyen.css` resources:

    ```html
    <script src="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.5.0/adyen.js"
       integrity="sha384-MpcW2OFcC1/y5nwF6UmvRfDXGISpg1rowHVybiS+wJObkUwgFpvbdpJxR2/bwmeA"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>
    ```

    ```html
    <link rel="stylesheet"
      href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.5.0/adyen.css"
      integrity="sha384-aWycvW8Dygg+6QHTq56FJMi4CJBjQt4LbRO3zWUuyfbv0A8g3rb5FR/vHyHKfiY0"
      crossorigin="anonymous">
    ```

    You can find the SRI hashes for all previous versions of our JavaScript and CSS files added in their corresponding release notes.

  * You can now render billing address fields in Drop-in and Card Component to collect the shopper's billing address for Address Verification System (AVS) implementation.

  * Drop-in and Components for payment methods that support saving payment details for recurring transactions can now show a preauthorization button. Instead of a **Pay** button, the button prompts the shopper to confirm the preauthorization. The preauthorization button is shown when you send a zero value amount (zero-auth transaction).

  ### Improved

  `shippingOptionRequired` and `shippingOptionParameters` configuration are now available on [Google Pay Component](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-component#payment-data) and in [Drop-in](/payment-methods/google-pay/web-drop-in#payment-data).

  ### Fixed

  * The `showPayButton` configuration is now working for [Multibanco Component](/payment-methods/multibanco/web-component).
  * The preselected bank is now shown if you specify an issuer ID in the `issuer` configuration for [iDEAL Component](/payment-methods/ideal/web-component#optional-configuration) and in [Drop-in](/payment-methods/ideal/web-drop-in#optional-configuration).

  ### Updating

  ```html
       integrity="sha384-MpcW2OFcC1/y5nwF6UmvRfDXGISpg1rowHVybiS+wJObkUwgFpvbdpJxR2/bwmeA"
       crossorigin="anonymous"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet"
      href="https://checkoutshopper-live.adyen.com/checkoutshopper/sdk/3.5.0/adyen.css"
  ```
  ```html
      integrity="sha384-aWycvW8Dygg+6QHTq56FJMi4CJBjQt4LbRO3zWUuyfbv0A8g3rb5FR/vHyHKfiY0"
      crossorigin="anonymous">
  ```

  ## 2020-01-20 — Android Components/Drop-in — Version 3.6.2

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-19-android-componentsdrop-in-5.14.0">upgrade to v5.14.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  ### Improved

  * We improved the error validation design: invalid shopper input will now be highlighted after the shopper clicks the **Pay** button.
  * ComponentView classes now have `highlightValidationErrors()` method.
  * Drop-in/Components use the correct separation format for American Express.
  * If the shopper cancels the 3D Secure authentication, you will now receive a `Cancelled3DS2Exception`.
  * We added new translation string files for supported locales.

  ## 2020-01-10 — iOS Components/Drop-in — Version 3.3.0

  *\<p>\<div data-component-wrapper="tag"> \<tag :variant=\&quot;warning\&quot;> End-of-Life \</tag> \</div> \<br> This version is \<a href="/online-payments/upgrade-your-integration/#lifecycle">\<strong>End-of-Life\</strong>\</a> since \<strong>October 1, 2025\</strong>. \<br> We recommend that you \<a href="/online-payments/release-notes/#releaseNote=2025-08-27-ios-componentsdrop-in-5.20.0">upgrade to v5.20.0\</a> or later.\</p>*

  If you use [Xcode 15](https://developer.apple.com/xcode/), use [v4.11.1](/online-payments/release-notes?integration_type=ios\&version=4.11.1) or later for compatibility.

  ### New

  * We added options for customizing the Component appearance, for example with fonts and text colors.
  * You can now specify a custom localization key separator.

  ### Fixed

  We fixed an issue where a custom localization table name would apply to default localizations.

  ## 2019-10-29 — Checkout API — Version 52

  ### Changed

  * **POST** [/paymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/52/post/paymentMethods):

    * For giropay, the response now indicates `bic` as an optional field.
    * In the response, the [brands](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/52/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-paymentMethods-brands) array only contains `scheme` payment methods. Previously, tokenized cards, such as Apple Pay, were returned in the same array.

  ### Improved

  * Native payment method (previously supported through Hosted Payment Pages (HPP)): [Swish](/payment-methods/swish). Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **swish**.

  ## 2019-08-16 — Checkout API — Version 50

  ### Improved

  * **POST** [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/50/post/payments):
    * For [Multibanco](/payment-methods/multibanco), the response now contains the `action` object for `type`:**voucher**. You can use the information in the `action` object to [present the voucher](/payment-methods/multibanco/api-only/#additional) to the shopper.

  ## 2019-05-14 — Checkout API — Version 49

  ### New

  * **POST** [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/49/post/payments):
    * The response now contains an [`action`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/49/post/payments#responses-200-action) object that indicates the action to be taken for completing the payment.
  * **POST** [/payments/details](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/49/post/payments/details):
    * The response now contains the [`merchantReference`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/49/post/payments/details#responses-200-merchantReference) field. This is the reference you passed in the [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/49/post/payments) request.

  ### Changed

  * **POST** [/payments](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/49/post/payments):
    * [storePaymentMethod](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/49/post/payments#request-storePaymentMethod) replaces `enableOneClick` and `enableRecurring`.

  ### Improved

  * Native payment method (previously supported through Hosted Payment Pages (HPP)): [Oxxo](/payment-methods/oxxo). Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **oxxo**.

  ## 2019-02-18 — Checkout API — Version 46

  ### Improved

  * Native payment methods (previously supported through Hosted Payment Pages (HPP)):

    * [Gift cards](/payment-methods/gift-cards).
    * [Oney](/payment-methods/oney). Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **facilypay**.

  ## 2019-02-14 — Checkout API — Version 41

  ### Changed

  * **POST** [/paymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/41/post/paymentMethods):
    * For giropay, the [Business Identifier Code (BIC)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_9362) key is now returned in `bic` instead of `giropay.bic`.

  ### Improved

  * Native payment methods (previously supported through Hosted Payment Pages (HPP)):

    * [Boleto Bancário](/payment-methods/boleto-bancario/). Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **boleto**.
    * DOKU. Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **doku**.
    * Dragonpay. Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **dragonpay**.
    * [eContext](/payment-methods/econtext-konbini/). Payment method [types](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **econtext\_atm**, **econtext-stores**, **econtext\_seven\_eleven**, **econtext\_online**.
    * [Ratepay](/payment-methods/ratepay/). Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **ratepay**.

  ### New

  * For [WeChatPay](/payment-methods/wechat-pay/), support for payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **wechatpayMiniProgram**.
  * For payment methods that require you to present a voucher to the shopper, the response now includes [resultCode](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/41/post/payments#responses-200-resultCode):**presentToShopper**.

  ## 2018-06-27 — Checkout API — Version 40

  ### New

  * Payment methods:

    * [Bancontact](/payment-methods/bancontact):

      * [Bancontact card](/payment-methods/bancontact/bancontact-card): the shopper pays using a card. Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **bcmc**.
      * [Bancontact mobile](/payment-methods/bancontact/bancontact-card): the shopper pays using a bank app that supports Bancontact mobile. Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **bcmc\_mobile**.

    * [MoMo](/payment-methods/momo-wallet):

      * [MoMo website](/payment-methods/momo-wallet/?tab=website_1): the shopper pays using a desktop browser. Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **momo\_wallet**.
      * [MoMo app](/payment-methods/momo-wallet/?tab=mobile_app_2): the shopper pays using a mobile app. Payment method [type](/payment-methods/payment-method-types): **momo\_wallet\_app**.

  * **POST** [/paymentMethods](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/40/post/paymentMethods):

    * The response now contains the [`groups`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/40/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-groups) object, which is an array of payment methods that will be recognized.
    * For payment methods that support [recurring payments](/online-payments/tokenization), the response now includes [`supportsRecurring`](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/40/post/paymentMethods#responses-200-paymentMethods-supportsRecurring): **true**.

  ### Removed

  * For payments with wallet tokens, you no longer need to include to `additionalData` prefix in the [additionalData](https://docs.adyen.com/api-explorer/Checkout/40/post/payments#request-additionalData) object. For example, instead of `additionalData.paywithgoogle.token`, use `paywithgoogle.token`.

  ## 2018-02-23 — Checkout API — Version 37

  ### New

  * New payment method [types](/payment-methods/payment-method-types) for [WeChatPay](/payment-methods/wechat-pay/): **wechatpaySDK**, **wechatpayQR**, **wechatpayWeb**.

  ### Changed

  * **POST** `/payments`:

    * For iDEAL, `paymentMethod.issuer` replaces `paymentMethod.idealIssuer`.
    * In the response, `details.payload` replaces `returnUrlQueryString`.
